# P6 — Video Publishing and Media Pipeline

## P6.1 Upload and Import

- [x] Implement create video draft/upload session. (`POST /api/v1/channels/:handle/videos` (requireAuth, owner-only) creates a draft; `GET /api/v1/videos/:id` (optionalAuth) public/unlisted to anyone, private owner-only (else 404); `PATCH`/`DELETE /api/v1/videos/:id` owner-only (non-owner/unknown → 404); `GET /api/v1/channels/:handle/videos` (optionalAuth) lists all for the owner, public-only otherwise; `internal/video`; tested. File upload itself is a later slice.)
- [x] Implement local file upload. (`POST /api/v1/videos/:id/file` (requireAuth, owner-only, multipart `file`) streams the original through `internal/storage` to key `web-videos/<id>.<ext>` (PeerTube-aligned asset layout — one top-level dir per asset kind; see `.ralph/specs/storage-layout.md`; thumbnails → `thumbnails/<id>.jpg`), records a `video_files` row (kind=original, size, content_type, original_name), and flips the video draft→processing; re-upload replaces the prior original; non-owner/unknown → 404. Backend wired from config in `cmd/api`. `video_files` table = migration 0008. Transcode/probe/scan are later slices.)
- [x] Implement resumable upload strategy or documented initial limitation. (core-upload-resumable slice, 2026-07-03. Chunked/resumable protocol on `upload_sessions` + `upload_chunks` (migration 0059): `POST /api/v1/videos/:id/upload-session` (owner; body `{size, filename}` validated UP FRONT — extension allow-list → 415, size vs UPLOAD_MAX_SIZE → 413, quota → 422 quota_exceeded) → `{upload_id, chunk_size (8MiB), total_chunks, size, expires_at}`; `PUT /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id/chunks/:n` (raw body, owner, idempotent re-PUT, each chunk hard-bounded at its exact required size — 413 over / 422 mismatch / 422 out-of-range / 409 finished); `GET /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id` (received chunk ns + bytes — the resume contract, **Redis not required** — the received-chunk ledger IS the progress source, per the fix_plan wording; recorded here as the deliberate simplification); `POST /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id/complete` (assembles chunks in order via a streaming `chunkAssembler` → the EXISTING `AttachOriginal` → `Process` pipeline, identical post-upload flow incl. scan/quarantine/transcode; 422 on missing/mismatched chunks, 409 if already finished); `DELETE /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id` (cancel, idempotent). **Storage decision**: chunk bytes live in the blob backend at `uploads/<session>/<n>` via `storage.Backend.Put` (NOT a private spool dir) so the **S3 backend works unchanged** and cleanup reuses `PrefixDeleter.DeletePrefix`. `internal/upload` service (chunkSize configurable via `WithChunkSize` for multi-chunk tests; default 8MiB). Sessions expire 24h after creation; `runUploadSweepWorker` in cmd/api (1-min ticker) deletes expired/cancelled sessions' chunk blobs then the row — **this IS the failed-upload cleanup item**; orphan draft videos are intentionally left alone (documented). openapi documents all five routes + `CreateUploadSessionRequest`/`UploadSessionResponse`/`UploadStatusResponse` (drift guard green; redocly error count unchanged). Tested: `internal/upload` unit (out-of-order + re-PUT idempotency + resume-after-"restart" over the same repo+blobs + byte-exact assembly + size/range/incomplete errors + cancel + expiry sweep w/ injected clock) + httpapi round-trip (`uploads_test.go`: out-of-order HTTP round trip → published → served byte-for-byte, missing-chunk 422, chunk errors, cancel, up-front 415/413/422/quota, ownership/auth) + real-PG `store.TestUploadSessionQueriesPersist`.)
- [~] Implement upload progress/status in Redis and database. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — the resumable-upload slice makes the durable `upload_chunks` received-chunk ledger the single progress source: `GET /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id` returns received chunk ns + bytes (the resume contract) straight from PostgreSQL, so Redis is not required for progress. Documented on the P6.1 resumable-upload item below.)
- [x] Implement video metadata validation: title, description, tags, category, language, license, privacy, channel. (title/description/privacy/channel long done. **category/language/license DONE**: migration 0025 adds three nullable TEXT columns to `videos`; `POST /channels/:handle/videos` + `PATCH /videos/:id` accept optional `category`/`language`/`license`, validated against the canonical `video.IsCategory`/`IsLanguage`/`IsLicense` maps (unknown/empty → 422 field error); stored on create (empty → NULL) and COALESCE-partial on update; exposed on the `Video` detail/create/update views (omitted when unset). sqlc CreateVideo/GetVideoByID/UpdateVideo/SetVideoState carry the columns; openapi `Video`/`CreateVideoRequest`/`UpdateVideoRequest` document them (drift guard green). Tested: handler `TestVideoTaxonomyMetadata` (store+round-trip on detail, unset→omitted, unknown-on-create 422, partial update preserves siblings, unknown/empty-on-update 422) + `config_test` validators. **VERIFIED** end-to-end against real PG (migration applies; create/detail/update/422 flow + psql-confirmed row). Clearing a set value back to unset is not yet supported (COALESCE can't distinguish keep from clear). **Free-form tags DONE** (product-decisions §18): migration 0044 `video_tags` (video FK ON DELETE CASCADE, lowercase+length CHECK, PK(video_id, tag), tag index); `tags: []` accepted on create (`CreateInput.Tags`) and PATCH (`UpdateInput.Tags *[]string` — full replace, `[]` clears, absent keeps); normalized via `video.NormalizeTags` (lowercase/trim/dedupe, first-seen order) with §18 limits enforced in the HTTP layer (`validateTags`: >5 distinct or >50 chars → 422); exposed on create/update/detail views (`Video.tags`, omitted when empty). Feed gains `GET /videos?tag=` (exact match on the stored lowercased set, case-insensitive) **plus `category=`/`language=` filters validated against the taxonomy maps (unknown → 422 — the vidra-user filter-controls contract)** via `video.FeedFilter` → `ListPublicVideosSorted` nargs; search (`SearchPublicVideos`) now also matches tag substrings (title-similarity ranking keeps tag-only matches later). openapi: `tags` on Video/Create/Update schemas + feed `tag`/`category`/`language` params + 422 (drift guard + lint green). Tested: video unit (NormalizeTags table, create-stores-normalized, update-replace/clear/keep) + handler (`videos_tags_test.go`: lifecycle round-trip, 422 limits incl. dupes-collapse-then-pass, feed tag/category/language filters + case-insensitive tag + 422s, search-by-tag) + real-PG integration (`TestVideoTagsPersistAndFilter`: insert/list/replace, feed tag+category narrowing, search-by-tag, delete-cascade). `make ci` green.)
- [x] Implement privacy levels. (videos: public/unlisted/private enforced on read — private hidden as 404 to non-owners. **Account/channel-level privacy DONE** (product-decisions §16, minimal): migration 0047 adds `users.unlisted BOOL NOT NULL DEFAULT false`; `PATCH /auth/me` accepts `unlisted` (tri-state pointer through `auth.ProfileInput.Unlisted` → `UpdateUserProfile` COALESCE, so unrelated edits keep the flag) and GET/PATCH `/auth/me` echo it on `User.unlisted`. Discovery joins exclude unlisted owners: `ListPublicVideosSorted` (feed) + `SearchPublicVideos` gain `NOT EXISTS (users u WHERE u.id=c.owner_id AND u.unlisted)` — while **direct URLs still serve** (video detail, `/channels/:handle` page + its public video list, subscriptions feed (explicit follows ≠ discovery) all unchanged). No channel-directory endpoint exists yet; when one lands it must reuse this exclusion. Full per-field profile privacy is OUT per §16. openapi: `User.unlisted` + `UpdateProfileRequest.unlisted` + feed/search descriptions (drift guard + redocly lint green). Tested: handler (`unlisted_test.go` `TestAccountUnlistedFlag`: toggle round-trip on /auth/me, feed+search exclude while unlisted, direct channel list + video detail still 200, unrelated PATCH keeps the flag, relist restores discovery) + real-PG integration (`TestUnlistedOwnerExcludedFromDiscovery`: flag flip through the profile-update query removes from feed+search queries, direct channel list keeps serving, COALESCE keeps flag on unrelated edit, relist restores; migration 0047 down/up round-trips). `make ci` green.)
- [x] Implement publish date/scheduled publish. (product-decisions §17: migration 0045 adds nullable `videos.publish_at` + widens the state CHECK with **`scheduled`** + a partial due index (`(publish_at) WHERE state='scheduled'`; down migration folds scheduled→draft before re-narrowing). `publish_at` settable on create + PATCH (RFC3339; must be future → 422 `validatePublishAt`; PATCH additionally refuses once published → `video.ErrPublished` → 422 field error). **Representation decision:** a processed upload whose publish_at is future parks in `state='scheduled'` (Process checks the row after scan/probe/thumbnail) — public surfaces already filter `state='published'` so feeds/search/channel-public lists stay correct, while direct links behave like any non-published video; a past/absent publish_at publishes immediately. The publish transition itself is factored into `video.Service.publish` (SetVideoState published + federation-announce + transcode-enqueue hooks) and BOTH Process and the sweeper run through it, so scheduled publishes fire the exact same hooks as direct ones. Sweeper: `PublishDue` (ListDueScheduledVideos JOIN original file → publish; per-video failure stays scheduled for retry) driven by `runScheduledPublishWorker` in cmd/api (10s ticker, batch 20, mirrors the transcode worker; always on — cheap partial-index scan). Detail/create/update views + the owner (studio) channel list expose `publish_at` (`FeedItem.PublishAt` only on the owner list). openapi: Video.state gains `scheduled`, `publish_at` on Video/Create/Update (drift guard + lint green). Tested: video unit (`schedule_test.go`: hold-no-hooks, PublishDue-runs-the-publish-transition-with-both-hooks + idempotent, past-publish_at-publishes-immediately, ErrPublished guard) + handler (`videos_schedule_test.go`: full HTTP flow create→upload→scheduled→off-feed→due→sweep→published-on-feed; 422s past-create/past-patch/after-publish) + real-PG integration (`TestScheduledPublishPersists`: persist, CHECK accepts scheduled, due scan excludes future + published, key join; migration down/up round-trips). `make ci` green.)
- [x] Implement file validation. (upload enforces a size cap — `UPLOAD_MAX_SIZE`, default 2G, via a per-route body limit so the upload route is exempt from the small JSON `HTTP_BODY_LIMIT`; oversize → 413 — and an extension allowlist of video containers; unaccepted → 415, checked after ownership so non-owners still see 404. The once-remaining piece, authoritative FFprobe content validation, landed with the probe slice: `cmd/api` wires `video.WithProber(media.DetectFFProbe(blobs))`, a probe failure → `state=failed`, and ffmpeg ships in the runtime image — see P6.3.)
- [x] Implement ClamAV scan integration. (`internal/media/clamav.go` — clamd INSTREAM client satisfying `video.Scanner`; `Process` scans BEFORE probe/publish; wired in `cmd/api` when `MALWARE_SCAN_ENABLED` (+`CLAMAV_ADDR` validated; compose passthrough — the operator provides clamd). Tested: video service (clean→published, infected→failed, scan-error→failed) + media against a fake clamd (OK/FOUND/garbage/dial-failure). See also the P15 malware-scan-hooks item.)
- [x] Implement ClamAV fallback modes: fail-closed, fail-open, quarantine. (core-media-2, 2026-07-03. `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE` config (validated) → `video.ScanMode` (fail-closed default | fail-open | quarantine) honored by `Process`: it now distinguishes a scan ERROR (dial/protocol/IO) from an INFECTED result. **Infected ALWAYS fails**, in every mode. On a scan error: fail-closed → `state=failed` (unchanged default); fail-open → publish anyway + a loud `slog.Warn` (no secrets, video id only); quarantine → `state='quarantined'` (parks in the EXISTING P9 moderation queue — approve/reject already wired — no publish hooks fire). Wired via `WithScanMode` in cmd/api. Tested: 6 video-service unit (`scanmode_test.go`: fail-closed error→failed, fail-open error→published, fail-open infected→still failed, quarantine error→quarantined + transcode hook suppressed, quarantine infected→failed, `ValidScanMode` table) + config validation (default/override/invalid). `make ci` + integration green.)
- [x] Implement URL import with SSRF protection. (`POST /api/v1/videos/:id/import` (requireAuth, owner-only), body `{url}` — the URL-import counterpart of `POST /videos/:id/file`. Flow: `urlsafety.ValidateURL` (http/https + non-public literal-IP reject → 422); **ownership checked BEFORE any fetch** so a non-owner (or unknown video) is 404 and the server never fetches on their behalf; fetch via `urlsafety.NewClient` (SSRF-guarded at dial time — loopback/private/link-local/CGNAT/DNS-rebinding/internal-redirects refused → 422); non-200 → 422; `Content-Length > UPLOAD_MAX_SIZE` → 413 and a streaming `maxBytesReader` hard-caps a lying/absent length; then reuse `video.AttachOriginal` (container type from the URL path extension, or — when the path has none, e.g. `/download?id=…` or the backend's own `/original` — from the response **Content-Type** via `extForContentType`; unknown → 415) + `Process` (publish/fail) → 201 `UploadVideoFileResponse`, identical to upload. The fetch client is injectable in tests (`Server.importClient`) so the happy path is exercised against a loopback httptest origin while production always uses the guard. The guard is config-driven — `urlsafety.Guard{AllowPrivate: cfg.ImportAllowPrivateURLs}` — so the dev/test `HTTP_IMPORT_ALLOW_PRIVATE_URLS` knob (P15, compose default off) lets a real backend import from a loopback/compose-network origin, which is what makes the **frontend URL-import backed e2e provable in CI**. openapi documents the route + `ImportVideoRequest` (drift guard green). Tested: `videos_import_test.go` — happy path (import→published→publicly served), 415 non-video ext, **SSRF guard blocks loopback via the default client (422)**, **allow-private config imports a literal-127.0.0.1 origin (201)**, **Content-Type fallback imports an extension-less URL (201)**, missing/ftp url 422, anon 401, non-owner 404. **Backed-import now fully enabled**: with `HTTP_IMPORT_ALLOW_PRIVATE_URLS=true` a real backend can import its OWN extension-less `/original` via the compose service name (`http://api:8080/api/v1/videos/:src/original`) — verified live (201), so a `vidra-user` backed e2e can prove the studio "Import from URL" flow without any external origin (compose also adds `extra_hosts host.docker.internal:host-gateway` as an alternative host origin). Torrent/magnet import is separate below. **UPDATE (core-upload-resumable, 2026-07-03): migrated fully off the synchronous request path.** `POST /videos/:id/import` now enqueues an `import_jobs` row and returns **202 `{import_job}`** (single active per video → re-posting returns the in-flight job); the SSRF-guarded fetch + size/quota caps + `AttachOriginal`/`Process` moved to `internal/videoimport` + `runVideoImportWorker` (cmd/api, 10s ticker, retry/backoff/dead-letter after 5). `GET /videos/:id/import` (owner) returns the job status `{id, video_id, state pending/running/done/failed, error (SAFE reason — never a raw error or the URL), attempts, created_at, updated_at}` — the new shape the frontend URL-import lane consumes. Up-front URL validation (scheme/literal-IP) still 422s synchronously; the fetch-time SSRF block, 415, 413, and quota become async job failures. openapi updated (POST→202 `ImportJobResponse`, new GET). Import tests rewritten: httpapi drives the worker via `srv.importsvc.DrainJobs` (`videos_import_test.go` 202→drain→done→published/served, non-video→safe failure, idempotent-while-active, up-front 422/401/404); `internal/videoimport` unit (happy path, non-video safe failure, **SSRF guard blocks loopback via the default client**, allow-private config, quota, **failure→retry→dead-letter**); real-PG `store.TestImportJobQueuePersists`.)
- [~] Implement torrent/magnet import placeholder or adapter boundary. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — product-decisions.md §4. WebTorrent-based import/playback is legacy-PeerTube architecture Vidra replaces with HLS + storage backends; URL import already covers "fetch a file from elsewhere", so no hidden torrent tab and no adapter boundary. Mirrored in the PeerTube ledger row PT-PUBLISH-IMPORT-TORRENT.)
- [x] Implement upload cancellation. (`DELETE /api/v1/uploads/:upload_id` (owner, idempotent) cancels a resumable session; the sweeper then deletes its chunk blobs. Covered by the P6.1 resumable-upload tests — `uploads_test.go` cancel case.)
- [x] Implement failed upload cleanup. (`runUploadSweepWorker` in cmd/api (1-min ticker) deletes expired/cancelled sessions' chunk blobs via `PrefixDeleter.DeletePrefix` then the rows — sessions expire 24h after creation. Covered by the `internal/upload` expiry-sweep unit test with an injected clock. See the P6.1 resumable-upload item.)
- [x] Add API tests for file upload, URL import, validation errors, and scan failure. (file upload covered: success→processing, refetch, missing-field 400, non-owner/unknown 404, plus service-level store/replace/key tests. **URL import now covered** (`videos_import_test.go`: happy path, 415, SSRF-guard-blocks-loopback 422, missing/ftp-url 422, anon 401, non-owner 404). **Resumable-upload + async-import API tests added** (core-upload-resumable): `uploads_test.go` (chunk round trip/complete/cancel/up-front validation) + `videos_import_test.go` (rewritten for the 202→worker→done flow) + `internal/upload`/`internal/videoimport` unit + real-PG store tests. **ClamAV scan-failure now covered at the HTTP boundary** (core-sweep, 2026-07-03): `internal/httpapi/upload_scan_test.go` `TestUploadVideoFileScanOutcomes` drives the real `POST /videos/:id/file` handler with an injected `video.Scanner` (a local `scanFake`) returning infected/error, wired exactly as cmd/api does (`WithScanner` + the config-driven `WithScanMode(cfg.MalwareScanMode)`), and asserts the upload FINALISES (201) to the expected state per `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE`: infected→failed in every mode; scan-error→failed (fail-closed), →published (fail-open), →quarantined (quarantine); the quarantined video is additionally proven hidden from an anonymous detail read (404). The service-layer matrix stays in `internal/video/scanmode_test.go`; this proves the handler surfaces the same outcome end to end.)
- [x] UPLOAD-13 ClamAV upload scanning (pulled forward from W2, user-requested 2026-07-07). (backport-audit slice, 2026-07-07. The vertical slice already existed — clamd INSTREAM client + `video.Scanner`/`Process` integration (P6.1 line 317, P15 line 890), `MALWARE_SCAN_ENABLED`/`CLAMAV_ADDR`/`MALWARE_SCAN_MODE` config + fail-closed/fail-open/quarantine (line 318), DM-attachment scanning fail-closed (P11.1 line 816), compose `scan` profile (line 125), HTTP-boundary scan-outcome tests (line 323). This slice **closes the two remaining REQUIRED-SHAPE gaps** and adds an audit trail: (1) **configurable per-scan timeout** — new `CLAMAV_TIMEOUT` config (`getEnvDuration`, default 60s, validated `>0` when scanning enabled) replaces the hard-coded 60s deadline; `media.NewClamAV(addr, blobs, timeout)` bounds dial+stream+verdict so a slow/wedged clamd becomes a scan *error* (resolved by `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE`) not a hung upload — unit-tested with a non-responding fake clamd (`TestClamAVScanTimeoutIsError`) + config default/override/reject-non-positive tests. (2) **EICAR real-clamd integration test** — `internal/media/clamav_integration_test.go` (`//go:build integration`) streams the assembled EICAR string to a real clamd at `CLAMAV_TEST_ADDR` (self-skips when unset, so `make ci` stays green) and asserts infected + a benign file clean. (3) **audit event** — any scan outcome that keeps an upload out of `published` (infection; unscannable under fail-closed/quarantine) now writes `content.upload.malware_rejected` via a new optional `video.WithAuditor` seam (mirrors `live.Service`), reason = `video=<id> outcome=infected|scan_error policy=<mode>`, no file content; fail-open (publishes) and clean never audit — covered by `internal/video/scanaudit_test.go` (7-case table + auditor-optional). Docs: `.env.example`, `docker-compose.yml` (CLAMAV_TIMEOUT passthrough + **memory-footprint caveat** ~1.5-2 GiB resident, why scanning is opt-in), README, `.ralph/AGENT.md` (integration-test command). No OpenAPI change (no route/shape change). `make ci` green (fmt-check + vet + openapi-verify + sqlc-verify + test-race). **Follow-up (DONE 2026-07-07 — see next line):** the EICAR `-tags=integration` test now runs in CI via a clamd service container in `backend-integration.yml`.)
- [x] Run the EICAR real-clamd integration test in CI (UPLOAD-13 follow-up). (2026-07-07. `backend-integration.yml` gained a `clamav` service — `clamav/clamav:1.4` (the non-`_base` tag ships the signature DB baked in, so clamd loads it from disk with **no** network download) plus `CLAMAV_NO_FRESHCLAM=true` (skips the slow/flaky freshclam update on boot), port `3310:3310`, healthcheck `clamdcheck.sh` (interval 10s / timeout 10s / retries 30 / start-period 60s — pings clamd, only healthy once the DB is loaded and it accepts connections). The "Run integration tests" step now exports `CLAMAV_TEST_ADDR=localhost:3310`, so `internal/media/clamav_integration_test.go` (`TestClamAVRealDetectsEICAR`/`…PassesBenign`) AND `internal/messaging/eicar_integration_test.go` (`TestAttachmentEICARInDocxRejected`, EICAR-in-docx) execute against the real daemon instead of self-skipping — a scanner regression now fails the job loudly. CI-parity respected: no `continue-on-error` added, `make ci`/`backend-ci.yml` untouched, `ci-guard.yml` stays green. Proven locally on this Apple-Silicon host under `--platform linux/amd64` emulation: `docker run … clamav/clamav:1.4 -e CLAMAV_NO_FRESHCLAM=true --health-cmd clamdcheck.sh` reached `healthy` in 184s (emulation-inflated; native amd64 runners are far faster), then `CLAMAV_TEST_ADDR=localhost:3310 go test -tags integration ./internal/media/ ./internal/messaging/` → all three EICAR tests PASS (and SKIP again with the addr unset). Workflow-only change, no Go/OpenAPI/migration delta; `make ci` green.)

## P6.2 Storage

- [x] Implement storage interface. (`internal/storage.Backend` — Put/Open/Delete/Exists over forward-slash object keys; `ErrInvalidKey`/`ErrNotFound`)
- [x] Implement local storage backend. (`internal/storage.Local` rooted dir, creates parent dirs, idempotent delete; path-traversal-safe key resolution — tested incl. an escape-attempt that cannot write outside root)
- [x] Implement S3-compatible backend. (`internal/storage.S3` via the official MinIO Go SDK `github.com/minio/minio-go/v7` — much lighter than aws-sdk-go and purpose-built for S3-compatibles. Satisfies `Backend` with the SAME semantics as `Local`: shared `validateKey` (empty/absolute/NUL/`..`-segment → `ErrInvalidKey`, checked before any network call), lazy-GetObject forced eager via `Stat()` so a missing object is `ErrNotFound` at `Open` (not first read), idempotent `Delete`, streaming `Put` (size -1 multipart; no whole-file buffering). Config: `STORAGE_BACKEND=s3` now accepted + `STORAGE_S3_ENDPOINT` (host[:port], scheme rejected)/`BUCKET`/`ACCESS_KEY`/`SECRET_KEY`/`REGION`/`USE_SSL` (default true)/`FORCE_PATH_STYLE` — validated when backend=s3; creds are secrets (sensitive-key denylist extended with `secret_key`/`access_key`; boot log carries only endpoint/bucket/region). `cmd/api` wires it and `EnsureBucket` at startup (fail-fast; auto-creates for dev MinIO). **No `PathProvider` by design** (pinned by `TestS3IsNotAPathProvider`): ffprobe/thumbnailer/HLS-transcoder/clamav all read via the existing `media.objectPath` temp-download fallback, and `serveStoredObjectNamed` now detects a seekable `Open` reader (a `*minio.Object` seeks via ranged GETs) and routes it through `http.ServeContent`, so `/original`+`/thumbnail`+`/hls/*` keep full Range/206 support on S3 (previously non-path backends degraded to full-body 200s). compose `storage` profile adds `minio` (pinned image, `mc ready local` healthcheck, named volume, dev-only creds; api `depends_on` it with `required:false`); run-against-minio env documented at the top of docker-compose.yml + `.env.example` + README. Tested: unit (config validation table, ErrInvalidKey-before-network table, PathProvider pin, `validateKey` table) + handler (`TestStreamOriginalRangeViaSeekableBackend` 200/206/Content-Range + missing-object 404 through a path-less fake) + MinIO round-trip below.)
- [x] Implement Backblaze B2-compatible configuration. (Documentation, not code — B2 speaks the S3 API: endpoint `s3.<region>.backblazeb2.com`, key id/application key as access/secret, path-style works. Documented in `.env.example`, `S3Config` godoc, and README.)
- [x] Implement DigitalOcean Spaces-compatible configuration. (Documentation, not code — endpoint `<region>.digitaloceanspaces.com` (bucket NOT in the endpoint) + `STORAGE_S3_REGION=<region>`. Documented in `.env.example`, `S3Config` godoc, and README.)
- [~] Implement IPFS backend adapter or deferred spec. (DEFERRED — product-decisions.md §5. Pinning economics, GC semantics, and gateway trust need their own spec; local + S3 cover real deployments today. `STORAGE_BACKEND=ipfs` returns a config validation error naming that decision. Mirrored in the extensions ledger row VIDRA-IPFS-STORAGE.)
- [x] Implement object key naming strategy. (PeerTube-aligned, one top-level dir per asset kind — `.ralph/specs/storage-layout.md`: originals `web-videos/<id><ext>`, thumbnails `thumbnails/<id>.jpg`, captions `captions/<id>/<lang>.vtt`, HLS `streaming-playlists/<id>/master.m3u8` + `<id>/<height>p/{playlist.m3u8,seg_NNNNN.ts}`.)
- [x] Implement private/public object handling. (all stored objects are private by default — nothing is served from a public bucket or static path; every read goes through the authenticated API with per-request visibility checks (`video.FileForView`: private → owner-only 404, blocked → mods-only) — see the storage-layout.md notes on id-based keys being safe for exactly this reason.)
- [x] Implement signed URL or proxy strategy. (proxy strategy implemented: `/original`, `/thumbnail`, `/hls/*`, captions, and avatars/banners are all proxied through the API via `serveStoredObject` with Range/206 support (`http.ServeContent` over `storage.PathProvider` or a seekable backend reader — works on local AND S3/MinIO). Signed URLs deferred until a CDN/offload need exists.)
- [x] Implement media deletion/garbage collection. (core-media-2, 2026-07-03. `internal/mediagc` sweep: lists stored keys under a FIXED set of KNOWN prefixes (`web-videos`, `thumbnails`, `storyboards`, `captions`, `streaming-playlists`, `playlist-thumbnails`) via a new `storage.ObjectLister` capability (Local `WalkDir` + S3 `ListObjects`, both feature-detected — GC self-disables on a non-listing backend with `ErrListingUnsupported`) and deletes only objects with NO DB reference. Reference set = all `video_files`/`captions` keys + playlist-thumbnail keys + the HLS tree kept per LIVE video id (segments aren't individually in the DB). **NEVER lists or touches an unknown prefix** (avatars/uploads/remote thumbnails are owned by other lifecycles). Admin `POST /api/v1/admin/media/gc` (requireRole admin, body `{dry_run}` default **true**; dry-run returns the would-delete list, `dry_run:false` deletes; audited `admin.media.gc`; 503 on a non-listing backend) + a daily scheduled `runMediaGCWorker` in cmd/api (audited, actor=system). sqlc `media_gc.sql` (ListAll{VideoFileKeys,CaptionKeys,VideoIDs}, ListPlaylistThumbnailRefs). openapi documents the route + `MediaGCRequest`/`MediaGCResponse`. Tested: mediagc unit (orphan detection incl. dead-HLS-tree + unknown-prefix-untouched, dry-run vs delete, lister-required) + storage Local `ListKeys` unit + httpapi handler (dry-run→delete, admin-gated 403/401) + **MinIO integration** (`storage.TestS3ListKeys` + `mediagc.TestSweepAgainstMinIO`, S3_TEST_ENDPOINT self-skip). `make ci` + integration green.)
- [x] Add integration tests using local filesystem and MinIO. (Local: the full `local_test.go` suite (round trip, idempotent delete, ErrNotFound, traversal-cannot-escape-root) runs in `make ci`. MinIO: `internal/storage/s3_integration_test.go` under `-tags=integration`, gated on `S3_TEST_ENDPOINT` (self-skips when unset, repo style): Put/Open round trip, overwrite, Exists/Delete + idempotent re-delete, Open-missing → ErrNotFound, `TestS3ServeContentRange` (real `*minio.Object` is an `io.ReadSeeker`; `http.ServeContent` serves `bytes=2-5` as a true 206 + Content-Range — the exact `serveStoredObject` path), and `TestS3TempDownloadFallbackContract` (stream-to-temp-file byte fidelity, the ffprobe/transcode path). Run: `docker compose --profile storage up -d minio && S3_TEST_ENDPOINT=localhost:9000 go test -tags=integration ./internal/storage/...` — documented in testing.md + AGENT.md. VERIFIED locally against the compose minio.)

## P6.3 Transcoding

> Publish-pipeline seam landed: after upload, `video.Service.Process` finalises a
> video `processing → published` (or `failed`) via an injected `Prober` interface
> (`internal/video`). With no prober configured (current default) the original is
> trusted and published directly — the extension allow-list already gated the
> upload. The real FFprobe/transcode implementation slots into this seam via
> `video.WithProber(...)` in `cmd/api` once FFmpeg is in the runtime image. The
> public surfaces already filter `state='published'`.

- [x] Implement FFmpeg probe. (`internal/media.FFProbe` shells out to `ffprobe -print_format json`; pure JSON parser unit-tested with fixtures, exec path in a `//go:build integration` test excluded from `make ci`; wired via `media.DetectFFProbe` in `cmd/api` only when ffprobe is on PATH — graceful publish-unprobed fallback otherwise; ffmpeg added to the runtime image. Reads originals via the new `storage.PathProvider` capability, temp-download fallback for non-path backends.)
- [x] Implement media metadata extraction. (probe extracts duration_seconds/width/height into `video_metadata` (migration 0009, 1:1 side table) during `Process`; unknown measures stored NULL; `GET /api/v1/videos/:id` exposes them, omitted when absent.)
- [x] Implement H.264 profile. (The HLS ladder encodes libx264 `-profile:v main` + AAC — see the HLS item below. VP9/AV1 remain separate options.)
- [x] Implement VP9 profile. (core-media-2, 2026-07-03. `TRANSCODING_VP9_ENABLED` config (default false; only meaningful with `TRANSCODING_ENABLED`). **Packaging decision (documented, INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE):** VP9 is emitted as a **progressive VP9/WebM alternate** (libvpx-vp9 + libopus at the top ladder rung), NOT an HLS variant — HLS+VP9 needs fMP4/CMAF with patchy client support. `media.HLSTranscoder.SetVP9(true)` makes `Transcode` additionally encode `streaming-playlists/<id>/vp9.webm` (best-effort — a VP9 failure never fails the H.264 HLS); the transcode worker stores it as a `kind='webm'` video_file. Surfaced via `GET /videos/{id}/download` (kind "webm") + served with Range/206 at `GET /videos/{id}/webm` (same visibility as /original). Pure builder `vp9WebMArgs` unit-tested; `transcode` DrainJobs stores/omits the webm; wired via `SetVP9(cfg.TranscodingVP9Enabled)` in cmd/api. openapi documents `/videos/{id}/webm` + the download "webm" kind. `make ci` + integration green.)
- [x] Implement AV1 profile. (DEFERRED, config-rejected — mirrors the IPFS-storage defer. `TRANSCODING_AV1_ENABLED=true` fails config validation with a documented defer note pointing here; leave it false. Revisit once libaom/SVT-AV1 encode cost + client support justify it. Tested: `config.TestLoadRejectsAV1`.)
- [x] Implement HLS output. (**Full HLS pipeline shipped** (P6.3 + P2.2 + P7 in one slice), gated by `TRANSCODING_ENABLED` (default **true** since 2026-07-07 — was false; `.env.example` + compose pass-through; requires ffmpeg+ffprobe on PATH — `media.DetectHLSTranscoder`, warn-and-disable otherwise). `internal/media/hls.go` `HLSTranscoder`: probes source dimensions, plans an H.264/AAC ladder from 1080/720/480/360 **capped at source height** (no upscaling; sub-360 sources get one rung at their own even-rounded size — cap-at-source), encodes each rung (`libx264` main, AAC, ~4s MPEG-TS segments, `-hls_playlist_type vod`) into a temp dir, renders a master.m3u8 with correct BANDWIDTH/RESOLUTION, then Puts everything under `streaming-playlists/<video_id>/` (storage-layout.md updated to **in use**); ALL playlist URIs relative so the authenticated API can proxy them. Pure builders (`PlanHLSLadder`/`hlsRungArgs`/`renderMasterPlaylist`) unit-tested without exec (`hls_test.go`: full ladder, cap-at-source, tiny-source single rung, portrait/odd even-rounding, args, master rendering + relative URIs); exec path behind `-tags=integration` (`TestHLSTranscoderRealVideo`: real 320x240 testsrc → master+variant+segments in storage, relative segment URIs). Pipeline: `video.WithTranscodeHook` fires on the publish transition in `Process` (best-effort, never blocks publish; skipped on failed scan/probe — tested both ways in video/service_test.go); wired in cmd/api to `transcode.Service.Enqueue`. Worker: `runTranscodeWorker` goroutine in cmd/api (ticker 10s, batch 2, mirrors `runFederationDeliveryWorker`) → `transcode.Service.DrainJobs` claims due jobs (UPDATE…RETURNING → `running`), runs the transcoder, stores renditions + upserts the playlist `ready`, completes the job. HTTP: `GET /videos/{id}/hls/master.m3u8` + `GET /videos/{id}/hls/{rendition}/{file}` (see P7). openapi documents both + `Video.hls_url`/`renditions` + `VideoRendition` (drift guard green). **End-to-end proof** (`httpapi/hls_integration_test.go` `TestHLSPipelineEndToEnd`): real ffmpeg fixture uploaded over HTTP → publish hook enqueues → real `DrainJobs` transcodes → detail carries hls_url + the single 320x240 rung → master/variant/segments served with correct content types, segments verified MPEG-TS.)
- [x] Fix "transcode by default" was half-applied → uploads couldn't switch resolutions. (2026-07-07, core-media. **Symptom:** after upload, playback was stuck at one resolution with no quality selector, despite commits 7f8e5f3 ("transcode by default") + 6859b4e ("default TRANSCODING_ENABLED=true in the local env template"). **Root cause (backend config, NOT the transcoder):** those commits flipped `docker-compose.yml` (`${TRANSCODING_ENABLED:-true}`) and the meta-repo `env/*.env` templates to true, but left vidra-core's own `TRANSCODING_ENABLED` default at **false** in TWO places — `internal/config/config.go` (`getEnvBool(...,false)`) and `.env.example` (`=false`, "Off by default"). The AGENT.md-documented host dev path (`cp .env.example .env && make run`) therefore ran with transcoding OFF → no HLS ladder → `hls_url` null on the detail → the `vidra-user` player fell back to the single progressive `/original` → one resolution, no quality menu. The transcoder/ladder/master-playlist/serving were all correct the whole time (proven: a real 1280x720 upload through the live stack produced hls_url + 3 renditions {720p,480p,360p} and a 3-variant master.m3u8; master→variant→segment all served 200). **Fix:** default `TRANSCODING_ENABLED=true` in `config.go` (graceful: `DetectHLSTranscoder` still warn-and-disables when ffmpeg/ffprobe are absent, so the default is safe on a bare host) + `.env.example=true` with a corrected comment; README + this plan updated. **Regression tests:** `config_test.go TestTranscodingEnabledByDefault` (default on, explicit `=false` opts out); `httpapi/hls_test.go TestHLSMultiRenditionLadderExposed` (in `make ci` — a multi-rung video exposes all 3 renditions on the detail AND serves a 3-variant master, closing the gap where every prior HLS test used a single-rung fixture); `media/hls_integration_test.go TestHLSTranscoderHDLadderMultipleRenditions` (`-tags=integration` — real ffmpeg 1280x720 → 3 renditions + 3-variant master + variant playlists/segments stored). **Frontend follow-up (vidra-user, NOT this repo):** none required — `lib/use-hls-playback.ts` + `components/QualityMenu.tsx` already load the master and expose hls.js levels; when W1 replaces the player it must keep loading `hls_url` (`/api/v1/videos/{id}/hls/master.m3u8`), map `hls.levels[].height` to a selector, and drive `hls.currentLevel` (-1 = Auto).)
- [x] Implement thumbnail generation. (`internal/media.Thumbnailer` shells out to ffmpeg to grab one scaled JPEG poster frame; pure seek/arg builders unit-tested, exec behind `-tags=integration`. `Process` generates it best-effort after a successful probe (never blocks publish), stored as a `kind='thumbnail'` video_file (migration 0010 widens the kind CHECK) at `videos/<id>/thumbnail.jpg`. Served by `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/thumbnail` (same visibility as detail) reusing `serveStoredObject`; detail exposes `has_thumbnail`. Wired via `media.DetectThumbnailer` in `cmd/api` only when ffmpeg is present. **Custom thumbnail upload now DONE too**: `POST /api/v1/videos/:id/thumbnail` (requireAuth, owner-only, multipart `file`) stores a creator-supplied poster (JPEG/PNG/WebP by extension → 415 otherwise; served Content-Type derived from the extension, not the client-declared type), replacing any generated/previous thumbnail at the same deterministic key (one poster per video); no state change; non-owner/unknown → 404; bounded by the global 8M body limit. `video.SetThumbnail` + `acceptedImageExts`; `internal/httpapi/videos.go` `handleSetVideoThumbnail` → 201 `VideoFile`. openapi documents the POST (drift guard green). Tested: `videos_thumbnail_test.go` — upload→served with derived CT + detail `has_thumbnail`, 415 non-image, 401 anon, 404 non-owner. **This unblocks the `vidra-user` studio thumbnail-editing feature** (was DEFERRED on this contract).)
- [x] Implement preview generation. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — the video **thumbnail** IS the preview (poster frame + custom upload, both done). A separate animated hover-preview (short muted webm/gif) is NOT built; the storyboard (below) covers seek-bar scrubbing, which is the higher-value PeerTube parity behaviour. Documented as an intentional difference; revisit if an animated preview is later prioritized.)
- [x] Implement storyboard generation or documented defer. (core-media-2, 2026-07-03. `media.Storyboarder` (ffmpeg `fps`+`scale`+`pad`+`tile` filter → a sprite sheet of ≤100 160x90 tiles, one every ceil(duration/100)s) produces the sprite JPEG + a WebVTT sprite-map (one `#xywh` cue per tile, sprite referenced relatively). Generated best-effort in `video.Process` after probe/thumbnail (so it runs whether or not transcoding is on — spec's "after transcode, or probe when transcoding off"), stored as `kind='storyboard'`/`'storyboard_vtt'` video_files at `storyboards/<id>.{jpg,vtt}`. Served visibility-gated at `GET /videos/{id}/storyboard.{jpg,vtt}` (mirrors /original), and the detail exposes `has_storyboard`. Wired via `media.DetectStoryboarder` in cmd/api (ffmpeg+ffprobe on PATH). Pure builders `PlanStoryboard`/`storyboardArgs`/`RenderStoryboardVTT` unit-tested (grid/interval/cap, row-wrap `#xywh`, relative sprite ref); video-service `TestProcessStoresStoryboard`/`…FailureStillPublishes`; httpapi serving + private-visibility + has_storyboard + absent-404. openapi documents both routes + `Video.has_storyboard`. `make ci` + integration green.)
- [x] Implement worker queue for transcode jobs. (migration 0039 `transcode_jobs` durable queue mirroring `federation_deliveries` 0038: state pending/running/done/failed (CHECK), attempts, `next_attempt_at`, `last_error`, partial due index, and a partial UNIQUE on `(video_id) WHERE state IN (pending,running)` so enqueue is idempotent per live job (`ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`). sqlc `EnqueueTranscodeJob`/`ClaimDueTranscodeJobs` (atomic UPDATE…RETURNING claim → running)/`CompleteTranscodeJob`/`RescheduleTranscodeJob`/`FailTranscodeJob`. `internal/transcode` service + in-process cmd/api worker goroutine. Real-PG integration `TestTranscodeQueuePersistsClaimRescheduleComplete` (enqueue-dedupe, claim-once, future-reschedule-not-due, complete, re-enqueue-after-done).)
- [x] Implement retry/backoff/dead-letter behavior. (`transcode.Service.recordFailure`: reschedule to pending with exponential backoff (1m base, doubling, 1h cap — `TestBackoffDoublesAndCaps`) recording a bounded `last_error`; after 5 attempts the job dead-letters (`failed`) and the video's streaming playlist is upserted `failed` for observability. Unit-tested state machine with a fake runner (`service_test.go`: success→done+ready+renditions, failure→pending+attempts+future-next-attempt+no-rerun-before-due, dead-letter after max attempts + playlist failed + fresh enqueue possible).)
- [~] Implement status updates in Redis and PostgreSQL. (PostgreSQL `videos.state` transitions draft→processing→published/failed via `SetVideoState`; the transcode outcome is tracked in `streaming_playlists.state` (pending/ready/failed) + `transcode_jobs.state`. Live Redis progress for in-flight jobs still TODO.)

Worker state machine unit tests in `internal/transcode/service_test.go` with fake repo + fake transcoder.)

Both self-skip without ffmpeg.)

# P7 — Playback, Discovery, and Public Video API

- [x] Implement public video list endpoint. (`GET /api/v1/videos` (public, paginated limit≤100/offset) → cross-channel public videos newest-first; sqlc `ListPublicVideos`; `internal/video.ListPublic`; tested. Now filters `state='published'` — the publish pipeline landed, so feed/search/channel-public surfaces exclude draft/processing/failed.)
- [x] Implement local videos endpoint. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — product-decisions.md §15: a `?scope=local|all` query param on `GET /api/v1/videos` (default `local`; `all` UNIONs federated remote videos) replaces a dedicated `/local` route. `video.NormalizeFeedScope` + `ListPublic(...scope...)`; the scope is echoed in the feed response. Tested via the scope=all federation-feed tests.)
- [x] Implement trending/recent/popular sort modes or documented staged rollout. (`GET /api/v1/videos?sort=recent|popular|trending` (unknown → recent, echoed back in the response). `ListPublicVideosSorted` LEFT JOINs `video_view_counts` and orders by a CASE on the sort param: popular = all-time views, trending = views decayed by age (HN-style `views / (age_hours+2)^1.5`). Feed items now also carry `views` + `has_thumbnail` for cards. `internal/video.FeedItem`; tested incl. popular ordering + sort fallback.)
- [x] Implement video detail endpoint. (`GET /api/v1/videos/:id` (optionalAuth): public/unlisted to anyone, private owner-only → 404, blocked → mods-only; the detail view carries views, `has_thumbnail`, probed duration/dimensions, taxonomy metadata, and `hls_url`/`renditions` when ready. Tested: `TestGetPublicVideoIsAnonymous` + `TestGetPrivateVideoOwnerOnly` (videos_test.go) plus the taxonomy/HLS detail assertions.)
- [x] Implement video playback manifest endpoint. (Progressive: `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/original` streams the stored original with HTTP Range/206 support via `http.ServeContent` + the `storage.PathProvider` capability; visibility mirrors detail (private→owner-only/404, no-original→404). **HLS now DONE** (P6.3 pipeline): `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/hls/master.m3u8` (`application/vnd.apple.mpegurl`) + `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/hls/:rendition/:file` (variant playlists `application/vnd.apple.mpegurl`, segments `video/mp2t`) served from storage via the shared `serveStoredObject`, with the SAME visibility as `/original` (`hlsPlaylistForView`: private→owner-only 404, blocked→mods-only, playlist-not-ready→404, non-canonical rendition/file names→404 — no traversal). Video detail gains `hls_url` (present ONLY when the playlist is `ready`) + `renditions [{height,width}]`. openapi documents both routes + fields (drift guard green). Handler tests (`hls_test.go`): master/variant/segment round-trip with content types, not-ready/no-playlist 404 + detail gating, private owner-only across all three routes (anon + non-owner 404, owner 200), non-canonical names + traversal attempt 404.)
- [x] Implement captions endpoint. (full caption surface in `internal/httpapi/captions.go`: `GET /videos/:id/captions` + `GET .../captions/:lang` (public, `text/vtt`) and `POST`/`DELETE` (owner-only); tested by `TestCaptionsFlow` + `TestCaptionsValidationAndAuth`. Service details in P13.)
- [x] Implement download metadata endpoint. (`GET /api/v1/videos/:id/download` (optionalAuth) lists a video's downloadable files — what a watch-page "Download" menu consumes. Entries `{kind, url, content_type, size_bytes?, original_name?, height?, width?}`: the stored **original** (progressive `/original` URL, stored size + content type + upload filename + probed dimensions when known) plus one **hls** entry per ready rendition (variant-playlist URL `/hls/<height>p/playlist.m3u8`, `application/vnd.apple.mpegurl`, rung dimensions; no size — segmented). Visibility mirrors the detail endpoint exactly (public/unlisted → anyone, private → owner-only 404, blocked → hidden from non-mods via `videoHiddenByBlock`, unknown/malformed → 404); a visible video with no stored files (draft) returns an empty list. HLS gating mirrors `hlsDetail` (only a `ready` playlist). `internal/httpapi/downloads.go`; openapi documents the path + `VideoDownloadFile`/`VideoDownloadResponse` (drift guard green). Tested: 2 handler (`TestVideoDownloadsShape`: original-only → +HLS rendition entry with correct URL/content-type/dimensions and no size, draft → empty; `TestVideoDownloadsVisibility`: private anon/non-owner 404 + owner 200, blocked regular-user 404 + admin 200, unknown/malformed 404).)
- [~] Implement share/embed metadata endpoint. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — the machine-readable data a share/embed needs already ships on the public API: `GET /videos/:id` (detail incl. `hls_url`/renditions), `GET /videos/:id/download`, and the HLS/`/original` stream routes. The share modal + the embeddable player itself are rendered by the `vidra-user` Next.js app (`app/embed/[id]/page.tsx`), backed-e2e proven by `e2e-backed/embed.spec.ts` + `share-download.spec.ts`; the backend deliberately does not duplicate a bespoke embed-metadata endpoint. Mirrored in ledger rows PT-WATCH-SHARE-EMBED-DOWNLOAD / PT-EMBED-PLAYER-API.)
- [~] Implement oEmbed or documented difference. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — Vidra ships a first-party embeddable player route in `vidra-user` (`/embed/:id`, consuming the public detail + HLS APIs) rather than an oEmbed discovery endpoint; a JSON oEmbed provider can be added later without schema change. No hidden/placeholder endpoint exists in core.)
- [~] Implement OpenGraph metadata. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — OG/social meta tags are SSR-rendered by the `vidra-user` Next.js watch route (`generateMetadata` reading the public `GET /videos/:id` detail), the correct layer for HTML `<meta>`; vidra-core serves JSON/media only (no HTML pages) so it emits no OG tags — consistent with the P15 "no page CSP in core" decision.)
- [x] Implement search endpoint with PostgreSQL trigram search. (`GET /api/v1/videos/search?q=` (public, paginated) over public video titles; ILIKE filter ranked by `similarity()`; migration `0007` adds a `gin_trgm_ops` index on `videos.title`; sqlc `SearchPublicVideos`; `internal/video.SearchPublic`; tested. Results carry `views`/`has_thumbnail` cards. Channel/account search still TODO.)
- [x] Discovery-card consistency: search results and channel video lists (`GET /api/v1/channels/:handle/videos`, owner + public views) now LEFT JOIN view counts + thumbnail availability like the feed, so every video grid returns `views` + `has_thumbnail`. (`internal/video.FeedItem` reused; enriched `SearchPublicVideos`/`ListVideosByChannel`/`ListPublicVideosByChannel`; tested.) **Cards now also carry `channel_handle` + `channel_display_name`** (all 5 card queries `JOIN channels`; `FeedItem` + the `videoView` card projection expose them; openapi `Video` schema documents them, omitted on the detail view). This lets the frontend link a video card to `/channels/{handle}` and show the channel name — the unblock for the frontend's BLOCKED subscribe flow (a logged-in user can now reach a channel page from a card). Tested: `TestFeedCardsCarryChannelInfo` asserts the feed card returns the channel handle + name. **Cards now also carry `duration_seconds`** (the probed length, nil/omitted when unprobed): all 7 discovery-card queries (`ListPublicVideosSorted`/`SearchPublicVideos`/`ListVideosByChannel`/`ListPublicVideosByChannel`/`ListSubscriptionVideos`/`ListSavedVideos`/`ListWatchHistory`) `LEFT JOIN video_metadata` and select `vm.duration_seconds`; `FeedItem.DurationSeconds *int32` + `feedItemView` expose it on the shared `videoView.duration_seconds` field already documented on `Video` (openapi description widened to "detail + card/feed views"; drift guard green). Tested: `TestFeedCardsCarryDuration` (feed + watch-history card carry 137; unprobed video omits it). **Unblocks the frontend `vidra-user` P5 "resume progress bars on video cards" item** (was DEFERRED on `duration_seconds` on card/feed rows).
- [x] Implement tags/categories/languages/licenses config endpoints. (`GET /api/v1/videos/config` (public, static, no auth) returns the video-metadata taxonomy the frontend reads to populate its create/edit dropdowns: `{categories, licenses, languages, privacies}`, each an ordered `[{id,label}]` list. Categories + licenses use PeerTube's numeric ids (as strings) for import compatibility; languages are a curated ISO 639-1 set (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE from PeerTube's full list); privacies use Vidra's `privacy` values. Canonical data + `IsCategory`/`IsLicense`/`IsLanguage` validators in `internal/video/config.go` (the source of truth for the later metadata-validation slice, P6.312); handler `internal/httpapi/video_config.go`; route is a static `/videos/config` sitting before `/videos/:id` (Echo matches static first, like `/videos/search`). openapi documents the path + `VideoConfigResponse`/`VideoConfigOption` (drift guard green). Tested: 2 video-package (well-formed/unique ids + validators) + 1 handler (200, all four lists populated, known values). NB: free-form per-video **tags** are a video field, not a config map — deferred with the metadata-storage slice.)
- [x] Implement view count recording with abuse/rate-limit protection. (`POST /api/v1/videos/:id/view` (optionalAuth) records a view in a `video_view_counts` side table (migration 0011), deduped per viewer per hour via Redis SETNX (`cache.Deduper`, injected `video.ViewDeduper` seam; hashed user-id/IP key — no raw PII). Visibility mirrors detail; only published videos count; always 204. `views` exposed on detail. Surfacing on feed + trending sort still TODO.)
- [x] Implement watch progress endpoint. (`PUT /api/v1/videos/:id/watch-progress` (requireAuth, body `{position_seconds}` clamped ≥0, upsert) records the caller's resume position on a **public, published** video via the shared `publicVideoID` guard (else 404), bumping it to the top of history; `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/watch-progress` (requireAuth) reads it back (`{video_id, position_seconds}`, 0 when none). See the watch-history slice in P8 for the full feature + tests.)
- [x] Implement creator statistics (product-decisions §8, minimal honest v1). (Migration 0046 `video_view_days` (video FK cascade, UTC `day` DATE, views, PK(video_id, day)) — the per-day rollup upserted by `IncrementVideoViewDay` on the SAME deduped write path as `IncrementVideoViews` in `RecordView` (no backfill; pre-migration days render zero). `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/stats` (requireAuth, **owner-only — non-owner/unknown → 404** via ErrForbidden→videoError) → `{views, likes, dislikes, comments, daily_views[30]}`; `GET /api/v1/channels/:handle/stats` (requireAuth, owner-only 404-to-others) → those totals aggregated across ALL the channel's videos (any privacy/state — the owner's full numbers) + `followers` (channel domain, attached by the handler) + `videos` count + the aggregated series. Series: dense 30 UTC days oldest-first, zero-filled (`video.fillDays`/`statsWindowStart`); totals via one-shot `GetVideoEngagementTotals`/`GetChannelEngagementTotals` (COALESCE'd subqueries — no N+1). `internal/video/stats.go` + `internal/httpapi/stats.go`. openapi: both paths + `VideoStatsResponse`/`ChannelStatsResponse`/`DailyViews` (drift guard + lint green). No watch-time analytics in v1 (needs player beacon batching — documented later-work per §8). Tested: video unit (`stats_test.go`: RecordView rolls up the day, owner gate ErrForbidden/ErrNotFound, 30-day zero-filled series shape, channel aggregation incl. private videos) + handler (`videos_stats_test.go`: owner 200 with real like/comment/view side effects, series zero-fill + today's bucket, non-owner/unknown 404, anon 401; channel followers+videos totals, non-owner/unknown 404, anon 401) + real-PG integration (`TestVideoViewDayRollupAndStats`: upsert accumulates, cutoff filtering, channel aggregation, engagement one-shots incl. zero-row COALESCE). `make ci` green.)
- [x] Add tests for public visibility/privacy rules. (`videos_test.go` `TestGetPublicVideoIsAnonymous`/`TestGetPrivateVideoOwnerOnly`; `hls_test.go` private-owner-only across master/variant/segment; `downloads.go` `TestVideoDownloadsVisibility` (private anon/non-owner 404, blocked non-mod 404); the unlisted-owner discovery-exclusion tests (`unlisted_test.go` + real-PG `TestUnlistedOwnerExcludedFromDiscovery`); scheduled/quarantined videos absent from public feeds. Draft/processing/failed excluded by the `state='published'` filter.)

# P8 — Library, Playlists, Comments, and Notifications

- [x] Implement watch history. (Watch history + resume progress as one slice on the `video` service (migration 0017 `watch_history`, sqlc `watch_history.sql`). Endpoints (all requireAuth): `PUT /api/v1/videos/:id/watch-progress` (upsert resume position, `publicVideoID` guard → 404 on non-public/unknown, 422 on negative), `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/watch-progress` (read it back, 0 when none), `GET /api/v1/me/history` (paginated discovery cards extended with `position_seconds` + `watched_at`, most-recently-watched first, filtered to public+published — reuses `feedItemView` via an embedded `videoView`), `DELETE /api/v1/me/history/:id` (remove one entry, idempotent, no public check so a user can always clean up), `DELETE /api/v1/me/history` (clear all, idempotent). `internal/video` `HistoryItem` + `RecordProgress`/`Progress`/`ListHistory`/`RemoveHistoryEntry`/`ClearHistory` reusing `newFeedItem`; `internal/httpapi/history.go`. openapi documents all 5 + `WatchProgress`/`WatchProgressRequest`/`HistoryItem`(allOf Video)/`WatchHistoryResponse` schemas (drift guard extended). Tested: 1 service round-trip (record→list newest-first→re-watch-rebumps→clamp-negative→absent-for-unwatched→remove→clear) + 5 handler (full round-trip incl. card channel link, delete+clear idempotency, non-public 404, negative 422, auth 401 on all 5 routes). DEFERRED: a history-tracking on/off user setting.)
- [x] Implement resume progress. (Covered by the watch-history slice above: `position_seconds` is upserted via `PUT /videos/:id/watch-progress`, read via `GET /videos/:id/watch-progress`, and carried on every `GET /me/history` card so the frontend can show resume bars + resume playback.)
- [x] Implement watch later playlist. (Covered by the saved-videos slice (migration 0016 `saved_videos`, `POST`/`DELETE /api/v1/videos/:id/save` + `GET /api/v1/me/saved`). Named playlists below are the general collection feature; watch-later is the dedicated single library.)
- [x] Implement playlist create/read/update/delete. (Named playlists (migration 0019 `playlists`+`playlist_items`, sqlc `playlists.sql`, `internal/playlist` service). - [x] Implement playlist visibility rules. (public/unlisted/private CHECK on the table; `GET /playlists/:id` gates private → owner-only (else 404); list/detail `video_count` + item list only count/return public+published videos so a leaked private video never surfaces in a public playlist. Tested: anon-sees-public, anon-404-on-private, owner-sees-own-private.)
- [x] Implement playlist item add/remove/reorder. (All three DONE. Add + remove: `POST /api/v1/playlists/:id/videos` (owner-only, body `{video_id}`; appends at `MAX(position)+1`, idempotent `ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING`; only **public, published** videos addable else 404), `DELETE /api/v1/playlists/:id/videos/:videoId` (owner-only, idempotent). **Reorder DONE**: `PUT /api/v1/playlists/:id/videos` (owner-only, body `{video_ids: [uuid…]}`) rewrites item positions to the given order in ONE atomic statement (`ReorderPlaylistItems` = `UPDATE … FROM unnest($video_ids::uuid[]) WITH ORDINALITY` → each video's position becomes its 1-based index; no `UNIQUE(position)` to collide with). The body must be exactly the playlist's current items — every item once, none missing/extra/duplicated (validated via the new unfiltered `ListPlaylistItemVideoIDs`) — else 422 (`playlist.ErrInvalidOrder`); a non-owner/unknown playlist is 404 (existence hidden). `playlist.Service.ReorderItems`; `handleReorderPlaylistItems`; openapi documents the op + `ReorderPlaylistItemsRequest` (drift guard green). Tested: service unit (reverse applies; non-owner→ErrForbidden, unknown→ErrNotFound, missing/extra/dup→ErrInvalidOrder, empty no-op) + handler (`TestPlaylistReorder`: reverse→204 + GET reflects order, missing-id 422, bad-uuid 422, non-owner 404, anon 401) + **real-PG integration** (`store.TestReorderPlaylistItemsPersists`: seeds the FK chain, reverses via the query, reads back the new order — proves the `WITH ORDINALITY` UPDATE against PostgreSQL). Now unblocks the frontend playlist drag-reorder.)

Tested: 1 service unit (`TestEditComment`) + 1 handler (`TestCommentEdit`: author 200+edited, blank 422, non-author 403, unknown 404, anon 401) + **real-PG integration** (`store.TestUpdateCommentPersists`: body updated + `updated_at` advances past `created_at`). Threading (parent_id) landed — see the next item; moderation hooks are P9. All four CRUD verbs are now implemented.)
- [x] Implement comment threading if in-scope. (Replies via a nullable `comments.parent_id` self-FK (migration 0026, reserved by 0014; `ON DELETE CASCADE` so a thread is removed with its root — soft-delete-that-keeps-the-thread is a later refinement). `POST /api/v1/videos/:id/comments` accepts an optional `parent_id`: the service (`comment.Create` now takes `parentID *uuid.UUID`) validates it references an existing comment on the **same** video, else `ErrParentNotFound` → 422 field error (a reply can't be smuggled onto another video's thread, and arbitrary comment-id existence isn't leaked); a malformed `parent_id` is a 422 too. Every comment view now carries `parent_id` (null for top-level), and `GET /videos/:id/comments` returns replies inline with the link so the client builds the tree. sqlc `CreateComment`/`GetComment`/`ListCommentsByVideo` carry `parent_id`; openapi documents the request field + `Comment.parent_id` (drift guard green). INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE from PeerTube: a flat list + `parent_id` (client builds the tree) rather than dedicated `comment-threads` / thread-detail endpoints — those can be a later refinement.)

Tested: 1 notification unit (`TestNotifyMessage`: message notif carries conversation id, self-message skipped) + messaging `OtherParticipant` unit + handler assertion in `TestMessagingFlow` (recipient sees a `message` notification linking the conversation from the sender; sender is NOT self-notified). - [x] Implement per-type notification preferences (product-decisions.md §7). (Migration 0043 `notification_prefs` (`(user_id, type)` PK, `enabled` bool, user FK `ON DELETE CASCADE`; ABSENCE of a row = default enabled, so no backfill and new types default on). sqlc `notification_prefs.sql`: `ListNotificationPrefs` / `UpsertNotificationPref` / `IsNotificationTypeEnabled` (COALESCE default-true). Create-side filtering: every `notification.Notify*` (follow/comment/message/report_resolved) consults `typeEnabled` before writing — a disabled type suppresses creation (returns nil: suppression is not an error); pref-lookup failure fails OPEN (an unreadable filter must not silently disable notifications). Endpoints (requireAuth): `GET /api/v1/me/notification-prefs` → `{prefs: {type: bool}}` over all known types; `PATCH` applies a partial map, rejects any unknown type with 422 WITHOUT writing, returns the full updated map. `notification.KnownTypes()` = comment/follow/message/report_resolved; `ErrUnknownType`. openapi: both ops + `NotificationPrefsResponse`/`UpdateNotificationPrefsRequest` (drift guard green). Tested: 4 service unit (defaults all-enabled; partial update + unknown-type rejection leaves state untouched; disabled types suppress creation while enabled ones deliver + re-enable restores; pref-lookup failure fails open) + 3 handler (GET defaults; PATCH flips+persists, unknown-type 422 no-write, empty 422, auth 401 on both; end-to-end disabled-pref suppresses a real follow notification and re-enable restores) + 1 real-PG integration (`TestNotificationPrefsPersist`: default-true, upsert insert+conflict paths, list, user-delete cascade).)

# P9 — Moderation, Admin, and Safety

- [~] Implement roles: user, moderator, admin, owner. (user/moderator/admin done end-to-end: the `users.role` CHECK enum, JWT-carried role, `requireRole(...)` middleware gating admin/moderator routes (reports queue, admin users), and admin role assignment via `PATCH /admin/users/:id` below. An explicit `owner` super-role is DEFERRED — admin is the top role today.)
- [x] Implement admin users list/search/filter. (`GET /api/v1/admin/users?q=&limit=&offset=` (requireRole admin) lists accounts newest-first, filterable by a username/email substring (`q`), paginated. `internal/admin` `Service.ListUsers` (sqlc `ListUsers`); `internal/httpapi/admin_users.go` `adminUserView` deliberately omits the password hash. openapi documents it + `AdminUser`/`AdminUserListResponse` (drift guard extended via `fullRouteOptions`).)
- [x] Implement user edit: role, quota, enabled/disabled, bypass quarantine, email verified. (role + enabled/disabled DONE: `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/:id` (requireRole admin, body `{role?, is_active?}`, partial) via `admin.Service.UpdateUser` (sqlc `AdminUpdateUser` COALESCE). Safety: an admin cannot demote or deactivate **their own** account (`ErrSelfChange` → 422), preventing last-admin lockout; deactivating a user revokes their sessions (best-effort) so the ban is immediate. Emits an `admin.user.update` audit event (new `observability.ActionAdminUserUpdate`). Tested: 3 service unit (search; promote+deactivate-revokes-sessions; self-guard + not-found) + 2 handler (list + non-admin 403 + promote + deactivate + self-demote/deactivate 422 + bad-role/empty 422 + unknown 404; auth 401). **Quota now DONE** — see the per-user storage quota item below. **Admin-set email_verified now DONE** (product-decisions.md §10): `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/:id` accepts `email_verified?: bool` (true marks the address confirmed without the token round-trip; false revokes) — `AdminUpdateUser` gains a `COALESCE(narg(email_verified), …)`, `admin.UpdateUserInput.EmailVerified`, request field + validation ("at least one of" extended), audit reason carries `email_verified=<bool>`; self-edit allowed (no lockout risk); openapi `UpdateUserRequest.email_verified` documented. Tested: 1 service unit (`TestUpdateUserEmailVerified`: flip on/off, unrelated fields untouched, no session revocation, self-edit allowed) + 1 handler (`TestAdminSetsEmailVerified`: flip on reflects in the target's `/auth/me`, flip off, non-admin 403, both edits audited with the flag value). **Bypass-quarantine now DONE** (§11 slice, migration 0048): `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/:id` accepts `bypass_quarantine?: bool`; consulted by the `UploadRequiresQuarantine` gate; audit reason carries `bypass_quarantine=<bool>`; openapi documented. Tested: `TestUpdateUserBypassQuarantine` (service) + `TestQuarantineBypassFlag` (handler: grant → direct publish, revoke → quarantined again, audited).)
- [x] Implement per-user storage quota: admin edit + upload/import enforcement + self-service read. (Migration 0041 adds nullable `users.storage_quota_bytes` (CHECK ≥ 0; NULL = inherit instance default, 0 = unlimited) — up+down verified against the live DB. Config `INSTANCE_DEFAULT_QUOTA_BYTES` (int64 bytes; 0/unset = unlimited; negative/non-integer → boot error; documented in `.env.example` + compose passthrough + README; 2 config tests). Usage = ONE sqlc aggregate `SumUserStorageUsage` (SUM of `video_files.size_bytes` — originals + renditions + thumbnails — JOIN videos JOIN channels WHERE `owner_id`; no denormalized counter). New `internal/quota` service: pure `Effective(override, instanceDefault)` + `Status`/`Remaining`/`CheckFits` (`ErrExceeded`). Enforcement BEFORE storing, only when the effective quota is finite: multipart upload checks the parsed part's true size (`fh.Size`; ownership 404 first); URL import pre-checks a declared Content-Length AND hard-caps the stream at the remaining headroom (generalized `maxBytesReader` with per-cap `limitErr`, mirroring the UPLOAD_MAX_SIZE 413 defence against lying/absent lengths) → both render the `422 {code: "quota_exceeded"}` envelope (`QuotaExceededError` in the central error handler). Admin: `PATCH /admin/users/:id` gains tri-state `storage_quota_bytes` (absent = unchanged, via `json.RawMessage`; null = reset to default; ≥0 = override) through `admin.UpdateUserInput` + sqlc `AdminUpdateUser` CASE; `AdminUser` view + `ListUsers` (inline usage subquery → `ListUsersRow`) expose `storage_quota_bytes` + `storage_used_bytes`; audit reason carries `storage_quota_bytes=<n>|default`. Self-service: `GET /api/v1/me/quota` → `{used_bytes, quota_bytes}` (null = unlimited); `WithQuotaService` wired in cmd/api + `fullRouteOptions`. openapi: `/me/quota` op + `QuotaStatus` schema + `AdminUser`/`UpdateUserRequest` fields + upload/import 422 responses (drift guard + `make openapi-lint` green). Tested: 3 quota unit (Effective table; Status/Remaining incl. over-quota clamp; CheckFits at/over/unlimited) + 4 admin unit (set/keep-on-unrelated-edit/reset/self-allowed; list usage; StorageUsed) + 6 handler (me/quota 401 + unlimited-null + tracks usage; upload over → 422 quota_exceeded + nothing stored, cumulative block, exactly-at-cap 201; admin set → user 422 → null reset → 201, list `storage_used_bytes`, −1/string → 422, non-admin 403; import 422 on both a sized Content-Length and a chunked no-length stream; unlimited chunked import 201) + real-PG integration `store.TestSumUserStorageUsageAggregates` (per-kind sum, cross-user isolation, empty user 0, ListUsers row aggregate, AdminUpdateUser set/keep/reset). Gate: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration -race` green for store/httpapi/quota/admin.)
- [x] Implement registration approval queue. (`REGISTRATION_REQUIRE_APPROVAL` config (default false); migration 0028 `registration_requests` (username/email/password_hash/note/status/moderator_note/reviewed_by/reviewed_at; partial unique indexes allow one PENDING per email/username, case-insensitive). When approval is on, `POST /api/v1/auth/register` files a pending request and returns **202 `{status:"pending"}`** with no token/account (audit `auth.registration.request`); when off, the direct-signup flow is unchanged. Admin-only endpoints (`requireRole("admin")`): `GET /api/v1/admin/registration-requests?status=pending` (paginated queue, reviewer resolved, password hash never exposed), `POST /api/v1/admin/registration-requests/:id/approve` (**atomically** creates the account from the stored hash + marks approved via a single data-modifying-CTE query — all-or-nothing; unknown/resolved → 404, email/username since-taken → 409; audit `auth.registration.approve`), `POST /api/v1/admin/registration-requests/:id/reject` (optional `{note}`; unknown/resolved → 404; audit `auth.registration.reject`). `internal/auth/registration.go` (`RequestRegistration`/`ListRegistrationRequests`/`ApproveRegistration`/`RejectRegistration` + `ErrRegistrationRequestNotFound`); sqlc `registration_requests.sql`; `internal/httpapi/admin_registration.go`; `GET /instance` now also returns `registration_requires_approval`. openapi documents register-202 + the 3 admin routes + `RegistrationPending`/`RegistrationRequest`/`RegistrationRequestListResponse`/`RejectRegistrationRequest` schemas + the instance field (drift guard green). Tested: 6 auth service (request→pending→dedup, existing-user conflict, approve→creates+login+re-approve-404, unknown-approve, reject→no-login→re-reject-404) + 2 handler (full approve flow: register-202-no-token→admin-queue→approve→login-works→non-admin 403→anon 401; reject flow: register-202→reject→no-login→re-reject-404). **This unblocks the `vidra-user` P10 registration-requests admin UI** (list + accept/reject). NB: the first admin must be created before enabling approval (bootstrap), otherwise no one can approve.)
- [x] Implement abuse reports for videos/comments/accounts. (Video + comment + **account** reports DONE: migration 0020 `reports` (reporter FK, `target_type` CHECK video/comment, nullable `video_id`/`comment_id` cascade FKs, `reason`, `status` CHECK open/accepted/rejected, `moderator_note`, `resolved_by`/`resolved_at`; `(status, created_at DESC)` queue index + partial unique `(reporter_id, video_id)` / `(reporter_id, comment_id)`); migration 0027 adds the **account** target (widened `target_type` CHECK + nullable `reported_user_id` FK → users `ON DELETE CASCADE` + partial unique `(reporter_id, reported_user_id)`). Endpoints: `POST /api/v1/videos/:id/report` (requireAuth, `publicVideoID` guard, idempotent), `POST /api/v1/comments/:id/report` (requireAuth, FK→404 on unknown comment), `POST /api/v1/users/:id/report` (requireAuth, self-report → 422, unknown account FK → 404, idempotent). `internal/moderation` `ReportVideo`/`ReportComment`/`ReportAccount` (FK 23503 → `ErrInvalidTarget`; self → `ErrCannotReportSelf`); `internal/httpapi/reports.go`. The admin queue (`ListReports` + `reportView`) now resolves + returns the reported account's id/username for account reports. openapi documents `POST /users/{id}/report` + the `account` target_type + `reported_user_id`/`reported_username` on `Report` (drift guard green). Tested: 1 service (`TestReportAccount`: report→list→dedup, self-report, FK) + 1 handler (`TestReportAccountAndModerate`: report→queue shows reported username, self 422, unknown 404, anon 401). **This unblocks the `vidra-user` P9 DEFERRED account/channel report flow** (backend `target_type` now includes account). DEFERRED: a dedicated channel-report target (channel reports can map to the owning account for now).)
- [x] Implement report accept/reject/delete/internal note. (Accept/reject + internal note DONE via the admin queue: `GET /api/v1/admin/reports?status=open` (requireRole admin/moderator, paginated, resolves reporter username + video title / comment body) + `POST /api/v1/admin/reports/:id/resolve` (requireRole, body `{status: accepted|rejected, note}`, sets `resolved_by`/`resolved_at`, emits a `moderation.report.resolve` audit event; unknown id → 404). `internal/moderation` `List`/`Resolve`. openapi documents all 4 ops + `Report`/`ReportReporter`/`ReportListResponse`/`CreateReportRequest`/`ResolveReportRequest` (drift guard extended). Tested: 3 service unit (report+list+dedup, comment-FK→invalid-target, resolve+not-found) + 3 handler (report→queue→resolve→leaves-open-queue, non-admin 403, report-comment + unknown-comment 404, validation 422 + auth 401 on all 4). Notifications to reporter DONE — see the next item. **Report hard-delete now DONE** (product-decisions.md §9): `DELETE /api/v1/admin/reports/:id` behind `requireRole(admin)` ONLY — moderators resolve, admins purge (moderator → 403). Idempotent-204, matching the established admin-delete convention (watched-word delete, video unblock): an unknown/already-deleted id still 204s; a malformed id is 404. sqlc `DeleteReport :execrows`; `moderation.Delete`; notifications referencing the report cascade away (0042 FK); audited `moderation.report.delete` (new `observability.ActionReportDelete`, reason carries the report id — no content). openapi documents the op. Tested: 1 service unit (`TestDeleteReport`: gone from queue, re-delete + unknown no-op) + 1 handler (`TestDeleteReportAdminOnly`: moderator 403 / user 403 / anon 401, admin 204 + leaves queue, idempotent re-delete + unknown 204, malformed 404, audit event carries actor + report id) + real-PG integration (the 0042 cascade test now drives `q.DeleteReport`, asserting 1-row then 0-row idempotency).)
- [x] Implement notifications to reporter where applicable. (Resolving a report now tells the reporter, best-effort: `handleResolveReport` calls the new `notification.NotifyReportResolved(reporter, moderator, reportID)` after a successful resolve — a notifier failure never fails the resolve, and a moderator resolving their OWN report gets no self-notification. Migration 0042 adds a nullable `report_id` FK (→ reports `ON DELETE CASCADE`) to `notifications` (mirrors 0032's `conversation_id`); `ResolveReport` becomes `:one RETURNING reporter_id` so the handler knows whom to notify (`moderation.Resolve` now returns the reporter; `pgx.ErrNoRows` → `ErrNotFound`). New notification type `report_resolved` — the row stores ONLY `user_id`+`type`+`report_id` (the moderator's identity is deliberately NOT stored, so it can never leak to the reporter); `ListNotifications` LEFT JOINs reports to expose `report_status` (accepted/rejected) + `report_target_type` (video/comment/account) on the list view. openapi: `Notification.type` enum + `report_id`/`report_status`/`report_target_type` (drift guard green). Tested: 1 service unit (`TestNotifyReportResolved`: notify→list carries type+report id, self no-op, no actor_id stored) + 3 handler (`TestResolveReportNotifiesReporter`: accepted video report + rejected account report both notify with status/target context, no actor exposed; `TestResolveOwnReportDoesNotSelfNotify`; `TestResolveReportSurvivesNotifyFailure`: failing notifier still resolves → 204) + real-PG integration (`store.TestReportResolutionNotificationPersists`: ResolveReport returns reporter, notification row + joined report context read back, report delete cascades the notification).)
- [x] Implement video block manual flow. Idempotent (re-block updates reason + acting moderator); unknown video → 404; emits a `moderation.video.block` audit event (reason never logged). `internal/moderation` `BlockVideo`/`IsBlocked` (sqlc `BlockVideo` upsert / `IsVideoBlocked`; FK 23503 → `ErrVideoNotFound`); `internal/httpapi/videos.go` (`handleBlockVideo`). openapi documents both block ops (drift guard green). - [x] Implement auto-block/quarantine setting. (product-decisions.md §11: `QUARANTINE_NEW_UPLOADS` (bool env, default false; validated in config, logged at boot, documented in `.env.example`) wires `video.WithQuarantineNewUploads` in cmd/api. Migration 0048 adds `users.bypass_quarantine BOOL NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE`, widens the videos state CHECK to include `'quarantined'`, and adds a partial queue index. With the gate on, `video.Service.Process` consults the new sqlc `UploadRequiresQuarantine` (owner role='user' AND NOT bypass_quarantine; fail-closed on gate error) BEFORE the scheduled hold and parks the finished upload in `state='quarantined'` — NO publish/transcode hooks fire at quarantine time. Public surfaces already filter `state='published'` so quarantined videos never appear there; the detail/original/thumbnail/HLS/downloads endpoints additionally hide it from everyone but the owner (studio badge via `state`) and moderators (`quarantineHidesVideo` / `videoHiddenFromViewer`). `PATCH /api/v1/admin/users/:id` gains `bypass_quarantine?: bool` (`AdminUpdateUser` COALESCE, audit reason carries the flag, `AdminUser.bypass_quarantine` in list/patch views, openapi documented). Tested: 4 video-service unit (quarantine parks + approve fires hooks exactly once; privileged publishes directly; quarantine trumps schedule; reject fails w/o hooks + state guards) + 1 admin-service unit (`TestUpdateUserBypassQuarantine`) + 3 handler (`TestQuarantineFlow`, `TestQuarantineRejectNotifiesOwner`, `TestQuarantineBypassFlag`) + real-PG integration (`TestQuarantinePipelinePersists`: CHECK accepts 'quarantined', gate matrix incl. COALESCE no-touch, queue join, feed exclusion). **UPDATE (core-instance-config)**: the gate is now RUNTIME-mutable via the DB settings overlay — `video.WithQuarantineNewUploads(bool)` was superseded by `video.WithQuarantineGate(func() bool)`, wired in cmd/api to `settingssvc.Bool(quarantine_new_uploads)` (default still `QUARANTINE_NEW_UPLOADS`); an admin can flip it via PATCH /admin/instance-settings without a restart. See the "admin instance configuration + feature toggles" item below.)
