- [x] Implement muted accounts. (Model + management DONE: a signed-in user mutes/unmutes another account and lists their mutes. migration 0022 `muted_accounts`; sqlc `muted_accounts.sql` (`MuteAccount` idempotent upsert / `UnmuteAccount` / `ListMutedAccounts` JOIN users for identity); `internal/mute` service (`Mute`/`Unmute`/`List`; self-mute → `ErrCannotMuteSelf`, unknown target FK 23503 → `ErrUserNotFound`). Endpoints (all requireAuth): `POST /api/v1/me/mutes/accounts/:id` (mute; self → 422, unknown → 404, idempotent 204), `DELETE /api/v1/me/mutes/accounts/:id` (unmute; idempotent 204), `GET /api/v1/me/mutes/accounts` (paginated list, newest mute first, with the muted account's username/display_name). `internal/httpapi/mutes.go`; wired via `WithMuteService` in `cmd/api`. To make a mute target reachable from the UI, the comment view now also exposes `author_id` (so the frontend can "mute this commenter"); openapi documents all three endpoints + `MutedAccount`/`MutedAccountListResponse` + the `Comment.author_id` field (drift guard green). Tested: 3 service unit (mute→list→unmute idempotent; self-mute; unknown-target) + 2 handler (full mute/list/unmute round trip + idempotency; self 422, unknown 404, anon 401 on all three). **Comment filtering effect now DONE**: `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/comments` is now `optionalAuth` and hides comments authored by accounts the viewer has muted — `ListCommentsByVideo` gained a nullable `viewer_id` filter (`NOT EXISTS muted_accounts WHERE muter_id = viewer AND muted_id = c.user_id`; NULL viewer = anon = no filtering, so the public behaviour is unchanged); `comment.Service.ListByVideo` takes `(viewerID, viewerAuthed)` and the handler passes the optional principal. openapi marks the operation optional-auth and notes the per-viewer mute filter. Tested: `TestCommentsHideMutedAuthors` (ada mutes bob → ada no longer sees bob's comment but an anon viewer still does → unmute restores it). **Feed/video filtering effect now DONE too**: the muter no longer sees a muted account's videos in the main feed (`GET /videos`), search (`GET /videos/search`), or subscriptions (`GET /me/subscriptions/videos`). `ListPublicVideosSorted`/`SearchPublicVideos` gained a nullable `viewer_id` filter (`NOT EXISTS muted_accounts WHERE muter_id = viewer AND muted_id = c.owner_id`; NULL/anon = no filtering); `ListSubscriptionVideos` reuses its `follower_id` as the muter; feed + search routes are now `optionalAuth` and `video.ListPublic`/`SearchPublic` take `(viewerID, viewerAuthed)`. Direct channel visits (`GET /channels/:handle/videos`) are intentionally NOT filtered — muting hides from discovery, not from a deliberate visit (PeerTube parity). openapi marks feed + search optional-auth + notes the per-viewer filter. Tested: `TestFeedHidesMutedAccounts` (charlie mutes bob → bob's video gone from charlie's feed + search but an anon viewer still sees it → unmute restores it). The account-mute feature is now complete backend-side; frontend mute UI is VERIFIED (see `vidra-user`). Muted **instances** (federation) is separate.)

(A signed-in user blocks/unblocks another account and lists who they've blocked; a block **symmetrically** cuts off direct messaging (either direction → 403). migration 0033 `user_blocks` (PK (blocker_id, blocked_id), no-self CHECK, reverse index); sqlc `user_blocks.sql` (`BlockUser` idempotent upsert / `UnblockUser` / `ListBlockedUsers` JOIN users / `IsBlockedBetween` symmetric EXISTS); `internal/block` service (`Block`/`Unblock`/`List`/`IsBlockedBetween`; self → `ErrCannotBlockSelf`, unknown target FK 23503 → `ErrUserNotFound`). Endpoints (all requireAuth): `POST /api/v1/me/blocks/:id` (self → 422, unknown → 404, idempotent 204), `DELETE /api/v1/me/blocks/:id` (idempotent 204), `GET /api/v1/me/blocks` (paginated, newest first, with identity). `internal/httpapi/blocks.go`; wired via `WithBlockService` in `cmd/api`. Messaging enforcement via `messaging.Blocker` + `WithBlocker` (see P11.1): `StartConversation`/`SendMessage` → `ErrBlocked` → 403; participant check precedes the block check so a non-participant stays 404 (no existence leak). openapi documents all three endpoints + the messaging 403s + `BlockedUser`/`BlockedUserListResponse` (drift guard green). Tested: 4 block service unit (block→list→unblock idempotent; self; unknown FK; `IsBlockedBetween` symmetric) + 2 messaging service unit (start blocked; send blocked but non-participant still ErrNotParticipant) + 2 handler (`TestBlockUserFlow` CRUD + self 422 + unknown 404 + anon 401; `TestMessagingBlockedByUserBlock` start→block→both-directions-403→unblock→send-works). **Unblocks a `vidra-user` block/unblock UI + block-list page.** **Block-hides-content now DONE** (product-decisions.md §13): the mute-based NOT EXISTS filters on `ListCommentsByVideo`, `ListPublicVideosSorted` (feed), `SearchPublicVideos`, and `ListSubscriptionVideos` additionally exclude authors/owners the viewer has BLOCKED (`user_blocks WHERE blocker_id = viewer AND blocked_id = author/owner` — per-viewer, one direction, anonymous unaffected; direct channel visits stay intentionally unfiltered like mutes). No schema/param changes — sqlc regenerated in place. openapi block/unblock descriptions document the content effect. Tested mirroring the mute filter tests: `TestFeedHidesBlockedAccounts` (feed + search + subscriptions hide → anon unaffected → unblock restores) + `TestCommentsHideBlockedAuthors` (per-viewer comment filter round-trip) + real-PG integration `TestBlockHidesContentFilters` (all four queries against real `user_blocks` rows via BlockUser/UnblockUser, anon NULL-viewer pass-through). `make ci` + integration suite green 2026-07-03.)

(federation-remote-content.md §8, core-fed-inbound slice. **Per-user instance mutes**: migration 0051 `muted_instances`; sqlc `instance_moderation.sql` (`MuteInstance` idempotent upsert / `UnmuteInstance` / `ListMutedInstances` newest-first); new `internal/instancemod` service (`NormalizeDomain` lowercases + validates a bare host[:port] — scheme/path/userinfo/space/over-253 → `ErrInvalidDomain` → 422). Endpoints (requireAuth): `POST/DELETE /api/v1/me/mutes/instances/:domain` (idempotent 204), `GET /api/v1/me/mutes/instances` (paginated). The filtering effect on feeds/search is now WIRED (core-fed-follow slice): the subscriptions/search/scope=all-feed UNION queries exclude the viewer's muted instances and admin-blocked instances. **Admin instance blocklist**: `blocked_instances` table; endpoints (requireRole admin/moderator, audited `moderation.instance.block/unblock`): `GET/POST /api/v1/admin/instances/blocked` (POST body `{domain, reason≤2000}`, idempotent upsert refreshing reason+admin) + `DELETE /api/v1/admin/instances/blocked/:domain`. Enforcement wired: (1) the federation inbox drops activities whose (signature-verified) signer's host is blocked — accepted 202, never dispatched, NOT marked processed so an unblock lets a redelivery in; (2) `GetRemoteVideoByID` excludes blocked domains (NOT EXISTS) so existing remote content is hidden; (3) `DrainDeliveries` cancels (dead-letters with `cancelled: destination instance is blocked`) pending deliveries to blocked hosts instead of sending. openapi documents all 6 ops + `MutedInstance(ListResponse)`/`BlockedInstance(ListResponse)`/`BlockInstanceRequest` (drift guard green). Tested: instancemod unit (NormalizeDomain matrix; mute/unmute/list normalized idempotent; block reason bounding + blocked_by) + federation unit (blocked-domain inbox drop not-marked-processed; drain cancellation) + handler (mute flow incl. 401/422 + case-normalization; admin 401/403; block→list→unblock flow incl. missing/invalid domain 422) + real-PG integration (`TestInstanceModerationPersists` both tables round-trip; `TestRemoteVideoIngestionPersists` block hides the read model + drops inbound + unblock restores). Gate: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration -race` green (federation/instancemod/remotevideo/httpapi) 2026-07-03.)

When a comment is posted, `handleCreateComment` best-effort calls `watchword.FlagComment(commentID, body)` which matches the body against the list (`MatchWatchedWords`: case-insensitive substring via `strpos(lower(text), lower(word))`) and records a row per matched term (`RecordWatchedWordMatch`, idempotent) — never blocks the post. Moderators review via `GET /api/v1/admin/watched-word-matches` (requireRole admin/moderator, paginated, newest match first, resolving the matched term + comment body/author/video). sqlc `MatchWatchedWords`/`RecordWatchedWordMatch`/`ListWatchedWordMatches`; `watchword.FlagComment`/`ListMatches`; `internal/httpapi/admin_watched_words.go`. openapi documents the route + `WatchedWordMatch`/`WatchedWordMatchListResponse` (drift guard green). Tested: 1 service (`TestFlagCommentAndListMatches`: 2-term match, clean=0, idempotent) + 1 handler (`TestWatchedWordMatchesFlow`: add term → comment with term flagged → appears in queue → clean comment adds none → non-mod 403 → anon 401). **Video tagging now DONE** (product-decisions.md §12): migration 0049 makes `watched_word_matches.comment_id` nullable, adds nullable `video_id` FK → videos `ON DELETE CASCADE`, a CHECK that exactly one of comment_id/video_id is set, and a partial unique index `(watched_word_id, video_id) WHERE video_id IS NOT NULL`. `watchword.FlagVideo(videoID, title+"\n"+description)` (sqlc `RecordWatchedWordVideoMatch`, idempotent) is called best-effort from `handleCreateVideo` AND `handleUpdateVideo` (an edit can newly introduce a term). `ListWatchedWordMatches` reworked to serve BOTH kinds: LEFT JOINs resolve per-type context (comment body/author + its video, or the flagged video's title + owner as author); `watchword.Match` gains `Type` (comment|video) + `VideoTitle`; the queue view carries a `type` badge, `video_title` (link target), and omits comment fields on video rows; openapi `WatchedWordMatch` reshaped (required: type/video_id/video_title). Auto-hold intentionally NOT added for videos — §11 quarantine is the hold mechanism (documented in the spec + route description). Tested: 1 service unit (`TestFlagVideoAndListMatches`: 2-term match, clean=0, idempotent, typed video rows w/o comment fields, mixed queue ordering) + 1 handler (`TestWatchedWordVideoMatchesFlow`: clean create flags nothing, description match on create, edit newly flags, idempotent re-edit, title+owner context) + real-PG integration (`TestWatchedWordVideoMatchesPersist`: partial-unique idempotency, both-targets/no-target CHECK violations, typed queue rows, video-delete cascade). `make ci` + integration suite green 2026-07-03.)

- [x] Implement admin audit log. (Durable, append-only audit trail: migration 0029 `audit_log` (action, result, nullable `actor_id` stored **without an FK** so the trail survives account deletion, safe `reason`, `request_id`, `occurred_at`; indexes on `occurred_at DESC` and `(action, occurred_at)`). `internal/audit` service (`Record`/`List`); the httpapi `s.audit` choke point now persists every security-audit event best-effort (in addition to the slog line) when the audit-log service is wired — so auth/moderation/admin/registration events all land. `GET /api/v1/admin/audit-log?action=&limit=&offset=` (requireRole admin) lists newest-first, filterable by action, resolving `actor_username` best-effort via LEFT JOIN (null when the account was deleted); rows never carry secrets/PII. sqlc `audit_log.sql`; `internal/httpapi/admin_audit.go`; wired via `WithAuditLog` in `cmd/api`. openapi documents the route + `AuditLogEntry`/`AuditLogListResponse` (drift guard extended via `fullRouteOptions`). - [x] Implement admin instance configuration + feature toggles (DB-backed settings overlay). (core-instance-config slice, fix_plan P10, 2026-07-03. A DB-backed dynamic instance-settings layer that OVERLAYS the static config for a defined MUTABLE subset; boot-time-only secrets/infrastructure (DB DSN, KEKs, JWT secret, storage backend) intentionally STAY config-only — documented in the migration + the `internal/instancesettings` package doc. **Migration 0065** `instance_settings` (`key TEXT PK`, `value TEXT NOT NULL` — bools stored canonically `'true'`/`'false'`, a row's presence = overridden so a string CAN be overridden to `''`, `updated_by UUID` nullable FK → users **ON DELETE SET NULL** so the setting survives its admin's deletion (audit-integrity parity with `blocked_instances.blocked_by`), `updated_at`); up+down reversible (verified down 1 → up against the live DB). sqlc `instance_settings.sql` (`ListInstanceSettings`/`UpsertInstanceSetting`/`DeleteInstanceSetting`); regenerated `sqlcgen` committed (sqlc-verify green). **`internal/instancesettings` service**: a canonical registry of 12 mutable keys — `instance_name`/`instance_description`/`terms_url`/`privacy_url`/`contact_email` (string) + `registration_enabled`/`registration_require_approval`/`quarantine_new_uploads`/`uploads_enabled`/`imports_enabled`/`live_enabled`/`comments_enabled` (bool) — each with a config-default resolver (`Defaults`, built from `cfg` in cmd/api) + a per-key validator (non-empty ≤100 name, ≤5000 desc, optional http(s) URL ≤2000, optional email ≤254, bool). Loads all rows at boot into a mutex-guarded cache; typed `String(key)`/`Bool(key)` accessors return the DB override or the config default; `Snapshot()` yields effective value+default+overridden; `Apply()` validates the whole batch first (all-or-nothing), upserts/deletes, then **invalidates + reloads** the cache. The 4 feature toggles gained boot-time config defaults (`FEATURE_UPLOADS_ENABLED`/`_IMPORTS_ENABLED`/`_LIVE_ENABLED`/`_COMMENTS_ENABLED`, all default true; config struct + `.env.example`). **Admin API**: `GET /api/v1/admin/instance-settings` (requireRole admin → effective values + which are overridden), `PATCH /api/v1/admin/instance-settings` (requireRole admin; flat key→value object, bool for toggle keys / string for text keys / **null clears the override** back to config default; unknown key / type mismatch / content-invalid → 422 field errors, nothing written; audited `admin.instance.update` — new `observability.ActionAdminInstanceUpdate`, reason carries the changed KEY NAMES only, never values (contact_email is operator PII)). **Enforcement**: the registration gate (`handleRegister`) + the upload session/direct-file (`handleCreateUploadSession`/`handleUploadVideoFile`), URL-import (`handleImportVideoFile`), live-create (`handleCreateLiveStream`), and comment-create (`handleCreateComment`) gates now consult the EFFECTIVE toggle via `Server` helpers (`registrationEnabled()`/`uploadsEnabled()`/… — DB overlay when the settings service is wired, else config fallback so existing unit-test servers are unchanged); a disabled feature → **403 `feature_disabled`** (new `FeatureDisabledError` → central error handler). The quarantine gate switched from a boot-fixed bool to a runtime predicate: `video.WithQuarantineGate(func() bool)` (supersedes `WithQuarantineNewUploads`), wired in cmd/api to `settingssvc.Bool(quarantine_new_uploads)` so an admin can toggle it live (default still `cfg.QuarantineNewUploads`). `GET /api/v1/instance` now reflects the EFFECTIVE name/description/terms/privacy/contact + registration gates (not just static config). openapi documents both admin ops + `InstanceSetting`/`InstanceSettingsResponse`/`UpdateInstanceSettingsRequest` (drift guard green via `fullRouteOptions`; redocly error count unchanged at the 2 pre-existing `nullable` baseline). No new secret fields → the sensitive-key denylist is unchanged; no secrets logged. Tested: 6 `instancesettings` unit (overlay default/override/reset resolution, snapshot default+overridden, reload picks up external rows + ignores legacy keys, per-key validation matrix incl. all-or-nothing batch + valid empty-URL/email clears, empty-apply no-op) + 6 httpapi (`TestInstanceSettingsAdminFlow`: defaults/12 keys, override→reload reflected in GET /instance, audit reason = key names only + no value leak, null-reset, 5-case validation, anon 401/non-admin 403 both verbs; `TestUploadsToggleGate` both entrypoints on→works/off→403 + re-enable; `TestImportsToggleGate`; `TestLiveToggleGate`; `TestCommentsToggleGate` incl. reads stay open; `TestRegistrationOverlayGate` overlay-disable→register 403 + GET /instance reflects + re-enable) + real-PG integration (`store.TestInstanceSettingsPersistAndReload`: sqlc round trip, fresh-service reload sees persisted override, `updated_by` ON DELETE SET NULL keeps the row after admin deletion, delete-override→default). Gate: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration -race` green (store/httpapi/video/instancesettings) 2026-07-03. **Unblocks the `vidra-user` P10 admin instance-config + feature-toggles page.**)
- [x] Implement rate-limit management endpoints or config-only decision. (core-sweep slice, 2026-07-03. **DECISION: config-only, INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE** — product-decisions §3. Rate limits stay a deploy-time capacity decision (`RATE_LIMIT_*` / `AUTH_RATE_LIMIT_*` env); NO runtime mutation endpoint (a compromised admin could otherwise silently disable protection). Instead `GET /api/v1/admin/system` now surfaces the EFFECTIVE, non-secret config read-only via a new `rate_limits` object `{enabled, requests, auth_requests, window_seconds}` populated from `s.cfg` (`internal/httpapi/admin_system.go` `systemRateLimits`). openapi `SystemStatus` gains `rate_limits` (required; drift guard green). Tested: `TestSystemStatus` asserts the surfaced values mirror the effective config (120/10/60s); testConfig now carries the production-default limits so the assertion is meaningful. No secret involved — sensitive-key denylist unchanged.)

(real-PG `-tags=integration`: `TestBlockHidesContentFilters` (feed/search/subscriptions/comment filters against real `user_blocks`), `TestWatchedWordVideoMatchesPersist` (partial-unique + CHECK + typed queue + cascade), `TestInstanceModerationPersists` + `TestRemoteVideoIngestionPersists` (mute/block hides read model + drops inbound), `TestQuarantinePipelinePersists`, the report-resolution + 0042-cascade tests. All green in backend-integration on main 2026-07-03.)
- [x] Add Postman admin collection tests. (core-sweep, 2026-07-03. Curated Postman v2.1 collection + environment under `docs/postman/`, generated from `api/openapi.yaml` by the zero-dep `docs/postman/generate.mjs` (regen: `make postman`). Covers System probes (healthz/readyz/version/nodeinfo/instance) + Auth happy/error (register 201→token capture, register 422, login 200, login 401, `/auth/me` 200) + `admin/system` (200-or-403, asserts `rate_limits` on 200), with real status/shape assertions + token chaining. The generator fails on any path drift vs the contract. NOT a required gate (docs/manual QA). Verified with `newman run` against the live compose backend: 11 requests / 18 assertions / 0 failures. Full every-endpoint conversion documented via `npx openapi-to-postmanv2`.)

# P10 — Federation

> **DESIGN LANDED** — `.ralph/specs/federation.md` grounds this whole phase: it decides the
> actor model (AP key columns on `users`/`channels`, remote actors in a separate table), the
> id/URL scheme (root, not `/api/v1`), content negotiation, the OpenAPI drift-guard handling
> (federation routes excluded like the dev endpoint), and — importantly — the **private-key
> at-rest** approach (envelope encryption via a `FEDERATION_KEY_KEK`, resolving
> security.md:31). It orders the 21 items below into 7 buildable slices. Build in that order;
> the first (keypair-free) slice is config `PUBLIC_BASE_URL`/`FEDERATION_ENABLED` + NodeInfo.
>
> **Slice 1 SHIPPED** (keypair-free discovery): config `FEDERATION_ENABLED` (default false,
> master gate) + `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` (validated http(s) origin, no path, https in prod; trailing
> slash trimmed) in `internal/config`, `.env.example`, `docker-compose.yml`. `internal/federation`
> service (NodeInfo usage counts) over new `CountPublicVideos`/`CountComments` sqlc queries
> (`CountUsers` reused). Root routes `GET /.well-known/nodeinfo` + `GET /nodeinfo/2.1`
> (`internal/httpapi/federation.go`) mounted ONLY when `FEDERATION_ENABLED && fedsvc != nil` —
> so they 404 by default and stay OUT of the REST OpenAPI drift guard (exclusion-by-omission,
> like the dev endpoint; `TestOpenAPIContract` green). NodeInfo 2.1 advertises software=vidra,
> protocols=[activitypub], openRegistrations=RegistrationEnabled, usage={users, localPosts=public
> published videos, localComments}. Tested: `internal/federation` (usage mapping + error
> propagation) + `internal/httpapi` (discovery link, 2.1 doc shape + profile content type,
> absent-when-disabled 404).
>
> **Slice 2a SHIPPED** (actor-key crypto + schema foundation): migration 0035 adds dedicated
> 1:1 side tables `account_actor_keys` + `channel_actor_keys` (public_key_pem, private_key_pem,
> created_at; `ON DELETE CASCADE`) — **NOT** columns on users/channels, because adding columns
> there made existing queries' RETURNING lists diverge from the shared sqlc model and churned
> every service Repository (spec §2 decision revised accordingly). `internal/secretbox`:
> AES-256-GCM envelope encryption (`Seal`→`enc:<base64 nonce||ct>`, `Open`, `IsSealed`,
> `NewCipherFromBase64`) for private keys at rest; fully unit-tested (round-trip, fresh-nonce,
> wrong-KEK/tamper reject, non-prefixed reject, bad length/base64). Config `FEDERATION_KEY_KEK`
> (base64 32-byte; required in production when federation on, dev stores raw) with validation
> tests; `.env.example` + compose passthrough. Banned-log-key guard extended (`private_key_pem`,
> `kek`).
>
> **Slice 2b SHIPPED** (actor identity HTTP surface): sqlc queries (`GetUserActorByUsername`,
> `GetAccountActorKey`/`GetChannelActorKey`, `InsertAccountActorKeyIfAbsent`/`Insert…Channel…`;
> reuses `GetChannelByHandle`). `internal/federation` gained lazy RSA-2048 keypair minting
> (`ensureAccountKey`/`ensureChannelKey`: get → mint → insert-if-absent → re-read, race-safe;
> private key sealed via `internal/secretbox` when a KEK is set, raw in dev), actor-document
> builders (`AccountActor` Person, `ChannelActor` Group; `@context` incl. security vocab, derived
> inbox/outbox/followers/following + `publicKey`), and `WebFinger` (acct:name@domain → self link;
> own-domain-only). httpapi: content-negotiated `GET /accounts/:handle` + `/video-channels/:handle`
> (406 without an AP Accept, 404 unknown) + `GET /.well-known/webfinger` (400 bad/missing resource,
> 404 foreign domain) — all gated on `FEDERATION_ENABLED`, excluded from the REST drift guard.
> cmd/api builds the secretbox cipher from `FEDERATION_KEY_KEK` (warns when unset with federation on).
> Tested: federation unit (mint-once, seal-with-cipher, Person/Group build, WebFinger resolve/reject),
> httpapi handler (Person/Group serve, 406/404, WebFinger), and an **integration test proving minting
> persists + is idempotent against real Postgres** (run in backend-integration).
>
> **Slice 3a SHIPPED** (HTTP Signatures primitive): `internal/httpsig` — standalone cavage-draft
> "Signing HTTP Messages" (RSA-SHA256) used across the fediverse. `Signer.Sign` sets Date + Digest
> (`SHA-256=<base64>` over the body) + a `Signature` header covering `(request-target) host date digest`;
> `Verifier.Verify` parses the header, enforces the minimum covered set (digest required when a body is
> present), checks Date within skew (default 5m, injectable clock), verifies the Digest against the body,
> and RSA-verifies against a caller-supplied `ResolveKey(keyID)` callback — so the package needs no
> network or DB. Fully unit-tested: sign→verify round-trip, tampered body/signature, wrong key, stale
> Date, missing digest-coverage, unsupported algorithm, missing/garbled Signature header, base64 parse.
>
> **Slice 3b SHIPPED** (remote-actor fetch + cache): migration 0036 `remote_actors` (actor_url PK,
> type/preferred_username/domain/inbox/shared_inbox/public_key_pem/followers + fetched/updated_at) +
> sqlc `GetRemoteActor`/`UpsertRemoteActor`. `internal/federation` `resolveRemoteActor` serves from the
> cache on hit, else fetches the actor doc through the SSRF guard (`urlsafety.Guard{AllowPrivate}`,
> Accept: activity+json, 1 MiB body cap), parses id/type/preferredUsername/inbox/endpoints.sharedInbox/
> publicKey/followers, and upserts it. `Service.ResolveKey(keyID)` strips the `#fragment`, resolves the
> actor, and parses `publicKey.publicKeyPem` → `*rsa.PublicKey` — the resolver `internal/httpsig.Verifier`
> will use for inbound verification. `WithAllowPrivateFetch` (wired from HTTP_IMPORT_ALLOW_PRIVATE_URLS)
> + `WithFetchClient` (tests). Tested: unit (fetch→parse→cache reuse via httptest [origin hit once],
> no-key reject, SSRF-guarded-by-default reject, PEM parse) + integration (upsert/get + ON CONFLICT
> refresh against real Postgres).
>
> **Slice 4a SHIPPED** (inbound inbox gateway + Follow): migration 0037 `remote_follows` (channel_id FK,
> remote_actor_url, state, follow_activity_url; PK(channel_id, actor)) + `federation_inbox_activities`
> dedup log. httpapi `POST /inbox` + per-actor `/accounts/:h/inbox` + `/video-channels/:h/inbox` read the
> raw body (1 MiB cap), **`httpsig.Verify` the signature via `fedsvc.ResolveKey`** (401 on failure), then
> `HandleInbox` dedups by activity id and dispatches: a `Follow` of a local channel (signer must equal the
> Follow's `actor`) records an auto-accepted `remote_follows` row → 202; unknown types / foreign objects are
> accepted-and-ignored; malformed/mismatched → 400. sqlc `IsActivityProcessed`/`MarkActivityProcessed`/
> `InsertRemoteFollow`/`CountRemoteFollowers`. Gated on FEDERATION_ENABLED, excluded from the REST drift
> guard. Tested: unit (`HandleInbox` follow-records/actor-mismatch/dedup/ignore/malformed) + httpapi handler
> (signed Follow → 202 + recorded; tampered signature → 401) + integration (Follow persists + replay dedups
> against real Postgres). **Accept is auto-recorded locally; SENDING the Accept back is Slice 4b.**
>
> **Slice 4b SHIPPED** (signed outbound-delivery primitive): `internal/federation/deliver.go` — pure Go,
> no migration/queries. `unlockPrivateKey` opens the secretbox envelope when a stored actor key is sealed
> (else treats it as raw dev PEM) and parses PKCS#8 → `*rsa.PrivateKey` (errors if sealed with no KEK, or
> non-RSA). `channelActorSigner` mints-if-needed + unlocks a channel's key into an `httpsig.Signer`.
> `deliverActivity` SSRF-validates the inbox URL, `httpsig.Sign`s the payload (Date/Digest/Signature), and
> POSTs it (Content-Type activity+json) via the guarded client. `SendAcceptFollow` resolves the follower's
> inbox (remote-actor cache), builds the `Accept{object: Follow}`, and delivers it. Tested: unit — an
> httptest inbox that **verifies the signed Accept against the channel's minted public key** (correct
> keyId + Accept/Follow shape) + `unlockPrivateKey` raw/sealed round-trip + sealed-without-cipher reject.
> Not yet wired into the request path (no inline outbound call in the inbox response).
>
> **Slice 5a SHIPPED** (durable delivery queue + Follow→Accept wired): migration 0038 `federation_deliveries`
> (inbox_url, payload=unsigned activity, signing_channel_id/handle, state, attempts, next_attempt_at,
> last_error) + sqlc enqueue/claim-due/mark-delivered/reschedule/fail. `DrainDeliveries` claims due rows,
> signs each as its channel (`channelActorSigner`) and POSTs via `deliverActivity`; on failure it reschedules
> with exponential backoff (30s×2ⁿ, cap 6h) or dead-letters after 6 attempts. The inbox Follow handler now
> **enqueues an Accept** to the follower's cached inbox (no network in the request path — enqueue is a local
> write; the 202 returns immediately). cmd/api runs a single background worker (10s ticker) draining the queue
> when FEDERATION_ENABLED. Tested: unit (drain signs+delivers+marks via httptest inbox that verifies the
> signature; failure reschedules with attempts++ future) + `HandleInbox` enqueues + httpapi (signed Follow →
> 202 + Accept enqueued) + integration (enqueue/claim/reschedule against real Postgres). The Follow→Accept
> handshake is now durable end-to-end.
>
> **Slice 5b SHIPPED** (publish fan-out): sqlc `ListRemoteFollowerInboxes` (distinct inbox URLs, shared
> inbox preferred, of a channel's accepted `remote_follows` — a remote_follows⋈remote_actors join).
> `federation.AnnounceVideo(videoID)` loads the video (no-op unless public+published), its channel, and the
> follower inboxes, builds a `Create{Video}` (attributed to the channel actor, addressed to the public), and
> enqueues one signed delivery per inbox via the Slice 5a queue. Wired without coupling: `video.Service`
> gained an optional `WithPublishHook` that `Process` invokes on the published transition; cmd/api wires it
> to `AnnounceVideo` when FEDERATION_ENABLED (best-effort, logged, never blocks/rolls back publish). Tested:
> unit (fan-out enqueues one Create per follower + shape; skips private/unpublished/no-followers/unknown) +
> integration (publish → delivery enqueued to a real remote follower's inbox via the join, against Postgres).
>
> **Slice 5c SHIPPED** (actor collection endpoints): the followers/following/outbox collections the actor
> documents advertise are now served — `GET /video-channels/:h/{followers,following,outbox}` and
> `/accounts/:h/…`, content-negotiated (406 without an AP Accept), 404 unknown, gated on FEDERATION_ENABLED
> and excluded from the REST drift guard. Each returns a summary AS `OrderedCollection` with an accurate
> `totalItems`: channel followers = local `CountChannelFollowers` + `CountRemoteFollowers`; outbox =
> `CountPublicVideosByChannel` (new sqlc); following = 0; accounts are empty (attribution actors). Tested:
> federation unit (counts + not-found for unknown handle/kind) + httpapi (OrderedCollection shape/totalItems,
> 406/404, absent-when-disabled). Paged `orderedItems` (the actual videos/followers) are a later refinement.
>
> **Slice 5d SHIPPED** (inbound Undo{Follow}): the inbox now handles `Undo` — a remote actor un-following a
> local channel. `dispatchActivity` gains an `Undo` case → `handleUndo` parses the embedded Follow, checks
> the undoer is the request signer (and the Follow's actor), resolves the local channel, and
> `DeleteRemoteFollow` (new sqlc, idempotent). Undo of a non-Follow is accepted-and-ignored; actor mismatch →
> 400. Tested: unit (Undo removes the follow / non-Follow ignored / actor-mismatch rejected) + integration
> (follow → undo → CountRemoteFollowers 1 → 0 against real Postgres). Completes the inbound follow lifecycle.
>
> **DESIGN GATE for the remaining federation items.** "Receive remote video activity" (inbound
> Create/Announce) and "follow remote instance/channel/account" (OUTBOUND follow) are interdependent and hinge
> on **product decisions not covered by specs**: whether/how a local user follows a remote channel, how remote
> videos are stored (dedicated `remote_videos` table) and whether/where they surface in Vidra's feeds, and how
> remote content is moderated. These should be resolved by a spec addendum or user product input BEFORE
> implementation — do not build them blind.
>
> **Slice 5e SHIPPED** (outbound Update/Delete): editing/deleting a channel's own video now propagates to its
> remote followers. `video.Service` gained `WithUpdateHook`/`WithDeleteHook` (Update fires after a metadata
> update; Delete fires after removal, passed the video id + channel id + whether it was public). cmd/api wires
> them to `federation.UpdateVideo`/`DeleteVideo`. `UpdateVideo` sends `Update{Video}` while the video is still
> public+published, else a `Delete` (unfederate, e.g. went private); `DeleteVideo` sends a `Delete` (object =
> the video's AP URL) only if it had been public. Both reuse the shared `fanOutToFollowers` + delivery queue.
> Tested: unit (Update sends Update{Video}; went-private sends Delete; DeleteVideo sends Delete of the URL;
> non-public delete is a no-op).
>
> **Slice 5f SHIPPED** (paged outbox): the channel outbox is now a real paged AS collection. The base
> `GET /video-channels/:h/outbox` returns an `OrderedCollection` with `totalItems` + a `first` page link;
> `?page=N` returns an `OrderedCollectionPage` (`partOf` + `orderedItems` = the channel's public videos as
> `Create{Video}` activities, newest first, `next` link while a page is full). Backed by a new lean sqlc
> `ListChannelOutboxVideos` (LIMIT/OFFSET) + `ChannelOutboxPage`; the `videoObject` builder is shared with the
> delivery activities. 20/page. Tested: federation unit (page shape/partOf/next on a full page, no-next on a
> partial page, unknown-channel 404, `first` link on the base collection) + httpapi (base OrderedCollection
> has `first`; page returns an OrderedCollectionPage with the items). Still DESIGN-GATED: inbound remote
> video/comment receive + outbound follow-remote-channel (remote-content storage/feed-surfacing/moderation
> product model). NEXT (unblocked): federated comments as `Note` on local videos, or a golden-fixture
> contract test suite.
>
> **DESIGN GATE RESOLVED** by `.ralph/specs/federation-remote-content.md` (2026-07-03): remote-video
> storage/surfacing/moderation and the outbound-follow model are now specced; slices below implement it.
>
> **Slice core-fed-inbound SHIPPED** (remote-content §1 §2 §5 §8): migrations 0050 `remote_videos` +
> 0051 `muted_instances`/`blocked_instances`; inbox dispatch gains `Create{Video}` + `Announce{Video|url}`
> (signer/attribution/same-origin authority, SSRF-guarded 1 MiB object fetch for URL-only announces,
> bounded-field idempotent upsert by `object_url`, gated on the new `federation.FollowEdgeChecker` — NOT
> yet wired in cmd/api since the outbound `remote_channel_follows` edge table is the NEXT slice, so inbound
> remote videos are accepted-and-ignored until then, per §2); best-effort SSRF-guarded 2 MiB thumbnail cache
> at `remote-thumbnails/<id>.jpg`; REST `GET /api/v1/remote-videos/:id[/thumbnail]` (new `internal/remotevideo`,
> blocked-domain-filtered, drift-guarded in openapi); instance moderation (new `internal/instancemod`,
> `POST/DELETE/GET /me/mutes/instances[/:domain]`, audited admin `GET/POST/DELETE /admin/instances/blocked[/:domain]`)
> with the blocklist enforced at the inbox (post-signature drop, not marked processed), the remote-video read
> model, and the delivery drain (cancels to blocked hosts). Unit + handler + real-PG integration tests; `make ci`
> + `-tags=integration -race` green 2026-07-03. NEXT: outbound remote-channel follow (§3 — `remote_channel_follows`
> + WebFinger-driven follow + Accept/Reject handling + wire the ingestion edge-checker + subscriptions feed UNION).
>
> **Slice core-fed-follow SHIPPED** (remote-content §3 §4): migration 0052 `remote_channel_follows`
> (PK (user_id, remote_actor_url) per spec + surrogate UNIQUE `id` as the REST delete key; state
> pending|accepted; `follow_activity_url` matched by inbound Accept/Reject) + `federation_deliveries`
> gains `signing_user_id`/`signing_username` so the queue can sign as ACCOUNT actors
> (`accountActorSigner`, mirroring `channelActorSigner`). REST `POST/GET /api/v1/me/remote-follows` +
> `DELETE /api/v1/me/remote-follows/:id` (delete is BY ROW ID — documented in openapi; unfollow deletes
> immediately — local intent wins — then queues an account-signed `Undo{Follow}`); follow accepts
> `{handle: name@domain}` (WebFinger on the remote domain — https-first, plain-http fallback only under
> the dev allowPrivateFetch knob — SSRF-guarded, 64 KiB JRD cap) or `{actor_url}`; local targets 422;
> POST is 503 while FEDERATION_ENABLED=false (no outbound fetches on a non-federating instance) though
> the routes always mount (REST contract; AP root routes stay excluded from the drift guard). Inbound
> `Accept{Follow}`/`Reject{Follow}` flip/delete the row matched by `follow_activity_url` AND
> signer==followed-actor (enforced in the UPDATE/DELETE predicate). The §2 ingestion edge gate is now
> LIVE: `hasAcceptedFollowEdge` consults `HasAcceptedRemoteChannelFollow` through the repo by default
> (`WithFollowEdgeChecker` is now a test-only override; no cmd/api wiring needed). Feeds (§3/§4):
> `ListSubscriptionVideos`, `SearchPublicVideos`, and `ListPublicVideosSorted` became UNION ALL queries
> surfacing remote cards (`remote:true`, origin `domain`, `watch_url`/`stream_url`, `name@domain`
> channel_handle, thumbnail flag; local-only channel_id/privacy/state omitted) — subscriptions from
> ACCEPTED follows only; search by title match; the public feed only with the new `?scope=local|all`
> param (default local; unknown→local; remote rows drop out under tag/category/language filters and
> sort by 0 views under popular/trending); all three respect `blocked_instances` (everyone) + the
> viewer's `muted_instances`. openapi: 3 new ops + `RemoteFollow(ListResponse)`/
> `CreateRemoteFollowRequest` schemas + remote-card fields and `scope` on `Video`/`VideoFeedResponse`.
> Tests: federation unit (pending+account-signed enqueue/idempotent re-follow/WebFinger resolve via
> httptest/local+bad-target sentinels/Undo on unfollow/Accept flips + wrong-signer ignored/Reject
> deletes/repo-backed edge gate) + account-signed drain (httpsig-verified) + handler
> (`remote_follows_test.go` flow/validation/auth/503-disabled; `remote_feed_test.go` remote-card shape
> on subscriptions/feed-scope/search) + real-PG integration (`TestRemoteChannelFollowPersists`,
> `TestSubscriptionAndDiscoveryUnionsPersist` incl. mute/block filters + pending-not-enough, and
> `TestRemoteFollowFullLoop` — the §9 bar: an httptest remote instance serves WebFinger+actor, its
> inbox verifies the account-actor HTTP signature on the delivered Follow, the test answers Accept →
> accepted, a Create ingests through the live edge gate, the video appears in that user's
> subscriptions feed, and the Undo is verified on unfollow). Gate: `make ci` +
> `-tags=integration -race ./internal/...` green 2026-07-03. NEXT (remote-content): §6 federated
> comments, §7 inbound Update/Delete of remote videos, §8 remote_video reports/blocks.
>
> **Slice core-fed-comments SHIPPED** (remote-content §6 §7 §8-tail): migration 0053 comment
> remote attribution — `comments.user_id` DROP NOT NULL + `remote_actor_url` (FK → remote_actors
> ON DELETE CASCADE) + `remote_author_name` snapshot + `remote_object_url` UNIQUE (Note dedupe /
> Update/Delete lookup key) + CHECK exactly-one-author; migration 0054 — reports gain
> `target_type='remote_video'` + `remote_video_id` FK (one report per reporter+target) and
> `remote_video_blocks` mirrors video_blocks. **Inbound** (`internal/federation/notes.go`):
> `Create{Note}` — signer==activity actor==attributedTo, Note id same-origin as signer, inReplyTo
> must resolve to a LOCAL public+published video (its AP URL, our /comments/<id> URL, or a known
> remote comment's object URL — threading); body HTML-stripped + truncated to the §6 5000-char cap
> (the 2000 cap stays composer-side for local comments); author-name snapshot from the cached
> signer actor; idempotent upsert by remote_object_url; watched-words flagging applies
> (`federation.WithCommentFlagger(watchwordsvc)` in cmd/api). `Update{Note}` — original attributed
> actor only. `Update{Video}` now dispatches to the §7 upsert path (same authority + edge gate as
> Create). `Delete` — object may be a known remote video, a federated comment, or a cached remote
> actor; authority = attributed actor OR same-origin signer; an actor Delete cascades away its
> videos + comments + follow edges (FKs). **Outbound** (§6): `comment.Service` gained
> `WithCreateHook`/`WithUpdateHook`/`WithDeleteHook` (fire for LOCAL comments only — remote-authored
> and moderator-deleted-remote stay local-only) wired in cmd/api to
> `federation.AnnounceComment`/`UpdateComment`/`DeleteComment`: Create/Update/Delete{Note}
> (inReplyTo = the video AP URL, or the parent's URL for replies — a remote parent keeps its origin
> object URL), attributed to AND account-signed as the commenter (`fanOutAsAccount`, key minted
> pre-enqueue) to the channel's remote follower inboxes via the delivery queue — no
> comment→federation import coupling. **Views** (§6): `ListCommentsByVideo`/`ListAdminComments`
> author join became LEFT + LEFT JOIN remote_actors; comment views expose `remote`,
> `author_domain`, nullable `author_id`, remote_author_name-as-username; remote comment rows honor
> blocked_instances (everyone) + the viewer's muted_instances; `CountComments` (NodeInfo
> localComments) now counts local only. **Remote-video moderation** (§8-tail): REST
> `POST /api/v1/remote-videos/:id/report` (requireAuth, FK-404, idempotent) +
> `GET /api/v1/admin/remote-videos/blocked`, `POST/DELETE /api/v1/admin/remote-videos/:id/block`
> (requireRole admin/moderator, audited `moderation.remote_video.block/unblock`); blocked remote
> videos vanish from subscriptions/search/scope=all feed (`remote_video_blocks` NOT EXISTS in the
> three UNION branches) and `GetRemoteVideoByID`; report queue rows carry remote-video
> title+domain. openapi: 4 new ops + `BlockedRemoteVideo(ListResponse)` schemas + Comment/
> AdminComment/Report schema updates (drift guard green; AP routes stay excluded). Tests:
> federation unit (`notes_test.go` — parse/authority/threading incl. remote-sibling/truncation/
> HTML-strip/non-public-ignore/flagger; Update authority; Delete authority for comment/video/actor
> incl. cascade; `outbox_comments_test.go` — account-signed Create/Update/Delete{Note} shapes,
> reply threading to local + remote parents, remote-authored/non-public/no-follower/unknown
> no-ops) + comment unit (hooks fire local-only, remote rows flagged in lists, remote never
> editable) + handler (`admin_remote_videos_test.go` report 401/404/422/idempotent; block flow
> 403/404/idempotent + block-list shape + unblock; remote comment view shape) + real-PG
> integration (`comments_integration_test.go`: exactly-one-author CHECK, idempotent redelivery,
> instance mute/block filters on comment lists, actor-delete cascade,
> `TestRemoteVideoModerationPersists` — report FK/dedupe + queue context + block hides
> detail/subscriptions/search + unblock restores, and `TestFederatedCommentFullLoop` — the §9 bar:
> an httptest remote actor's Create{Note} lands as a comment on a local video, a local comment
> fans out through the create hook + delivery queue as an account-signed Create{Note} verified by
> the remote inbox, then inbound Update{Note} edits and Delete removes it). Gate: `make ci` +
> `DATABASE_URL=… -tags=integration -race ./internal/...` green 2026-07-03. Remaining in
> remote-content: none design-gated — v1 excludes local comments on REMOTE videos
> (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE per §6; interactions live at the origin).

> **Slice core-fed-contract SHIPPED** (fix_plan P10 "federation contract tests using fixtures" +
> remote-content §9 golden-fixture bar): test-only slice — no routes/migrations/sqlc changes; the
> suite is ordinary `go test` in `internal/federation`, so it runs inside `make ci` (test-race)
> automatically. **Corpus** (`internal/federation/testdata/inbound/`, 19 hand-built SANITIZED
> real-shaped files — never copied verbatim; domains peertube.example / mastodon.example /
> videos.example; public-key PEMs are base64 "NOT A REAL KEY" placeholders — no private keys
> anywhere): PeerTube- and Mastodon-shaped WebFinger JRDs (incl. Mastodon's extra profile-page +
> subscribe-template links), Person/Group actor docs (@context extension objects, endpoints.
> sharedInbox, playlists/featured extras), Follow (both flavors), Undo{Follow}, Accept/Reject
> {Follow} (fragment ids like `#rejects/follows/58`), full PeerTube Create{Video} (attributedTo
> ARRAY of Person+Group objects, url array with html watch link + HLS m3u8 + mp4 + magnet, icon
> array, PT212S duration, category object, views/sensitive extras), URL-only Announce + the
> standalone Video object doc (Person-first attribution), Update{Video}, Delete (video string
> object / Note Tombstone / actor self-delete with an LD-signature blob to ignore), Mastodon
> Create{Note} threaded to a local video (HTML content + entities + hashtag anchors, contentMap,
> replies collection), a cross-user reply Note, Update{Note}, and Like/View (accepted-and-ignored).
> **(a) Inbound** (`contract_fixture_test.go`): 19-case table drives every fixture through
> `HandleInbox` with a seeded edge-checker asserting dispatch outcome — follows recorded + Accept
> queued, Accept flips ONLY when the followed actor signs, Reject deletes, Create{Video} ingests
> the full shape (HLS preferred over mp4/magnet, html link = watch_url, PT212S→212s, RFC3339
> published) and is ignored without an edge / rejected on signer mismatch, Update upserts in
> place, Deletes remove video/comment and cascade an actor, Note stores stripped+unescaped body
> with author snapshot and remote-sibling threading, foreign Update{Note} can't edit, Like/View
> no-op but mark processed. `TestContractWebFingerResolution` follows `handle@domain` end-to-end
> through the JRD+actor fixtures via a fixtureTransport (no network); `TestContractAnnounce
> FetchesFixtureObject` exercises the object-fetch + owner-actor-resolution path from fixtures.
> **(b) Outbound goldens** (`contract_golden_test.go` + `testdata/golden/`, 16 files): every
> document we mint — Person/Group actor docs, WebFinger JRD, Accept{Follow} (captured off the
> queue in reply to the real-shaped Mastodon Follow), Follow + Undo{Follow}, Create/Update/
> Delete{Video}, Create{Note} + remote-parent reply + Update/Delete{Note}, outbox OrderedCollection
> + page + followers collection — canonicalized (volatile `/activities/<verb>/<uuid>` ids and
> minted key PEMs scrubbed, keys sorted, stable indent) and BYTE-compared against the golden; any
> shape regression flips the test (verified by mutating a golden → FAIL → restore). Regenerate
> intentionally with `UPDATE_GOLDEN=1 go test ./internal/federation -run ContractGolden`.
> **(c) Digest/Signature interop** (`contract_httpsig_test.go`): the PT Create{Video} fixture is
> signed with `httpsig.Signer` exactly like outbound delivery and verified through the service's
> own `ResolveKey` (remote-actor cache) like the inbox — asserting the fediverse-standard
> `SHA-256=<b64>` Digest, returned keyId, end-to-end dispatch of the verified body, and rejection
> of a tampered body + wrong-key signature. **No parsing gaps surfaced**: the existing parsers
> handled every real-shaped variant (attributedTo object arrays, Tombstones, id fragments, magnet
> mediaTypes, null/extra fields) — no parser fix needed this slice. Gate: `make ci` +
> `DATABASE_URL=… -tags=integration -race ./internal/...` green 2026-07-03.

## P10.1 ActivityPub

- [x] Implement local actor model for accounts. (Person actor: `account_actor_keys` side table + lazy RSA-2048 keypair minting (`internal/federation` `ensureAccountKey`, sealed at rest via `internal/secretbox`), served as `GET /accounts/:handle` — Slice 2a/2b.)
- [x] Implement local actor model for channels. (Group actor: `channel_actor_keys` + `ensureChannelKey`, served as `GET /video-channels/:handle` — Slice 2a/2b.)
- [x] Implement WebFinger. (`GET /.well-known/webfinger?resource=acct:name@domain` → self link to the actor URL; own-domain-only, resolves account then channel — Slice 2b.)
- [x] Implement ActivityPub actor endpoints. (Content-negotiated actor documents — `application/activity+json`, 406 otherwise — with `@context` (incl. security vocab), derived inbox/outbox/followers/following collection URLs, and the `publicKey` block. The collection endpoints themselves land in Slices 4-5 — Slice 2b.)
- [x] Implement inbox endpoint. (Shared `POST /inbox` + per-actor `/accounts/:h/inbox` + `/video-channels/:h/inbox`; HTTP-signature-verified (401 on failure), body-bounded, deduped by activity id, dispatched by type. A remote `Follow` of a local channel is recorded (auto-accepted); other types accepted-and-ignored for now — Slice 4a. Sending the Accept back + inbound Create/Announce/Delete land in later slices.)
- [x] Implement outbox endpoint. (Slices 5c+5f — `GET /video-channels/:h/outbox` returns an AS `OrderedCollection` (`totalItems` = `CountPublicVideosByChannel`, `first` page link); `?page=N` returns an `OrderedCollectionPage` of the channel's public videos as `Create{Video}` activities (newest first, `next` while full, 20/page, via `ListChannelOutboxVideos`). `/accounts/:h/outbox` + the followers/following collections are served too (summaries). Content-negotiated + FEDERATION_ENABLED-gated + excluded from the REST drift guard.)
- [x] Implement HTTP signatures. (`internal/httpsig` RSA-SHA256 cavage sign/verify over `(request-target) host date digest` with Digest + skew, unit-tested (Slice 3a). **Inbound verification WIRED** (Slice 4a): the inbox verifies every request via `httpsig.Verifier{ResolveKey: fedsvc.ResolveKey}` against the remote-actor key cache. **Outbound signing WIRED** (Slice 4b): `federation.deliverActivity` unlocks the local actor key (secretbox → rsa) and `httpsig.Sign`s each delivery — verified end-to-end by an httptest inbox in `deliver_test.go`. What remains for federation broadly is the *delivery queue* that schedules outbound sends (separate item below), not the signature mechanism itself.)
- [x] Implement JSON-LD signature strategy or documented compatibility plan. (Documented compatibility plan — `.ralph/specs/federation.md` §7: Vidra authenticates server-to-server with **HTTP Signatures** (RSA-SHA256) and does NOT emit JSON-LD/LD-Signatures, matching Mastodon's default and PeerTube interop. Object integrity rides the signed Digest, not LD proofs.)
- [x] Implement follow remote instance/channel/account. (core-fed-follow slice, remote-content §3: a local USER follows a remote CHANNEL (PeerTube subscription-model parity) — migration 0052 `remote_channel_follows` + REST `POST/GET /api/v1/me/remote-follows`, `DELETE /me/remote-follows/:id`; WebFinger-driven resolve (SSRF-guarded) or direct actor URL; pending row + `Follow` enqueued signed as the user's ACCOUNT actor (`federation_deliveries.signing_user_id`/`signing_username`, `accountActorSigner`); inbound `Accept`/`Reject` matched by `follow_activity_url` + signer==followed-actor; unfollow = immediate delete + queued `Undo{Follow}`. Accepted follows feed the subscriptions-feed UNION and the §2 ingestion gate. Following whole remote instances/accounts is not a v1 surface (channel follows are the PeerTube subscription parity); revisit if the ledger surfaces a concrete PT parity gap. Unit + handler + real-PG integration incl. the §9 full-loop test — see the core-fed-follow slice log above.)
- [x] Implement receive remote video activity. (core-fed-inbound slice, per federation-remote-content.md §1-2. **Storage**: migration 0050 `remote_videos` (id, `object_url` UNIQUE = the AP object id / dedupe key, `remote_actor_url` FK → remote_actors ON DELETE CASCADE, bounded title ≤500 / description ≤5000 — truncated never rejected, duration_seconds, published_at, watch_url, stream_url — HLS `application/x-mpegURL` link preferred else `video/*` else NULL, thumbnail_key, fetched/updated); sqlc `remote_videos.sql` (`UpsertRemoteVideo` idempotent by object_url RETURNING id+thumbnail_key / `SetRemoteVideoThumbnail` / `GetRemoteVideoByID` JOIN remote_actors + blocked-domain NOT EXISTS). **Inbox dispatch** (`internal/federation/ingest.go`): `Create{Video}` — signer must be the activity actor (else 400 `ErrActorMismatch`), the object must live on the signer's origin AND be attributed to the signer (else accept-and-ignore); `Announce{Video|url}` — signer is the announcing channel, only same-origin objects are ingested (no proxy-fetching third-party origins), an unseen/URL-only object is fetched through the SSRF guard (1 MiB cap, activity+json Accept), attribution must be same-origin (owner actor resolved+cached for the FK, falls back to the signer). Metadata-only: media is NEVER mirrored. **Ingestion gate**: `federation.FollowEdgeChecker` (`HasAcceptedFollow`) — the §2 has-accepted-local-follower anti-spam gate. INTENTIONALLY NOT WIRED in cmd/api yet: it needs the OUTBOUND `remote_channel_follows` table from the NEXT slice (federation-remote-content.md §3); until then inbound Create/Announce are accepted-and-ignored (202, no store) exactly per spec. Tested via a fake edge-checker. **Read surface**: new `internal/remotevideo` service + REST `GET /api/v1/remote-videos/:id` (+ `/thumbnail`) — public, 404 for unknown/blocked-origin, `remote: true` + origin `domain` in the view; documented in openapi (REST drift guard green; AP root routes stay excluded). Tested: 14 federation unit (authority/actor-mismatch/foreign-attribution/cross-origin/no-edge ignore/bounds truncation/HLS-over-mp4 link pick/duration+published parse/idempotent upsert incl. same-activity dedupe vs new-activity update/Announce fetch via httptest origin/Announce foreign-object never fetched/gate-before-fetch) + remotevideo unit + handler (`remote_videos_test.go` 200 shape incl. remote/domain/stream_url/has_thumbnail, 404 unknown + malformed) + real-PG integration (`TestRemoteVideoIngestionPersists`: HandleInbox → row, upsert idempotency at the DB, thumbnail key round-trip, FK against real remote_actors). **UPDATE (core-fed-follow slice)**: the edge gate is now LIVE — `hasAcceptedFollowEdge` consults `remote_channel_follows` through the repo by default — and remote videos surface in the subscriptions feed, search, and the `?scope=all` public feed (§3-4; UNION queries with instance mute/blocklist filters). **UPDATE (core-fed-comments slice)**: inbound `Update{Video}` (same authority + edge gate as Create) and `Delete` of remote videos/actors are handled per §7 — item complete.)
- [x] Implement announce video from channel. (Slice 5b — on the published transition, `AnnounceVideo` builds a `Create{Video}` attributed to the channel actor and fans it out to the channel's accepted remote followers via the delivery queue (`ListRemoteFollowerInboxes`, shared-inbox-preferred, signed per channel). Wired through `video.WithPublishHook` (no video→federation coupling). A dedicated re-`Announce` of an existing/imported video and the outbox collection endpoint are later refinements.)
- [x] Implement federated comments. (core-fed-comments slice, remote-content §6 — inbound Create/Update{Note} on local videos stored with remote attribution (migration 0053; signer==attributedTo authority, same-origin Note ids, 5000-char truncate + HTML strip, watched-words flagging, threading via video URL / local comment URL / remote comment object URL), outbound Create/Update/Delete{Note} fan-out to the channel's remote followers signed as the commenter's ACCOUNT actor (comment-service hooks in cmd/api — no import coupling), comment views expose remote+author_domain with instance mute/blocklist filters. v1 INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE: local comments on REMOTE videos are excluded (origin's watch page is the place to comment) and a local moderator delete of a remote comment is local-only (no federated Reject upstream). See the core-fed-comments slice log above for the full test evidence.)
- [x] Implement federated deletes/updates. (Inbound `Undo{Follow}` DONE (5d). **Outbound `Update`/`Delete` of a channel's own videos DONE (5e)**: editing a public+published video fans an `Update{Video}` to remote followers; editing it out of public (e.g. → private) or deleting it fans a `Delete` (unfederate) — via `video.WithUpdateHook`/`WithDeleteHook` → `federation.UpdateVideo`/`DeleteVideo` → the delivery queue. **Inbound `Update`/`Delete` of REMOTE objects DONE (core-fed-comments slice, §7)**: `Update{Video}` upserts through the same authority+edge-gate path as Create; `Update{Note}` applies only for the original attributed actor; `Delete` handles remote videos, federated comments, and remote actors (authority: attributed actor OR same-origin signer; an actor Delete cascades its videos+comments+follow edges via FKs). Outbound comment deletes/updates fan out too (§6). Unit + integration evidence in the core-fed-comments slice log.)
- [x] Implement federation queue/retry/dead-letter. (`federation_deliveries` table + `DrainDeliveries` worker — Slice 5a. Claims due pending rows, signs each as its channel and POSTs via the SSRF-guarded client; success → delivered, failure → reschedule with exponential backoff (30s×2ⁿ, cap 6h) or dead-letter (state 'failed') after 6 attempts. A single background worker (cmd/api, 10s ticker) drains it. First producer wired: an inbound Follow enqueues a signed Accept. More producers (Create/Announce) land with the outbox slice.)
- [x] Implement remote media cache strategy. (Decided in federation-remote-content.md §5 and implemented in the core-fed-inbound slice: cache **thumbnails only** — video bytes are NEVER mirrored (bandwidth + licensing). On ingestion, `cacheRemoteThumbnail` best-effort fetches the object's `icon` URL through the SSRF guard (2 MiB cap read-one-past to detect oversize; content-type must be image/*), stores it at `remote-thumbnails/<id>.jpg` via the shared `storage.Backend` (`federation.WithMediaStorage(blobs)` in cmd/api), and records `thumbnail_key`; ANY failure is non-fatal (video ingests without a poster; an existing cached key is never re-fetched on upsert). Served at `GET /api/v1/remote-videos/:id/thumbnail` (image/jpeg; 404 when uncached). Tested: unit (cache round-trip against an httptest image origin — key + bytes land in a local backend; oversized poster skipped while ingestion succeeds) + handler (thumbnail 200/content-type/bytes + 404) + integration (thumbnail_key persists).)
- [x] Add federation contract tests using fixtures. (core-fed-contract slice — 19-file sanitized real-shaped PeerTube/Mastodon corpus in `internal/federation/testdata/inbound/` driven through `HandleInbox`/WebFinger/actor/object-fetch paths (19-case dispatch table + resolution tests, seeded edge-checker, fixtureTransport — no network), 16 golden files in `testdata/golden/` byte-guarding every outbound document we mint (actor docs, WebFinger, Accept, Follow/Undo, Create/Update/Delete{Video}, Create/Update/Delete{Note} incl. remote-parent reply threading, outbox collection/page/followers; canonicalized — volatile activity-id UUIDs + minted PEMs scrubbed; regenerate via `UPDATE_GOLDEN=1`), plus an httpsig sign→verify round-trip of a fixture through the service's real `ResolveKey` (Digest `SHA-256=<b64>`, tamper + wrong-key rejection, verified-body dispatch). Ordinary go tests → run in `make ci`. No parser gaps found (attributedTo object arrays, Tombstones, fragment ids, magnet links, extra/null fields all already handled). See the core-fed-contract slice log above.)

# P11 — Messaging

## P11.1 Normal Secure Messaging

- [x] Implement conversations. (1:1 DM: `conversations.dm_key` = sorted `"<uuid>:<uuid>"`, UNIQUE → idempotent start-or-get; migration 0031; `internal/messaging`, `POST /api/v1/conversations`)
- [x] Implement conversation participants. (`conversation_participants`; `GET /api/v1/me/conversations` inbox with other participant + last-message preview, most-recently-active first)
- [x] Implement message send/list/read. (`messages` table; `POST/GET /api/v1/conversations/{id}/messages`, newest-first, participant-only → non-participant/unknown conversation is 404; 5000-char body cap; `TouchConversation` bumps recency. **Sending now notifies the recipient** — a `message`-type notification linking the conversation, via `OtherParticipant` + `notification.NotifyMessage`; see P8 notification item + migration 0032.)
- [x] Implement message attachments. (core-dm-completeness, product-decisions.md §14, 2026-07-03. Migration 0064 `message_attachments` + storage at `dm-attachments/<conv>/<id>.<ext>`. `POST /api/v1/conversations/:id/attachments` (multipart `file`, participant-gated → 404, 25 MiB cap → 413, image/video/audio/pdf allowlist → 415, ENCRYPTED conversation → 422 documented) returns `{attachment_id,...}`; a plaintext send accepts `attachment_ids: []` (≤4, own-uploaded + same conversation + not-yet-sent, else 422); message list views carry `attachments: [{id,kind,content_type,filename,size_bytes}]`; `GET /api/v1/attachments/:id` streams the bytes participant-gated (unknown/stranger → 404). `messaging.UploadAttachment`/`AttachmentForDownload` + `SendMessage(attachmentIDs)`; body-limit exemption for the upload route (25 MiB). Tested: 3 service (upload+link+download+stranger-404, reject unsupported/non-participant/infected/scan-error, foreign+reused-attachment) + 1 handler (`TestDMAttachmentFlow`: 415 → upload → send → peer download → stranger 404 → non-participant upload 404) + real-PG integration (`TestDMCompletenessQueries`).)
- [x] Implement attachment virus scanning. (core-dm-completeness, 2026-07-03. `messaging.WithAttachments(blobs, scanner, max)` runs the shared `media.NewClamAV` scanner (wired only when `MALWARE_SCAN_ENABLED`) fail-closed: any infection OR scan error deletes the stored bytes and rejects with 422. Tested at the service layer (infected → `ErrAttachmentRejected`; scan-error → `ErrAttachmentRejected`).)
- [x] Implement link preview extraction with SSRF protection. (core-dm-completeness, product-decisions.md §14, 2026-07-03. New `internal/linkpreview` package: `FirstURL` extracts the first http(s) URL from a plaintext body; `Fetcher` GETs it through an SSRF-guarded `urlsafety.Guard` client (1 MiB `io.LimitReader`, html-only Content-Type, `x/net/html` OpenGraph title/description/image + `<title>` fallback). On send, the messaging service upserts a pending `link_previews` row keyed by the URL sha256, links the message, and runs the fetch on a DETACHED goroutine that resolves the shared cache row to ready/failed — the send is NEVER blocked or failed by a preview. `ListMessages` joins the preview only when ready. No fetch on encrypted conversations (no plaintext body). Tested: `internal/linkpreview` unit (FirstURL cases, OG/title parse, live httptest fetch, non-html reject) + service (`TestLinkPreviewAttached` ready-join via `WaitForPreviews`, `TestLinkPreviewFailureIsSilent`) + real-PG integration join.)
- [x] Implement read receipts. (core-dm-completeness, product-decisions.md §14, 2026-07-03. `conversation_participants.last_read_message_id` + `POST /api/v1/conversations/:id/read` (idempotent, advance-only; optional `{message_id}` else newest). Inbox `unread_count` (peer messages past the caller's watermark) on `ConversationSummary`; the thread response exposes `peer_last_read_message_id` (the peer's watermark). Per-user privacy toggle: **reused `notification_prefs` under a `read_receipts` pseudo-type** (documented choice over a dedicated column — least code, same storage) via `GET/PATCH /api/v1/me/messaging-prefs` `{read_receipts}`; when a peer disables it, their watermark is hidden from others. Tested: service (`TestReadReceiptsAndUnread` — unread counts, watermark, toggle hides) + handler (`TestDMReadReceipts`, `TestDMMessagingPrefsDefault`) + real-PG integration (unread + `GetPeerReadWatermark`).)
- [x] Implement per-message delete + report. (core-dm-completeness, product-decisions.md §14, 2026-07-03. `DELETE /api/v1/messages/:id` — sender-only tombstone (body → `[deleted]`, `deleted_at` set, attachments' bytes+rows removed, preview dropped; non-sender/unknown → 404, already-deleted idempotent 204). `POST /api/v1/messages/:id/report` — either participant reports (target_type=`message`, `message_id` + `message_body_snapshot` into `reports`, survives a sender tombstone; non-participant/unknown → 404, idempotent per reporter+message). Migration 0064 widens the reports target_type CHECK + adds `message_id`/`message_body_snapshot`; `moderation.ReportMessage` + `ListReports` surfaces it in the admin queue. Tested: service (`TestDeleteMessage`, `TestReportableMessage`, moderation `TestReportMessage`) + handler (`TestDMDeleteMessage`, `TestDMReportMessage`) + real-PG integration.)
- [x] Implement typing presence or explicitly defer. (INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE — polling model, no presence; documented in product-decisions.md §14.)
- [x] Implement blocking/reporting integration. (**Blocking DONE**: a user-to-user block model (migration 0033 `user_blocks`, `internal/block` service, `POST/DELETE/GET /api/v1/me/blocks[/:id]`) now gates direct messaging **symmetrically** — if EITHER user has blocked the other, `StartConversation` and `SendMessage` return `ErrBlocked` → HTTP 403 ("cannot message this user"). Wired via a small `messaging.Blocker` interface + `messaging.WithBlocker(blocksvc)` in `cmd/api` (participant check precedes the block check, so a non-participant is still 404, not a 403 existence-leak). See P9 "user blocks". **Reporting** integration is already available (a user can report the account they're messaging via `POST /users/:id/report`). **Block-hides-content now DONE** (§13, see the P9 user-blocks item): blocking also hides the blocked account's comments + videos from the blocker across comments/feed/search/subscriptions. **Per-message report now DONE too** (core-dm-completeness, §14) — see the "per-message delete + report" item below. No messaging reporting deferrals remain.)
- [x] Add messaging API tests. (`internal/messaging/service_test.go` — start/self/recipient-404/send/list/authz; `internal/httpapi/messaging_test.go` — full HTTP flow incl. idempotent start, sender identity, non-participant 404, self 422, unknown 404, anon 401)

## P11.2 Encrypted Messaging

- [x] Write E2EE threat model before implementation. (`.ralph/specs/e2ee.md` §1 — protects against server compromise/subpoena of content and passive network observers; explicitly NOT against metadata, key-substitution MITM (v1 mitigates via user-visible fingerprints), compromised endpoints, or history for new devices.)
- [x] Choose audited protocol/library; do not invent crypto. (`.ralph/specs/e2ee.md` §2 — Olm double ratchet via `@matrix-org/olm` (NCC Group audit 2016), 1:1 sessions only, CLIENT-side in vidra-user; vidra-core implements zero cryptography and treats all E2EE payloads as opaque bounded strings; migration path vodozemac.)
- [x] Implement device registration model. (migration 0058 `e2ee_devices` + `internal/e2ee` service: `POST/GET /api/v1/e2ee/devices` (register with public Curve25519 identity + Ed25519 signing keys, bounded name; list own = frontend availability probe), `DELETE /api/v1/e2ee/devices/:id` (owner-scoped, cascades OTKs + envelopes both directions), 20-device cap, `last_seen_at` bumped on OTK upload/send.)
- [x] Implement public identity/prekey endpoints. (`GET /api/v1/users/:id/e2ee/devices` (peer public keys) + `POST /api/v1/users/:id/e2ee/claim` — both participant-gated (404 without a shared conversation → no account/device enumeration) and refused across blocks (403). OTK upload `POST /api/v1/e2ee/devices/:id/one-time-keys` (batch ≤100, unclaimed pool ≤500, idempotent per key_id) + `GET .../one-time-keys/count` (owner replenish signal). Claim is ATOMIC single-use — `UPDATE … WHERE claimed_at IS NULL … FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED … RETURNING`, one key per device of the target, `one_time_key: null` when exhausted; audited COUNTS-ONLY (`e2ee.otk.claim`).)
- [x] Store ciphertext only for encrypted messages. (`conversations.encrypted` chosen at creation (`POST /conversations` `{encrypted:true}`), immutable, distinct `"e2ee:"` dm_key namespace so a pair can have both thread types. `POST /conversations/:id/messages` on an encrypted conversation accepts ONLY `{sender_device_id, envelopes:[{recipient_device_id, message_type, ciphertext}], expires_in_seconds?}` — one row per recipient device (client fans out), ciphertext opaque ≤64KiB (also a DB CHECK), recipient devices must belong to participants, sender device to the caller. `GET` returns ONLY the caller's devices' envelopes. Wrong shape per conversation type → 422 BOTH directions; plaintext endpoints otherwise completely unaffected (all pre-existing messaging tests pass unchanged with the e2ee service wired). Blocks apply unchanged via the same `messaging.Blocker` (start/send/claim/peer-list → 403).)
- [x] Implement disappearing message expiry metadata. (`expires_in_seconds` 30s–90d on encrypted sends → `expires_at` stamp; out-of-range → 422. Reads filter `expires_at > now()` so expiry is correct BETWEEN sweeps.)
- [x] Implement deletion/expiry worker. (`e2ee.SweepExpired` + `runE2EESweepWorker` in cmd/api — 10s ticker mirroring the transcode/account-export workers, batch 200, partial index `e2ee_messages_expiry_idx`, always on.)
- [x] Ensure backend cannot decrypt encrypted messages. (No key material beyond PUBLIC keys is ever accepted or stored; handlers/service never parse ciphertext; sensitive-key denylist extended (`ciphertext`, `envelope(s)`, `one_time_key(s)`, `identity_key`, `signing_key`) so the banned-logging guards fail any attempt to log envelope/key fields.)
- [x] Add tests for storage invariants and expiry behavior. (Unit `internal/e2ee/service_test.go`: start/idempotency/namespace, device caps + owner-scoping, OTK caps/idempotent upload/FIFO single-use claim/exhaustion-null/self-claim, send validation (plaintext-conversation refusal, foreign sender, outside recipient, duplicate, bad type, >64KiB, expiry bounds), per-recipient-device reads, block enforcement, deterministic-clock expiry filter + sweeper. Handler `internal/httpapi/e2ee_test.go`: full two-user device+OTK+claim+send+read round trip with fake ciphertext asserting BYTE-IDENTICAL return, shape-mismatch 422s both directions (+ plaintext still works), anon 401s on all 7 routes, ownership 404s, participant gating, blocks 403 + unblock restore, encrypted-flagged inbox with empty preview, notification parity. Integration `internal/e2ee/integration_test.go` (real PG): `TestE2EESchemaCiphertextOnly` (information_schema — E2EE tables carry EXACTLY the documented columns, no body/plaintext/content column anywhere), `TestE2EEClaimAtomicityUnderConcurrentClaims` (35 goroutines racing 20 keys → exactly 20 unique claims, 15 misses, pool drained), `TestE2EERoundTripAndExpiryOnRealPG` (byte-identical round trip + expired-row read filter + sweeper hard-delete). `make ci` + focused integration suite (e2ee/httpapi/store/messaging) green 2026-07-03; migration 0058 down/up round-trip verified.)
- [x] Block completion if no acceptable audited crypto approach is selected. (Satisfied by the Olm decision in `.ralph/specs/e2ee.md` §2/§6 — gate lifted. Frontend work (device setup UX, Olm sessions, fingerprints) is vidra-user's P8.2.)

# P12 — Live Streaming

- [x] Implement live stream create endpoint. (`POST /api/v1/channels/:handle/live` (requireAuth, owner-only; non-owner 403, unknown channel 404), body `{title, description?, privacy?, permanent?}` → 201 `{live_stream, stream_key, rtmp_url}`. migration 0034 `live_streams`; `internal/live` service; `internal/httpapi/live.go`. Also `GET /channels/:handle/live` (owner list, no keys), `GET /live/:id` (optionalAuth, privacy-gated public metadata, no key), `DELETE /live/:id` (owner). openapi documents all + schemas (drift guard green).)
- [x] Implement normal live vs permanent/recurring live model. (A `permanent` boolean on the stream (persisted, in create + `PATCH /live/:id` edit + views) distinguishes a reusable/recurring live from a one-shot. It now DRIVES the ingest-stop transition: `StopByIdentity` returns a permanent stream to `offline` (reusable — the key + stream persist across sessions) and a one-shot to `ended`. Tested in `internal/live/service_test.go` (`TestIngestStartStop`, `TestStartStopByIdentity`).)
- [x] Generate private stream key. (`live.generateStreamKey`: 256-bit crypto/rand token, base64url; returned to the streamer (OBS) exactly once on create and on `POST /live/:id/key` regeneration.)
- [x] Store stream key hashed or encrypted. (Only the SHA-256 hash is persisted (`live_streams.stream_key_hash`, unique — the ingest boundary will look a stream up by key hash); the raw key is never stored or re-returned. Mirrors the refresh/reset-token hashing approach.)
- [x] Implement RTMP ingestion integration boundary. (**Auth + state-flip DONE**: media-server-facing hooks `POST /api/v1/live/ingest/start` and `/stop` authenticate by the presented stream key (SHA-256 → `GetLiveStreamByKeyHash`) AND a shared secret `LIVE_INGEST_SECRET` (constant-time `X-Ingest-Secret` header; hooks 404 when the secret is unset, so they're only exposed when configured). Start flips `state`→`live`; stop → `offline` (permanent) or `ended` (one-shot). `live.StartIngest`/`StopIngest`; `internal/httpapi/live.go`; openapi documents both + `LiveIngestRequest` (drift guard green). Tested: service (start→live, stop→ended/offline-by-permanent, unknown key→ErrNotFound) + handler (disabled-without-secret 404, bad-secret 401, unknown-key 404, happy path flips state via GET). **Deploy wiring**: `docker-compose.yml` now passes `LIVE_INGEST_SECRET` + `LIVE_RTMP_URL` through to the api container (both empty by default → hooks stay disabled; a harness/deploy sets a strong `LIVE_INGEST_SECRET` to enable them). **VERIFIED end-to-end against the live compose stack + real Postgres** (`LIVE_INGEST_SECRET=…`): no-header/wrong-secret → 401, right-secret + unknown key → 404, and the full happy path register→create channel→create live stream (real 43-char key minted once)→ingest start (real key) → 200 `{state:live}` → owner list shows `live` → ingest stop → 204 → list shows `ended`. **Media-server config DONE (core-live-plane slice)**: committed `deploy/media/nginx.conf.template` (Arut's nginx-rtmp) + the `media` compose profile (image `alfg/nginx-rtmp`, shared `live_hls` volume). The hooks now accept BOTH JSON `{stream_key}` (harness) and the nginx-rtmp `name` form field; on the form call the on-publish hook returns a 302 that RENAMES the RTMP session to the **stream ID** (so the raw key never touches an on-disk path/URL — a credential-privacy requirement), and a post-rename re-invocation with the id is allowed without a redirect loop (`StartByIdentity` needsRename flag). nginx-rtmp cannot set headers, so the callbacks hit a local http shim in the same nginx that injects `X-Ingest-Secret`. `StopByIdentity` resolves the key OR the renamed id. Tested: `TestLiveIngestFormRedirectRename` (form→302 rename + re-publish-by-id 200 + stop-by-id 204), `TestStartStopByIdentity`.)
- [x] Implement HLS output path. (core-live-plane slice. The `media` profile's nginx-rtmp packages HLS into the shared `live_hls` volume keyed by the (renamed) stream ID — `<LIVE_HLS_ROOT>/<id>.m3u8` + `<id>-<n>.ts`. The api serves them read-only at `GET /api/v1/live/:id/hls/master.m3u8` and `…/hls/:file` (`internal/httpapi/live_hls.go`), gated by the stream's privacy (private→owner only) and state (only while `state=live`), mirroring the VOD `/hls` serving; the file name must be the id-scoped playlist or a numbered segment (traversal/foreign-id → 404), and the resolved path is re-verified to stay under `LIVE_HLS_ROOT`. Routes 404 when `LIVE_HLS_ROOT` is unset. The live view carries `hls_url` while live. Config: `LIVE_HLS_ROOT` (config.go, .env.example, README, compose). Tested: `internal/httpapi/live_test.go` `TestLiveHLSServing` (before-live 404 → live → master+segment 200 + hls_url), `TestLiveHLSDisabledWithoutRoot`, `TestLiveHLSPrivacyGate` (anon/other 404, owner 200) + `internal/live/recording_test.go` id/path normalisation.)
- [x] Implement live status updates. (The `state` column (offline/live/ended) is surfaced on every view AND is now driven by the ingest boundary above — a publisher connecting flips it live, disconnecting ends/resets it.)
- [x] Implement live replay conversion. (core-live-plane slice, P12. `live_streams.replay_enabled` (migration 0061; in create + `PATCH /live/:id` edit + views) opts a stream into recording + republish. The `media` profile's nginx-rtmp records each session (`<LIVE_HLS_ROOT>/rec/<id>-<unix>.flv`); on ingest-stop, when `replay_enabled`, the api best-effort creates a draft video on the stream's channel titled `"<title> (replay)"` (privacy inherited) and runs the recording through the SAME pipeline as an upload/import — `video.Service.CreateDraft`→`AttachOriginal`→`Process` (scan/probe/thumbnail/transcode) — so the replay appears as a normal published video. It runs DETACHED (`context.WithoutCancel` goroutine) so it never delays/fails the stop hook; every stage failure is logged + audited (`content.live.replay`), a missing recording is a quiet no-op, and replay stays dormant unless `LIVE_HLS_ROOT` is configured. `live.Service.RunReplay` + injected `VideoPipeline`/`RecordingStore`/`Auditor` (`internal/live/service.go`, `recording.go`). Tested: `internal/live/replay_test.go` (publishes with correct channel/title/privacy/owner/bytes/key + success audit; disabled→no-op; no-recording→quiet no-op; pipeline error→swallowed + failure audit; unconfigured→no-op) + `recording_test.go` (newest-session pick, id gate, traversal/unknown-ext rejection).)
- [x] Implement live stream delete/archive. (Delete: `DELETE /live/:id` (owner-only, 404 for non-owner/unknown, idempotent). Archive-to-VOD: the replay conversion above republishes a recorded session as a normal channel video. Edit: `PATCH /live/:id` (owner-only; title/description/privacy/permanent/replay_enabled; `TestLiveStreamEdit`).)
- [x] Add smoke test for live metadata and HLS path without requiring full RTMP in CI. (Metadata lifecycle fully tested without RTMP: `internal/live/service_test.go` (create returns key once + stores only the hash, get/list/edit/delete, regenerate rotates the hash, start/stop by key-or-id) + `internal/httpapi/live_test.go` (lifecycle + authz + validation + edit + ingest form/redirect). HLS-path smoke without RTMP: `TestLiveHLSServing`/`TestLiveHLSDisabledWithoutRoot`/`TestLiveHLSPrivacyGate` seed HLS files under a temp `LIVE_HLS_ROOT` and assert privacy/state gating. Real-PG `internal/store/live_streams_integration_test.go` covers the 0061 column + `UpdateLiveStream` + cascade.)
- [x] Add optional integration test profile for RTMP. (`internal/httpapi/live_rtmp_integration_test.go`, `//go:build integration`, self-skipping unless `LIVE_RTMP_TEST=1` AND ffmpeg is on PATH — so the standard `-tags=integration` gate never needs the media server. When run against `docker compose --profile core --profile media up`, it registers→channel→creates a replay-enabled live stream, ffmpeg-pushes a real 2s RTMP test pattern to the media server, and asserts the hooks flipped state (live→ended), live HLS was served, and the replay VOD (`"RTMP Session (replay)"`) was published to the channel. Documented run command in the file header + README.)
