# P13 — Captions and Whisper

- [x] Implement caption upload. (`POST /api/v1/videos/:id/captions` (requireAuth, owner-only, multipart `file` (.vtt) + `language` + optional `label`) validates the language tag + WebVTT signature, stores the .vtt via `internal/storage` at `captions/<id>/<lang>.vtt`, and upserts a `captions` row (re-uploading a language replaces it). Non-owner/unknown video → 404; bad language or non-WebVTT → 422; missing file → 400. `internal/video.AddCaption`; `internal/httpapi/captions.go`.)
- [x] Implement caption list/download/delete. (`GET /api/v1/videos/:id/captions` (public, published+public video via `publicVideoID`) → track metadata (language, label); `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/captions/:lang` (public) → serves the WebVTT with `Content-Type: text/vtt` (unknown lang → 404); `DELETE /api/v1/videos/:id/captions/:lang` (requireAuth, owner-only, idempotent → 204, also deletes the blob best-effort). `video.ListCaptions`/`OpenCaption`/`DeleteCaption`.)
- [x] Implement VTT validation. (`video.isWebVTT` requires the `WEBVTT` signature (optionally after a UTF-8 BOM) followed by EOF/newline/space/tab, per the WebVTT spec; upload is bounded to 4 MiB; the language tag must match a BCP-47-ish pattern (`^[A-Za-z]{2,3}(-[A-Za-z0-9]{1,8})*$`). Bad input → `ErrInvalidCaption` → 422.)
- [x] Implement optional Whisper job adapter. (core-whisper slice, 2026-07-03. `internal/media.WhisperClient` extracts a video's audio track (ffmpeg → 16 kHz mono `pcm_s16le` WAV, temp file; pure `whisperWavArgs` builder), POSTs it to `WHISPER_ENDPOINT`/inference as multipart (`file` + `response_format=verbose_json` + `temperature=0` + optional `language` hint — whisper.cpp server-compatible), and renders the verbose_json `segments[]` to WebVTT (pure `parseWhisperResponse`+`renderWebVTT`+`formatVTTTimestamp`, empty-cue skip, start-time sort). Endpoint is trusted operator config (not SSRF-guarded). Config: `WHISPER_ENABLED` (default false) + `WHISPER_ENDPOINT` (required+http(s)-validated when enabled) + `WHISPER_DEFAULT_LANGUAGE` (default `en`, BCP-47-validated). Audio-extraction seam (`WithAudioExtractor`) lets the HTTP/render path test without ffmpeg. See P1.2/P1.3.)
- [x] Implement auto-caption request/status. (`caption_jobs` durable queue (migration 0062, mirrors import_jobs: pending→running→done|failed, backoff+dead-letter, partial unique index = single active job/video). `internal/captionjob` worker: `POST /api/v1/videos/:id/captions/auto` (requireAuth, owner; body `{language?}` default WHISPER_DEFAULT_LANGUAGE; **503** when WHISPER disabled, **422** bad language tag, **409** while a job is pending/running, non-owner/unknown→404) → **202** `{caption_job}`; `GET /api/v1/videos/:id/captions/auto` (owner status, 404 when none). On completion the worker upserts the track through the EXISTING `video.AddCaption` path (replace-by-language) and notifies the owner via the NEW `caption_ready` notification type (honoring prefs; joined video title, no caption text stored). In-process `runCaptionJobWorker` ticker (WHISPER_ENABLED only). `error` is a SAFE reason (never the endpoint URL). openapi documents both routes + `AutoCaptionRequest`/`CaptionJob`/`CaptionJobResponse` + the `caption_ready` enum/prefs additions.)
- [x] Implement language metadata. (Each caption carries its own `language` tag (validated) + `label`. The global taxonomy config endpoint exists — `GET /api/v1/videos/config` (categories/licenses/languages/privacies; see P7) — and the once-remaining piece, per-video category/language/license STORAGE + validation, landed too: migration 0025 + `validateTaxonomy` against the `video.IsCategory`/`IsLanguage`/`IsLicense` maps, exposed on the video views — see the P6.1 metadata-validation item, VERIFIED against real PG.)
- [x] Add tests for manual captions. (`internal/video` unit: `TestIsWebVTT` (signature/BOM/glued-suffix/lowercase edge cases) + `TestLangPattern` (valid/invalid tags). `internal/httpapi` handler: `TestCaptionsFlow` (upload→list→download-with-`text/vtt`→re-upload-replaces→second-language→delete→gone→download-404) + `TestCaptionsValidationAndAuth` (non-WebVTT 422, bad-lang 422, missing-file 400, non-owner 404, anon upload/delete 401, list on a private draft 404). Real storage exercised via the handler test's `storage.Local`.)
- [x] Add Whisper mocked integration tests. (core-whisper. `internal/media`: `TestWhisperWavArgs` (ffmpeg arg vector), `TestRenderWebVTT`/`TestRenderWebVTTEmpty`/`TestFormatVTTTimestamp`/`TestParseWhisperResponse`(+ordering)/`TestParseWhisperResponseMalformed` (pure VTT conversion). `internal/captionjob`: `TestEnqueueGating` (disabled/bad-lang/default/409), `TestDrainHappyPath` (worker vs an httptest fake whisper server + stubbed audio extractor → caption stored with valid WEBVTT + owner notified), `TestDrainRetriesThenDeadLetters` (500→retry ladder→dead-letter, safe error, no caption), `TestDrainDisabledIsNoop`, `TestLatestForVideoNotFound`. `internal/httpapi`: `TestAutoCaptionRequestAndStatus`/`RespectsLanguage`/`Conflict`(409)/`Disabled`(503)/`AuthAndOwnership` (401/404/422). Config: `TestWhisperDefaultsAndOverride` + 3 validation-failure tests. **Real ffmpeg** `-tags=integration` `TestWhisperExtractAudioRealFFmpeg` (generates a sine-audio fixture, extracts, ffprobes 16000 Hz / mono). `make ci` + `-tags=integration -race ./internal/...` green.)

# P14 — Simple Crypto Donations

> Implemented in the `core-donations` slice: `internal/donation` (service +
> `networks.go` validators + `verify.go` EIP-191), `internal/httpapi/donations.go`,
> migration `0063_donation_addresses`, sqlc `donation_addresses.sql`, and the
> `donations` OpenAPI tag. NON-CUSTODIAL and display-only by design — there is no
> balance/payment/payout column or code anywhere.

- [x] Add user/channel donation address fields. (`donation_addresses` table, migration 0063: `owner_id` FK users CASCADE, optional `channel_id` FK channels CASCADE (NULL = account-level), `network`, `address`, `label`, `verified`, challenge fields, `created_at`. A COALESCE-based unique index makes `(owner, channel-scope, network, address)` unique even for NULL channel_ids — verified by `TestDonationAddressQueriesPersist` against real PostgreSQL, incl. the account-level duplicate → unique violation. CRUD at `POST`/`GET`/`DELETE /api/v1/me/donation-addresses[/:id]` (requireAuth); a channel-scoped add authorises the caller owns the channel (`GetChannelByID` → `ErrForbidden`/`ErrChannelNotFound`).)
- [x] Support address type/network metadata. (Curated closed set `bitcoin|ethereum|litecoin|monero` — a DB CHECK backstop plus per-network address-format regexes in `donation.ValidAddress` (`networks.go`); an unknown network or malformed address → `422` with field errors. Tabled in `TestValidAddressPerNetwork` (P2PKH/P2SH/bech32 btc, checksummed+lowercase eth, ltc legacy+bech32, 95-char monero, cross-network paste rejects).)
- [x] Add signed challenge flow to verify address ownership where feasible. (`POST /api/v1/me/donation-addresses/:id/challenge` → `{message, expires_at}` — a nonce + instance + network + address message, 10-min expiry, nonce is a PUBLIC random challenge (never a key). `POST .../verify {signature}` verifies for **ethereum** (EIP-191 `personal_sign`: keccak256 prefix hash → secp256k1 pubkey recovery via the minimal vetted `github.com/decred/dcrd/dcrec/secp256k1/v4` lib, chosen over the heavyweight go-ethereum since we need only recovery + keccak256; address = last 20 bytes of keccak256(pubkey), case-insensitive compare). **bitcoin** (BIP-137) is intentionally deferred and **monero/litecoin** have no practical message-signing standard, so `SupportsVerification` is ethereum-only and those networks' challenge/verify return `501 not_implemented` — honest, never faked crypto. Tested: eth round-trip (in-test keygen → sign → verify → verified) at both service and HTTP layers, wrong-signer → `ErrSignatureMismatch`/`422`, malformed sig → `ErrBadSignature`, stale-nonce → mismatch, 10-min expiry via injected clock → `ErrChallengeExpired`, no-challenge → `409`, bitcoin challenge+verify → `501`.)
- [x] Display verified/unverified status via API. (Public reads `GET /api/v1/users/:id/donation-addresses` (account-level only) and `GET /api/v1/channels/:handle/donation-addresses` expose `verified` per address — only what the owner added — and NEVER the internal nonce/expiry (`donationAddressView` omits them). `TestDonationAddressCRUDAndPublicRead` + `TestDonationAddressChannelScopeAndPublicChannelRead` assert account vs channel scoping and that a channel-scoped address does not leak onto the account read.)
- [x] Do not custody funds. (Nothing in the schema, service, or API references a balance, amount, transaction, invoice, or settlement; documented in the migration header, the `donation` package doc, the openapi descriptions, and README.)
- [x] Do not implement premium subscriptions, payouts, balances, escrow, or payment processing. (None added — the slice is address display + optional ownership proof only.)
- [x] Add tests for wallet validation and verification state. (`internal/donation/service_test.go` — validation tables + verify round-trip/expiry/nonce-mismatch; `internal/httpapi/donations_test.go` — CRUD, public reads, channel scope, eth verify over HTTP, btc `501`, and a no-secrets audit assertion (verify emits `content.donation.verify` with no denylisted key, signature never in logs); `internal/store/donation_addresses_integration_test.go` — real-DB queries + unique index + cascade. All green under `make ci` and `-tags=integration -race`.)

# P15 — Security Hardening

- [x] Add SSRF protection package/policy for URL imports and link previews. (`internal/urlsafety`: `ValidateURL` (http/https only, host required, no userinfo, literal-IP range check), `IsBlockedIP` (fail-closed: loopback/unspecified/link-local/multicast via !GlobalUnicast, RFC1918+ULA via IsPrivate, CGNAT 100.64/10, IPv4-mapped), and `NewClient(timeout)` — an `http.Client` whose `net.Dialer.Control` re-checks every **resolved** candidate IP at DIAL time, so it defeats DNS-rebinding and blocks redirects that land internally; ignores proxy env; caps redirects; re-validates each redirect target's scheme. Per `.ralph/specs/security.md` SSRF rules. This is the reusable guard the URL-import (P6.1) + link-preview (P11) + federation-fetch (P10) slices must route outbound fetches through. Tested: accept/reject URL tables, `IsBlockedIP` table (incl. cloud-metadata 169.254.169.254, IPv6 ULA/loopback, CGNAT, IPv4-mapped), a live `httptest` loopback fetch that the client refuses, and `control` private/public. Callers still bound response body size themselves. **Refactored to a `Guard` type** (zero value = the secure default; `Guard{AllowPrivate:true}` relaxes ONLY the private/loopback IP checks — scheme/host/userinfo validation and fail-closed-on-unparseable still apply). `AllowPrivate` is a DEV/TEST escape hatch wired to config `HTTP_IMPORT_ALLOW_PRIVATE_URLS` (default false; loud boot warning; NEVER in production) so backed e2e can import from a loopback/compose-network origin (a public origin isn't reachable in CI). Tested: `TestGuardAllowPrivate` (allows loopback validate+dial, still rejects ftp + unparseable + secure-default still blocks) + `TestImportVideoAllowPrivateConfig` (config→guard→real client imports a literal-127.0.0.1 origin → published).)
- [x] Add upload file type allowlist. (original-file upload accepts only known video-container extensions — mp4/m4v/mov/webm/mkv/avi/ogv/ogg/mpg/mpeg/ts/flv/wmv/3gp; others → 415. See `internal/video.acceptedVideoExts`.)
- [x] Add malware scan hooks. (`video.Scanner` interface (mirrors `Prober`/`Thumbnailer`), run by `Process` BEFORE probe/publish: a not-clean result OR a scan error keeps the video out of `published` (fail-closed → `failed` state) and skips the probe. Adapter `internal/media.ClamAV` streams the stored original to a clamd daemon over INSTREAM (`OK`→clean, `FOUND`→infected, other→error). Config `MALWARE_SCAN_ENABLED` + `CLAMAV_ADDR` (validated: addr required when enabled; `.env.example` + compose passthrough); wired via `video.WithScanner(media.NewClamAV(...))` in cmd/api when enabled (operator provides clamd). Tested: `video` Process (clean→published, infected→failed with prober-must-not-run, scan-error→failed) + `media` ClamAV against a fake TCP clamd (OK/FOUND/unexpected-response/dial-failure).)
- [x] Add path traversal protections for local storage. (`internal/storage.Local.resolve` rejects empty/NUL-bearing/absolute/any-`..`-segment keys up front, then a belt-and-braces `filepath.Rel` check guarantees the resolved path stays under the root; applied uniformly by Put/Open/Delete/Exists/Path. Tested: `local_test.go` — `TestRejectsTraversalAndInvalidKeys` (table of `..`/absolute/NUL keys → `ErrInvalidKey`), `TestTraversalCannotEscapeRoot` (a `../outside.txt` Put cannot clobber a sentinel outside root), `TestLocalPathResolvesAndRejects`.)
- [x] Add CORS tests. (`internal/httpapi/cors_test.go` asserts the browser cross-origin policy matches the configured allow-list: an allowed `Origin` is echoed in `Access-Control-Allow-Origin`; a disallowed origin gets no CORS grant; a preflight `OPTIONS` from the allowed origin returns 204 with `Access-Control-Allow-Methods` including POST. Locks the CORS contract so a widened/dropped policy flips a green check.)
- [x] Add rate-limit tests. (Covered: `internal/ratelimit/ratelimit_test.go` (fixed-window limiter logic) + `redis_integration_test.go` (real Redis counter) + `internal/httpapi/ratelimit_test.go` + `auth_ratelimit_test.go` (429 + `X-RateLimit-*` headers, per-IP auth limiter via the middleware). Run under `make ci` / backend-integration.)
- [x] Add JWT key rotation plan or documented defer. (`.ralph/specs/security.md` "JWT signing-key rotation": documents the current HS256/single-`JWT_SECRET` scheme (alg-pinned verify; refresh is opaque DB-backed, independent of the secret), a rotation procedure available TODAY with bounded disruption (rotate the secret → outstanding access tokens 401 once → clients transparently `/auth/refresh` with their unaffected refresh token → new access token), and an explicit DEFERRAL of zero-downtime `kid`-keyring rotation with its trigger to implement + the test it must ship with.)
- [x] Add OAuth redirect validation. (Two layers, both tested: (1) the OAuth redirect URI sent to the provider is ALWAYS derived server-side — `PUBLIC_BASE_URL + "/api/v1/auth/oauth/<provider>/callback"` — never read from request params, and config REQUIRES `PUBLIC_BASE_URL` whenever any provider is on; asserted end-to-end at the fake provider's token endpoint. (2) the post-login `return_to` is accepted only as a same-origin RELATIVE path (`safeReturnPath`: must start `/`, never `//`, no `\`, no scheme/host — else 422 at begin) and travels inside the HMAC-signed state cookie, so the callback can only redirect within the instance (cookie tamper → 400). See the P4 OAuth abstraction entry for the full test matrix.)
- [x] Add secure headers. (`internal/httpapi/secure_headers.go` — a middleware mounted right after `Recover` (so even recovered 5xx carry them) setting on every response: `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `X-Frame-Options: DENY`, `Referrer-Policy: strict-origin-when-cross-origin`, `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy: same-origin`, `X-Permitted-Cross-Domain-Policies: none`, plus `Strict-Transport-Security` (2y, includeSubDomains) **in production only** (`cfg.Environment == "production"`; omitted on plain-HTTP localhost). No CSP is set — this service serves JSON/media, not HTML, so a page CSP belongs to `vidra-user`; CORS stays with the dedicated CORS middleware. Tested: `TestSecureHeadersPresent` (all base headers, HSTS absent outside prod) + `TestSecureHeadersHSTSInProduction` (HSTS present + base headers still apply). No route/contract change.)
- [x] Add audit logging for sensitive actions (typed audit events, no secrets; see P17.2 and `.ralph/specs/observability.md`). (The `s.audit` helper (`internal/httpapi/auth.go`) emits a typed, secret-free audit event via slog AND persists it best-effort via `audit.Service`. Wired across the sensitive actions observability.md §75-78 enumerates: login success/failure, logout + logout-all, password-reset request/complete, email-verify request/confirm, account deactivate, registration request/approve/reject, admin role/user update, moderation report-resolve + video block/unblock, auth rate-limit — plus **destructive content deletion** (video + channel delete, this slice: `ActionVideoDelete`/`ActionChannelDelete`). Each is test-covered (`audit_test.go`, `audit_content_test.go`) per the observability.md line-83 rule (capture slog, assert the event). No denylisted keys leak (`TestNoSensitiveLogKeys`).)
- [x] Enforce no-secrets-in-logs via the secrets-in-logs guard test (P17.2). (`TestNoSensitiveLogKeys` in `internal/observability/logging_guard_test.go`, runs under `make ci`.)
- [x] Add fuzz tests for URL parsing. (`FuzzValidateURL` in `internal/urlsafety/urlsafety_test.go` — asserts `ValidateURL` never panics and that any accepted URL is http/https with a host and no userinfo; seed corpus runs under `make ci`, and a 10s `go test -fuzz` pass (270k+ execs) found no crash. Metadata/ActivityPub-parsing fuzz targets remain for those slices.)
- [x] Add fuzz tests for metadata parsing. (`internal/media/fuzz_test.go` `FuzzParseFFProbe` over `parseFFProbe([]byte)` (the ffprobe-output parser; a crafted upload can steer ffprobe's JSON) — asserts no panic on arbitrary bytes and that any returned Metadata is non-negative + duration below the cap. Fuzzing surfaced a float→int overflow: a huge `format.duration` (e.g. `1e19`) rounded to a NEGATIVE `DurationSeconds`; hardened `parseFFProbe` with `maxProbeDurationSeconds` (~100 days) so absurd/overflowing durations are dropped. Seed corpus runs under `make ci`; short `go test -fuzz` (~900k execs) found no crash. Completes the P15 fuzz targets — see also the URL-parsing + ActivityPub/httpsig fuzz above.)
- [x] Add fuzz tests for ActivityPub parsing when implemented. (`internal/httpsig/fuzz_test.go` — `FuzzParseSignatureHeader` (the hand-written, attacker-controlled Signature-header parser on the inbound federation path) + `FuzzVerify` (the full inbound verification path with an arbitrary Signature header + body). Both assert no panic on any input; the parser also must never return a non-error empty map. Seed corpus runs under `make ci`; short `go test -fuzz` passes (~1M execs each) found no crash. The JSON-based AP envelope/actor parsing uses `encoding/json` (panic-safe by construction). Remaining: metadata-parsing fuzz — see below.)

# P16 — Testing Strategy

- [x] Add unit test pattern and examples. (pattern documented in `.ralph/specs/testing.md` §Layers — fakes/httptest, no external deps; 95 `*_test.go` files across `internal/` follow it)
- [x] Add integration test pattern with PostgreSQL and Redis. (`-tags=integration`, self-skipping when a dependency is absent; 12+ files across store/cache/ratelimit/storage/media/federation/httpapi/transcode/profileimage/cmd/api; `make test-integration`; documented in testing.md + AGENT.md)
- [x] Add smoke test for API startup. (`cmd/api/smoke_integration_test.go` `TestAPIStartupSmoke` (`-tags=integration`; self-skips without `DATABASE_URL`/`REDIS_URL`): `go build`s the REAL api binary and exec's it against the live PostgreSQL + Redis on a reserved loopback port (tempdir `STORAGE_LOCAL_ROOT`), proving the full production wiring end to end — config load, store/cache connections, service construction, route registration — not the fakes unit tests use. Polls `/readyz` until 200 (asserting `postgres`+`redis` components both `ok`; a boot crash surfaces immediately with captured logs instead of a poll timeout), asserts `/healthz` → `{status:ok}` and `/version` → `{name:vidra, version, go}`, then SIGTERMs and requires a clean exit 0 with "shutdown complete" in the logs. Race-safe (mutex'd log sink; exit result consumed exactly once; killed on test failure so nothing leaks). Runs in `make test-integration` / the backend-integration workflow (`./...` includes `cmd/api`); passed locally in ~4.5s under `-race` against the compose PG/Redis.)
- [x] Add Postman collection and environment for live DB tests. (core-sweep, 2026-07-03. Curated Postman v2.1 collection + environment under `docs/postman/`, generated from `api/openapi.yaml` by the zero-dep `docs/postman/generate.mjs` (regen: `make postman`). Covers System probes (healthz/readyz/version/nodeinfo/instance) + Auth happy/error (register 201→token capture, register 422, login 200, login 401, `/auth/me` 200) + `admin/system` (200-or-403, asserts `rate_limits` on 200), with real status/shape assertions + token chaining. The generator fails on any path drift vs the contract. NOT a required gate (docs/manual QA). Verified with `newman run` against the live compose backend: 11 requests / 18 assertions / 0 failures. Full every-endpoint conversion documented via `npx openapi-to-postmanv2`.)
- [x] Add fuzz test target list. (targets listed in testing.md §Layers and implemented — `FuzzValidateURL` (urlsafety), `FuzzParseFFProbe` (media), `FuzzParseSignatureHeader`/`FuzzVerify` (httpsig); seed corpora run under `make ci` — see P15)
- [x] Add benchmark target list. (core-sweep, 2026-07-03. `testing.B` benches on the read-hot paths: `internal/auth/bench_test.go` `BenchmarkTokenParse`/`BenchmarkTokenIssue` (JWT HS256 verify/mint — every authenticated request), `internal/media/bench_test.go` `BenchmarkRenderMasterPlaylist`/`BenchmarkPlanHLSLadder` (pure HLS master-playlist render — every proxied master request), and integration-tagged `internal/store/bench_integration_test.go` `BenchmarkListPublicVideosSorted` (public feed query) + `BenchmarkSearchPublicVideos` (pg_trgm trigram title search) against live PG over a 500-video seeded corpus (self-skip without DATABASE_URL). Run via `go test -run='^$' -bench=.` (add `-tags=integration` + DATABASE_URL for the store benches). NOT wired into `make ci` (keeps the gate fast) — exercised by the bench-fuzz workflow. Verified locally: all six benchmarks execute green.)
- [x] Add tiny media fixtures. (generated, not committed: a 2s 320x240 ffmpeg testsrc fixture built at test time powers `TestHLSTranscoderRealVideo`, `TestHLSPipelineEndToEnd`, and the ffprobe/thumbnail integration tests)
- [x] Add testcontainers or Compose-based test runner. (Compose-based: `docker compose up` postgres/redis(/minio) + `make test-integration`; CI mirrors it with service containers in backend-integration.yml)
- [x] Add CI jobs for unit tests. (`.github/workflows/backend-ci.yml` — the canonical `make ci` gate (fmt-check+vet+openapi-verify+sqlc-verify+test-race) with PG/Redis service containers)
- [x] Add CI jobs for integration tests. (`.github/workflows/backend-integration.yml` — `make test-integration` against real postgres:16 + redis:7 + ffmpeg, fresh-DB migrations applied first)
- [x] Add CI jobs for smoke tests. (the API startup smoke `TestAPIStartupSmoke` rides backend-integration.yml — its `./...` includes `cmd/api`; no separate workflow needed)
- [x] Add scheduled or manual fuzz/benchmark workflows. (core-sweep, 2026-07-03. `.github/workflows/bench-fuzz.yml` — `workflow_dispatch` (manual, with a `fuzztime` input) + weekly `schedule` (Mon 06:00 UTC), NEVER on push/PR so the required `make ci` gate stays fast (ci-guard clean: no continue-on-error; backend-ci.yml untouched). Two jobs: `bench` runs `go test -tags=integration -run='^$' -bench=. -benchmem ./...` against a fresh-migrated postgres:16 + redis:7 service pair and uploads `bench-results.txt`; `fuzz` runs a short `go test -fuzz` (default 60s) over each existing target — `FuzzValidateURL` (urlsafety), `FuzzParseSignatureHeader`/`FuzzVerify` (httpsig), `FuzzParseFFProbe` (media) — collecting all targets before failing and uploading any `testdata/fuzz` reproducers on a crash. YAML validated; fuzz targets smoke-run locally.)
- [x] Document when Ralph should run focused vs full test suites. (testing.md §"How to run" + `.ralph/AGENT.md`: `make ci` = the fast canonical gate (fmt-check+vet+openapi-verify+sqlc-verify+test-race), focused `go test ./internal/<pkg>/...` during a slice, `make test-integration` for the dependency-gated `-tags=integration` suite, and `make bench`/the bench-fuzz workflow for the slow benches/fuzz kept out of `make ci`.)

# P17 — Observability and Operations

> Follow `.ralph/specs/observability.md`. Logging/tracing ship with the code they
> describe, not in a later phase.

## P17.1 Developer-friendly logging

- [x] Add structured logs (slog JSON to stdout).
- [x] Add request IDs (Echo RequestID + per-request slog line).
- [x] Accept + echo the `X-Correlation-ID` header and log it as `correlation_id` (the OTel-off correlation contract that pairs with `vidra-user`). (`internal/httpapi/correlation.go` middleware, mounted after `RequestID` and before the request logger: reads inbound `X-Correlation-ID`, mints from the request id when absent, **sanitises** it (untrusted input — URL-safe token chars only, ≤128, so no CR/LF/log injection), echoes it on the response, and threads it through the request context so the request-log line carries `correlation_id`. Tested: echo inbound, mint-when-absent (== request id), sanitise unsafe input, fall back to mint when fully unsafe, and `correlation_id` present in the request log. The observability guards pass over the new code.)
- [x] Centralize logger construction in `internal/observability` and inject it. (`observability.NewLogger(w, level, format)` in `internal/observability/logger.go` is the single logger constructor — parses level (debug|info|warn|error) + format (json|text), returns a `*slog.Logger`, errors on bad input (`ParseLevel` exported for reuse). `cmd/api` builds it from config after `config.Load()`, `slog.SetDefault`s it, and injects it into the server via `httpapi.WithLogger`; a bootstrap Info/JSON logger covers pre-config-load errors. Tested: level filtering, json/text formats, empty→info/json defaults, invalid level/format rejected, `ParseLevel` table.)
- [x] Add `LOG_LEVEL` and `LOG_FORMAT` (json/text) config + `.env.example` + tests. (`config.LogLevel`/`LogFormat`, default info/json, lowercased on load, validated in `validate()` (unknown value → error). `.env.example` documents both in a logging section. Tested: defaults, override+case-normalisation, invalid level/format rejected.)
- [ ] Propagate the request-scoped logger (request_id/trace_id) through service and store layers via `context.Context`.

## P17.2 Security-friendly logging

- [~] Add a redaction helper + denylist of sensitive field names in `internal/observability`; route struct/config logging through it (never log `cfg` whole). (Denylist DONE + now enforced: `IsSensitiveKey`/`sensitiveKeys` in `audit.go` is the canonical list, and the secrets-in-logs guard below fails the build on any denylisted slog key. `cfg` is never logged whole today — `cmd/api` logs only individual non-sensitive fields. A value-scrubbing `Redact(...)` helper for the rare "must log a struct" case remains optional.)
- [x] Add the banned-logging guard test (`TestNoForbiddenLogging`): no `fmt.Print*`/`log.Print*`/`println` diagnostics outside `main`/tests. (`internal/observability/logging_guard_test.go` — an AST guard that parses every non-test, non-`package main` file in the module and fails on `fmt.Print*`, `log.Print*`/`Fatal*`/`Panic*`, or the `println`/`print` builtins (`fmt.Fprint*`/`Sprint*`/`Errorf` allowed). Runs under `make ci` via `test-race`; being an ordinary Go test IS the enforcement — no Makefile change. Green on the current tree; a negative check confirmed it catches a planted `fmt.Println`.)
- [x] Add the secrets-in-logs guard test: fail when a denylisted key is used as an slog/span/metric key. (`TestNoSensitiveLogKeys` in the same file — flags a denylisted `IsSensitiveKey` name used as a structured-log key in three idioms: inline in an slog key/value call (`Debug/Info/Warn/Error[Context]`, `Log`, top-level `slog.*` — key positions computed per signature), at an even index of a `[]any{}`/`[]interface{}{}` args slice (the slog args-builder idiom used by `server.go`/`audit.go`), and as an slog attribute-constructor key (`slog.String("token", …)`). Green on the current tree; a negative check confirmed it catches planted inline/slice/attr-ctor keys.)
- [x] Implement typed audit events for auth/admin/moderation actions (durable, no secrets); add per-action audit tests asserting no denylisted field. (See P15.) (Typed `observability.AuditEvent` + `Action*`/`Result*` constants emitted from the `s.audit` choke point across auth/registration/moderation/admin handlers; now ALSO persisted durably to the `audit_log` table (migration 0029, `internal/audit`) and exposed at `GET /admin/audit-log` — see the admin-audit-log item in P9. No denylisted field can reach a slog line (the `TestNoSensitiveLogKeys` guard) and audit rows carry only action/result/actor_id/reason/request_id; `audit_test.go` asserts no denylisted key + password never logged.)

## P17.3 OpenTelemetry (traces + metrics)

- [x] Add OTel Go SDK setup + graceful shutdown in `internal/observability`, wired in `cmd/api` (and worker), no-op when disabled. (`observability.SetupTracing(ctx, TracingConfig)` (`internal/observability/otel.go`): when disabled returns a no-op shutdown (global stays the no-op provider, zero cost); when enabled builds an OTLP trace exporter (grpc or http/protobuf, `WithInsecure`, lazy-connect so a down collector never blocks startup), a `sdktrace` provider with a `service.name`/`service.version` resource, sets it global + the W3C TraceContext+Baggage propagator, and returns `tp.Shutdown`. `cmd/api` calls it and defers shutdown. Tested: disabled→no-op, unknown-protocol→error, enabled→constructs+shutdown.)
- [x] Add config `OTEL_ENABLED`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT`, `OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL`, `OTEL_SERVICE_NAME` + `.env.example` + validation. (`config.OTelEnabled`/`OTelExporterEndpoint`/`OTelExporterProtocol` (grpc default) /`OTelServiceName` (default `vidra-core`); validated when enabled: protocol ∈ {grpc, http/protobuf}, endpoint required. `.env.example` documents all four. Tested: defaults, enabled-valid, missing-endpoint→error, bad-protocol→error. `METRICS_ENABLED` lands with the metrics slice below.)
- [x] Instrument HTTP (otelecho), datastore (pgx/Redis spans), and outbound HTTP calls. (core-observability slice, 2026-07-03. HTTP DONE (otelecho). **Datastore DONE**: `store.WithTracing()` installs a pgx `QueryTracer` (`postgres.query` client span per query; args NEVER recorded — no secrets) and `cache.WithTracing()` adds a go-redis `Hook` (`redis.<CMD>` span, command verb only). **Outbound DONE**: `observability.OTelHTTPTransport` (otelhttp) is installed via a new `urlsafety.SetTransportWrapper` seam, so EVERY SSRF-guarded client (federation/import/whisper/atproto/link-preview) gets a client span — the wrapper decorates the guarded transport, so the dial-time SSRF guard still runs beneath it. All three install ONLY when `OTEL_ENABLED` (cmd/api), so zero cost off. Tested: `TestQuerySpansEmitted` (real-PG span recorder), `TestOTelHTTPTransportInjectsTraceparent`.)
- [x] Accept inbound W3C `traceparent` from `vidra-user`; inject context on outbound calls. (Inbound DONE (SetupTracing propagator + otelecho). **Outbound injection DONE**: otelhttp injects the active trace context (W3C `traceparent`) onto outbound requests via the global propagator; `TestOTelHTTPTransportInjectsTraceparent` asserts a request through the SSRF-guarded client carries a well-formed `traceparent`, and `TestNoWrapperNoInjection` asserts the zero-cost off default injects nothing.)
- [x] Export RED metrics with bounded label cardinality (no IDs/tokens/raw URLs as labels); gate the metrics surface behind `METRICS_ENABLED` and document any route in `api/openapi.yaml`. (core-observability slice. `internal/observability/metrics.go` — prometheus/client_golang (justified in the file doc: de-facto standard, produces the Prometheus text format + a ready promhttp handler; a pull/scrape endpoint is the conventional ops surface vs an always-on OTLP push; tracing keeps using the OTel SDK). New `config.MetricsEnabled` (`METRICS_ENABLED`, default false). `GET /metrics` serves `vidra_http_requests_total` + `vidra_http_request_duration_seconds` labelled by method + Echo route TEMPLATE (`c.Path()`, never the raw URL/id) + status class (2xx/3xx/4xx/5xx) — BOUNDED cardinality — plus a `vidra_queue_depth{queue,state}` gauge pulled from the jobs snapshot at scrape time. Recording rides the request-logger choke point (final error-handler-resolved status) gated by `s.metrics != nil`, mirroring how otelecho is gated by `OTEL_ENABLED`. `/metrics` is a root ops surface INTENTIONALLY EXCLUDED from openapi.yaml (like the AP/dev routes; testConfig has MetricsEnabled=false so the drift guard never sees it) — documented in README + observability.md + docs/operations.md, with a "network-scope it" warning. Tested: `metrics_test.go` (RED counters/histograms + bounded labels + queue gauge + source-error-swallowed), `TestMetricsEndpointGating` (404 off / 200 on + records healthz).)
- [x] Stamp `trace_id`/`span_id` into slog output when OTel is enabled. (The request logger adds `trace_id`/`span_id` from `trace.SpanContextFromContext` whenever a span is active (`HasTraceID()`), pairing with the OTel-off `correlation_id`. Tested: `TestRequestLogIncludesTraceID` injects a span context and asserts both fields on the `request` line.)
- [x] Add optional Docker Compose profile for a local OTel Collector / Jaeger. (core-observability slice. `docker-compose.yml` profile `otel`: `otel-collector` (otel/opentelemetry-collector-contrib) receiving OTLP on 4317/4318 → `jaeger` all-in-one (UI 16686); config at `deploy/otel-collector.yaml`. Optional, NOT required by any test; `docker compose --profile otel config` validated. Documented in the compose header + README + docs/operations.md.)

## P17.4 Operations

- [x] Add health/readiness for dependencies. (`GET /readyz` pings postgres + redis and 503s when either is degraded; `GET /healthz` liveness; the same `componentHealth` helper feeds `GET /admin/system`. Tested in `health_test.go` + the startup smoke. External deps (clamd/whisper/media-server) are optional-by-config and not part of core readiness by design.)
- [x] Add worker status reporting. (core-observability slice. `GET /api/v1/admin/jobs` (admin-only) aggregates every durable queue — transcode_jobs, federation_deliveries, import_jobs, caption_jobs, account_exports, upload_sessions — into `{pending, running, done, failed, oldest_pending_age_seconds}`. No separate heartbeat store is needed: a healthy worker keeps pending/oldest_pending_age low; a stuck worker shows a rising oldest_pending_age. `internal/jobstatus` service over new `job_status.sql` sqlc aggregates. This is the frontend jobs-page contract.)
- [x] Add job retry/dead-letter visibility. (Same `GET /api/v1/admin/jobs`: dead-lettered rows surface as the per-queue `failed` count plus a merged, newest-first `recent_failures` list carrying only id/error/attempts — NEVER a source/inbox URL, storage key, or argument. Tested: `jobstatus_test.go` (normalise/merge/sort/cap + upload-session state remap + Depths), `TestAdminJobsSnapshot` (admin 200 + shape + non-admin 403 + anon 401), `jobstatus_integration_test.go` (queries valid against the live schema).)
- [x] Add admin-facing system status endpoint. (`GET /api/v1/admin/system` (requireRole admin) → an operational snapshot: `software` (name/version/commit/build_date/go_version from `internal/version`), `environment`, `uptime_seconds` (from a `Server.startedAt` stamped at `New()`), an overall `status` (ok|degraded), and per-dependency `components` (postgres/redis). Reuses a new shared `Server.componentHealth(ctx)` helper (extracted from `handleReady`, so readiness + status stay in lock-step); a nil Pinger reports `not_configured`. Always **200** (even when degraded) so the admin dashboard can render the degraded state, unlike `/readyz` which 503s. Reports only operational metadata — no secrets/PII. `internal/httpapi/admin_system.go`; registered gated on `s.authsvc != nil` (auth guards it) + in `fullRouteOptions` so the drift guard enforces it. openapi documents the route + `SystemStatus` schema (drift guard green). Tested: `TestSystemStatus` (admin 200 with vidra/go_version/env=test/status=ok/components present → non-admin 403 → anon 401). **This unblocks the `vidra-user` P10 system-status page.**)
- [x] Add backup/restore docs for PostgreSQL, media storage, and Redis assumptions. (core-observability slice. New `docs/operations.md`: pg_dump/pg_restore (custom format), media backup PER backend (S3 versioning/lifecycle, local volume snapshot, IPFS pinset), Redis = ephemeral cache (no backup), a restore-drill cadence, and the observability surfaces. Links the meta-repo `deploy/README.md`.)
- [x] Add production deployment notes. (core-observability slice. `docs/operations.md` "Production deployment notes" covers the fail-secure boot rules (`VIDRA_ENV=production` refuses dev JWT secret + dev mail capture, requires FEDERATION_KEY_KEK/MFA_KEY_KEK, Secure cookies, keep the SSRF guard on) and health/readiness probes, linking the reference single-host deployment in `deploy/README.md`.)

# P18 — PeerTube Import and Migration

> Import an existing PeerTube instance (its PostgreSQL DB + media storage) into
> Vidra. Follow `.ralph/specs/peertube-import.md`. Read-only on the source;
> idempotent, resumable, dry-runnable, admin-only, audited. Depends on the data
> models from P4–P10 existing; build incrementally as those land.

## P18.1 Preflight and source connection

- [x] Add read-only source-DB config (DSN) and source-storage config (local/S3) to `internal/config` + `.env.example` (off by default; source creds are secrets, never committed/logged). (core-peertube-import slice. `PEERTUBE_IMPORT_ENABLED` + `PEERTUBE_SOURCE_DATABASE_URL` (secret) + `PEERTUBE_SOURCE_STORAGE_*` (local/S3; S3 keys secret) + `PEERTUBE_IMPORT_CONFLICT_POLICY` in `internal/config`, validated (`validatePeerTubeImport`), documented in `.env.example`. Denylist extended with `source_dsn`/`source_database_url`/`peertube_source_dsn`/`source_secret_key`/`source_access_key`. `TestPeerTubeImportConfig`.)
- [x] Detect PeerTube schema/version on preflight; pin supported version range in `.ralph/specs/peertube-reference.md`; refuse unverified versions without `--force`. (`Source.DetectVersion` reads `application.migrationVersion`; `ClassifyVersion`/`IsSupported`/`VersionError` (range **700–900** ≈ PT 5.x–6.x, pinned in peertube-reference.md). `Importer.Preflight` refuses outside the range unless `Force`; the admin path NEVER self-forces (agents must not). `TestClassifyVersion`, `TestPeerTubeImportPreflightVersionGate` (refuses v500, proceeds with force).)
- [x] Verify source DB reachability, storage reachability, and free disk space before any write. (`Preflight` pings source+dest; free-disk check (`freeDiskBytes`, unix `statfs`) against a local destination vs the summed source video sizes.)

## P18.2 Mapping ledger and dry-run

- [x] Fill in the entity mapping ledger (PeerTube entity → Vidra model → status → notes) per the spec. (Filled table in `.ralph/specs/peertube-import.md`: user/channel/video/videoFile/thumbnail/caption/comment/playlist/tag/follow → supported/partial; HLS/moderation/prefs → deferred.)
- [x] Implement a durable import ledger mapping source UUID/id → Vidra id with per-row status (enables idempotency + resume). (Migration **0067** `peertube_import_ledger` `(entity_kind, source_id) → vidra_id + status`; sqlc `UpsertImportLedgerEntry`/`GetImportLedgerEntry`; each entity insert + its ledger upsert share ONE transaction. `TestPeerTubeImportEndToEnd` proves idempotency: re-run imports 0, row counts unchanged.)
- [x] Implement `--dry-run`: report counts, mapping plan, conflicts, and unsupported/partial entities; write nothing. (`Importer.Plan` → `Report` (per-kind `planned`, `conflicts`, `deferred`); writes nothing — `TestPeerTubeImportEndToEnd` asserts 0 users/ledger rows after a plan. CLI `--dry-run`; admin `mode=dry_run`.)

## P18.3 Entity import (incremental, idempotent)

- [x] Import users/accounts/actors, including identity; bcrypt password-hash strategy (keep if compatible, else disable + force reset). Never log hashes. (`importUsers`: local users only (remote excluded); bcrypt hash carried verbatim — self-describing cost verifies under Vidra bcrypt (`TestPeerTubeImportEndToEnd` verifies the carried hash against the plaintext); role 0/1/2 → admin/mod/user; actor keypair → `account_actor_keys` (private sealed under KEK or raw in dev). No hash/key logged — asserted.)
- [x] Import channels (+ ActivityPub actor handles/keypairs for federation continuity; see P10). (`importChannels`: handle = channel actor `preferredUsername`, owner via `account.userId`; keypair → `channel_actor_keys`.)
- [x] Import videos + `videoFile`/`videoStreamingPlaylist` (HLS) + thumbnails + captions, with media copy/re-probe (streaming, checksummed, resumable). (`importVideos`: metadata (privacy/state/category/licence/language/duration) + highest-res web file → `web-videos/`, thumbnail → `thumbnails/`, captions → `captions/` via `copyMedia` (streaming + sha-256, overwrite-idempotent, size-capped, ext-allowlisted). HLS renditions **deferred** — regenerate via Vidra transcoding (documented). `TestPeerTubeImportEndToEnd` asserts copied bytes match source.)
- [x] Import comments (threaded), playlists + elements, tags/categories/metadata. (`importComments` (local, non-deleted, `inReplyToCommentId` → `parent_id`), `importPlaylists` (REGULAR only, items via numeric→uuid resolve), tags → `video_tags`, category/licence as text (0025). Tested.)
- [x] Import follows/subscriptions; moderation data (blacklists/blocklists/abuse) where in scope, else mark `deferred`. (`importFollows`: accepted local→local → `channel_follows`. Moderation state marked **deferred** in the mapping ledger + report `deferred`.)
- [x] Apply the configured conflict policy (skip|rename|merge|fail) for username/handle/email/slug collisions. (`decideUser`/`importOneChannel` + `dedupeUsername/Handle/Email`; `TestPeerTubeImportConflictPolicies` proves skip keeps one alice, rename imports `alice-2`. `fail` aborts with `ErrConflictFail`.)

## P18.4 Surface, safety, tests, docs

- [x] Add the `cmd/peertube-import` CLI (source DSN, storage, conflict policy, `--dry-run`, `--resume`). (`cmd/peertube-import/main.go`: `--source-dsn`, `--source-storage`/`--source-local-root`/`--source-s3-*`, `--conflict-policy`, `--dry-run`, `--resume`, `--force` (human-only), `--no-media`; dest DB/storage/KEK from the server env; prints the JSON report.)

# Messaging v2

> Frontend-messenger enablement contract deltas against the EXISTING messaging
> pillar (attachments/read-state/link-previews/tombstones already ship, migration
> 0064). Spec: `.ralph/specs/messaging-v2.md`. Contract-first (openapi.yaml in the
> same commit as the handler), additive/backward-compatible only, `make ci` green +
> pushed before a box ticks. Binding invariants: never log message plaintext or
> attachment filenames (ids only); encrypted conversations stay opaque (attachment
> upload on encrypted remains 422); participant gating stays 404-shaped.

## P-MSG2.C1 Attachment image dimensions (D1) — DONE 2026-07-07
- [x] Migration `dm_attachment_dimensions`: `message_attachments` + nullable `width`/`height` (CHECK > 0); down migration drops them. (`migrations/0068_dm_attachment_dimensions.{up,down}.sql`; up+down verified reversible against real PG.)
- [x] `UploadAttachment`: `image.DecodeConfig` probe for kind=image via TeeReader before blob write (config-only decode; jpeg/png/gif/webp registered — added `golang.org/x/image/webp` for webp); probe failure non-fatal (NULLs) logged as `attachment dimension probe failed` with attachment_id ONLY; dimensions > 20000px either side → 422 (`ErrAttachmentDimensions`); ClamAV fail-closed path unchanged and still ordered before linkability. (`internal/messaging/service.go` `UploadAttachment`, bounded `capWriter` tees the header of the single Put stream.)
- [x] Contract: `DMAttachment` + `UploadAttachmentResponse` gain optional `width`/`height` in openapi.yaml (+ upload 422 description); sqlc columns extended (`CreateMessageAttachment`/`GetMessageAttachment`/`ListAttachmentsForMessages`).
- [x] Tests: `TestDMAttachmentDimensions` (real png/jpeg/gif encode + `testdata/tiny.webp` fixture + corrupt→NULL + non-image→NULL + oversize 20001px→422) echoes on upload + list; DB-proof `TestMessagingV2AttachmentDimensionsPersist` (persisted columns + CHECK>0) green against real PG; existing 413/415/422/scan tests green; observability log-guard green. (Evidence: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration` run.)

## P-MSG2.C2 Keyset pagination `before_id` (D2) — DONE 2026-07-07
- [x] Contract: optional `before_id` (uuid) query param on `GET /conversations/{id}/messages` — mutually exclusive with `offset` (422), unknown/foreign-conversation cursor 422, malformed 422, non-participant still 404; documented in openapi.yaml (param + 422 response).
- [x] sqlc `ListMessagesBefore` with row-value keyset `(created_at, id) < (before_created_at::timestamptz, before_id::uuid)` ordered `created_at DESC, id DESC`; index extended in `migrations/0069_messages_keyset_index` (`messages_conversation_created_idx` + `e2ee_messages_recipient_idx` gain trailing `id DESC`); encrypted envelope listing gets the same cursor treatment (`ListE2EEMessagesForRecipientBefore` + `GetE2EEMessageCursor`, e2ee `ListMessagesBefore`).
- [x] Tests: DB-proof `TestMessagingV2KeysetStablePaging` (page1 → concurrent insert → `before_id` page2, no duplicate/skip; empty boundary) against real PG; handler `TestDMKeysetPagination` (both-params/malformed/unknown/foreign 422, non-participant 404, empty boundary); service `TestKeysetPagination`; encrypted `TestListMessagesBeforeKeyset`. (Evidence: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration` run.)

## P-MSG2.C6 DM attachment platform limits — Messenger parity (D6, DECIDED 2026-07-07) — DONE 2026-07-07
- [x] Record in `product-decisions.md` (§14a amendment): DM attachments do NOT count against the user storage quota (the `quota` package is intentionally NOT wired into the messaging attachment path); Vidra enforces Messenger-parity platform limits instead (per-file 100 MiB = 104,857,600 bytes; 30 attachments per message; allowlist gains office docs as kind `doc`; per-user upload rate limit 60/10m; sources in messaging-v2 RESEARCH §10). (`.ralph/specs/product-decisions.md` §14a.)
- [x] Migration: `message_attachments.kind` CHECK gains `'doc'` (`migrations/0070_dm_attachment_doc_kind.{up,down}.sql`; DROP+ADD the confirmed `message_attachments_kind_check`; up applies, a `kind='doc'` insert succeeds, and down reverts — all verified against real PG). Contract: `DMAttachment.kind`/`UploadAttachmentResponse.kind` enums gain `doc`; `SendMessageRequest.attachment_ids.maxItems: 4 → 30`; upload description/413/415 updated (100 MiB, kinds) + a documented `429` response in openapi.yaml (`make openapi-verify` + Redocly lint green).
- [x] Implementation: `MaxAttachmentBytes` → 100 MiB and `MaxAttachmentsPerMessage` → 30 (`internal/messaging/service.go`; the upload route is exempt from the JSON body limit and the service's `io.LimitReader(max+1)` bounds the stream, so no body-limit middleware change is needed). `attachmentKind` adds the six office MIME types + doc/docx/ppt/pptx/xls/xlsx filename fallbacks → kind `doc`. ClamAV scan hook UNCHANGED: still fail-closed and ordered BEFORE `CreateMessageAttachment` (proven by the fail-closed unit test asserting zero rows). Compensating control: per-USER upload rate limit via the existing `ratelimit` package — new `attachmentUploadRateLimit` middleware keyed `dm-attach:<userID>` on `POST /conversations/{id}/attachments`, wired in `cmd/api` behind `RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` (config `ATTACHMENT_UPLOAD_RATE_LIMIT_REQUESTS`=60 / `ATTACHMENT_UPLOAD_RATE_LIMIT_WINDOW`=10m, validated), 429 + `Retry-After` + `X-RateLimit-*`, fails open. Docs: `.env.example`, `README.md`.
- [x] Tests: service `TestAttachmentDocKind` (six office MIME + octet-stream/.docx fallback → `doc`; tar → 415), `TestAttachmentDocKindScannedFailClosed` (docx + infected/scan-error → `ErrAttachmentRejected`, **zero rows persisted** = scan-before-linkability; the untagged EICAR-in-docx cover), `TestAttachmentSizeCapBoundary` (injected cap: at→201, +1→413 even with a lying Size), `TestSendRejectsTooManyAttachments` (31 → `ErrInvalidAttachments`, no message row), `TestAttachmentLimitsAreDecidedValues` (locks 104857600 / 30). Handler `TestDMAttachmentDocKind` (docx+xlsx → 201 `kind=doc`, tar → 415), `TestDMSendTooManyAttachments` (31 ids → 422), `TestAttachmentUploadRateLimit` (per-user 3rd → 429 + Retry-After, other user independent) + no-op-when-unset. DB-proof `TestMessagingV2DocKindPersists` (real PG: `kind='doc'` Create/Get/List round-trip + tombstone `DeleteAttachmentsByMessage` + `kind='exe'` still CHECK-rejected). Integration `TestAttachmentEICARInDocxRejected` (real clamd, EICAR inside a real zip/docx → rejected + zero rows; self-skips without `CLAMAV_TEST_ADDR`). Existing 25 MiB-era 413/415 tests updated (messages now say 100 MiB / include doc). No filename/body in any new log line (observability guard green). (Evidence: `make ci` green + `-tags=integration` store + Redocly lint.)

## P-MSG2.C7 E2EE × attachments stance (D7 — documentation only)
- [ ] Record in `.ralph/specs/e2ee.md`: encrypted-thread attachments remain 422 in v2; future design = client-encrypted opaque blobs (ciphertext + size + ids stored; filename/content-type/dimensions/content-key NEVER stored or logged; ClamAV N/A on ciphertext — threat-model note required); D1's dimension probe is confirmed plaintext-path-only.
- [ ] Ledger rows updated (typing/presence + delivered-receipts remain INTENTIONAL_DIFFERENCE; no WS/SSE). (Evidence: spec + ledger diff.)

## Ordering
- [ ] C1 and C2 first (they gate frontend S6/S3); C3 next (cheap, unblocks avatar polish); C6 (decided — limits slice) before the attachment-heavy UI ships; C4/C5 opportunistic (unscheduled); C7 alongside C1. Each slice: openapi.yaml + implementation + tests in one commit series, endpoint inventory + ledger updated, `make ci` green, pushed.

# P19 — IPFS Media Mirroring (W6 / P2P-01…04, STOR-04/05)

> Spec: `.ralph/specs/ipfs-media.md` — read it before touching any task.
> Supersedes the P6.2 defer note (`product-decisions.md §5`, the old
> `[~] Implement IPFS backend adapter or deferred spec`): that defer is RESOLVED
> by this spec; `STORAGE_BACKEND=ipfs` stays **rejected** (IPFS is a mirror
> sidecar, never an authoritative backend). Also closes the long-open "Add
> optional Compose profile for IPFS/Kubo" (`fix_plan.md:127`) in Slice 5.
>
> Rules for every slice, per Operating Rules + Definition of Done: one coherent
> vertical slice per loop; contract-first (`openapi.yaml` + migration + sqlc
> BEFORE handlers/worker); no placeholder behavior; evidence in the item note
> (tests run, contract, migration id); the ledger row `VIDRA-IPFS-STORAGE`
> (DEFERRED → IN_PROGRESS → …) and `product-decisions.md §5` are updated in the
> first slice; canonical gate `make ci`
> (fmt-check vet openapi-verify sqlc-verify test-race) green + pushed each slice.
> ⚠️ Privacy invariant for EVERY slice: nothing non-public is ever enqueued for a
> public IPFS pin — the eligibility-gate unit table (Slice 1) is the regression
> fence and must be extended whenever a media class or visibility rule changes.

## P19.1 Foundation: ledger, config, client (P2P-01 groundwork)

- [x] Migration `media_ipfs_pins` (polymorphic pin ledger + durable queue keyed by `object_key`; states pending/pinned/failed/unpinning/unpinned; partial due-index mirroring `transcode_jobs_due_idx`; provenance `video_id`/`owner_user_id` SET NULL FKs) + down migration + sqlc queries (Upsert intent, ClaimDue FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED, MarkPinned/MarkFailed/MarkUnpinned, CountByStateClass, GetByObjectKey, ListByVideo); `make sqlc` regenerated, `sqlc-verify` green. (2026-07-07: `migrations/0071_media_ipfs_pins.{up,down}.sql`; queries `internal/store/queries/media_ipfs_pins.sql` regenerated into `internal/store/sqlcgen`; `make sqlc-verify` green.)
- [x] Config surface: `IPFS_ENABLED` (default false), `IPFS_API_URL`, `IPFS_GATEWAY_URL` (both required when enabled), `IPFS_ADD_TIMEOUT` (60s), `IPFS_PIN_CONCURRENCY` (2), `IPFS_RECONCILE_INTERVAL` (5m), `IPFS_MIRROR_PRIVATE` (default false; =true WITHOUT `IPFS_CLUSTER_API_URL` is a hard config error — private media never goes to a public network), `IPFS_CLUSTER_API_URL`, `IPFS_CLUSTER_TOKEN` (SECRET — extend `observability.IsSensitiveKey`). Keep `STORAGE_BACKEND=ipfs` rejected; repoint the `config.go` "ipfs later" comment at the spec. Tests: config validation table incl. the privacy guard (mirrors `TestLoadRejectsAV1` style). (2026-07-07: `internal/config/config.go` + `IsSensitiveKey` `ipfs_cluster_token`; `TestLoadIPFSConfig*` in `internal/config/config_test.go`; `.env.example` documents the surface.)
- [x] `internal/ipfs` package: Kubo RPC client (`/api/v0/add?pin=true&cid-version=1&raw-leaves=true`, `/api/v0/add?...&wrap-with-directory=true&recursive=true` for directory adds, `/api/v0/pin/add`, `/api/v0/pin/rm`, `/api/v0/version` health probe) behind a small interface + in-memory `FakeIPFSClient` for every unit test (guardrail: unit tests NEVER touch a real node). Port the archive's CID validation contract (CIDv1-only, codec whitelist raw/dag-pb/dag-cbor, length cap, traversal/control-char/URL-encoding rejection, SHA-256/Blake2b) with its table/fuzz tests — behavioral reference only, no code copied. (2026-07-07: `internal/ipfs/{client.go,fake.go,cid.go}` + `cid_test.go` (table + `FuzzValidateCID`) + `fake_test.go`; unit tests nodeless.)
- [x] Contract-first stubs: `GET /api/v1/ipfs/status` (admin) + `POST /api/v1/admin/ipfs/reconcile` (admin) in `openapi.yaml`, handlers answering 503 `ipfs_disabled` when `IPFS_ENABLED=false` (mirrors the PeerTube-import 503 pattern); `openapi-verify` green. Evidence: `make ci` green + pushed; ledger row flipped to IN_PROGRESS with a pointer to the spec. (2026-07-07: `internal/httpapi/admin_ipfs.go` + `IPFSDisabledError` in `errors.go`; routes in `server.go`; `TestIPFSStatusDisabled*`/`TestIPFSReconcileDisabled*` in `admin_ipfs_test.go`; enabled-but-unwired path answers 501 `not_implemented` pending P19.2/P19.6.)

## P19.2 Mirror worker + image classes + reconciliation + admin status (P2P-01)

- [x] `internal/ipfsmirror` worker: claims due `pending`/`unpinning` rows (SKIP LOCKED, exponential backoff, dead-letter to `failed` after max attempts — same shape as `transcode_jobs`/`import_jobs`), streams bytes from the authoritative `storage.Backend`, add+pin via the client, persists `cid`. Concurrency bounded by `IPFS_PIN_CONCURRENCY`. Structured logs: `ipfs_pin_ok/failed{media_class, byte_size, attempts}` at info WITHOUT the CID (CID at debug only — no CID spam); metrics gauges/counters per spec §9. (2026-07-07: `internal/ipfsmirror/service.go` `DrainDue`/`process`/`pin`/`unpin`/`recordFailure` — bounded worker pool sized by `Concurrency`, per-RPC timeout, `RescheduleIPFSPin` backoff then `MarkIPFSPinFailed` dead-letter; `ipfs_pin_ok/ipfs_unpin_ok` at info without CID, CID at debug. `runIPFSMirrorWorker` in `cmd/api/main.go` drains + reconciles when `IPFS_ENABLED`. Tests `TestWorkerPinsPendingRow`/`TestWorkerDeadLetters` in `service_test.go` (race-clean). Note: pin gauges/counters are surfaced via `/ipfs/status` counts + structured logs; a dedicated Prometheus collector is deferred.)
- [x] Eligibility gate + enqueue hooks for the single-file image classes: user/channel avatars + banners (`internal/profileimage` — enqueue on upload in the same tx; unpin old key on re-upload, matching the existing delete-old-blob behavior), video thumbnails, storyboard jpg+vtt, captions, playlist covers. Gate per spec §3: parent video `public`+`published`; owner not `unlisted`; playlist `visibility='public'`. **The eligibility unit table is the privacy fence**: every class × {public, private, unlisted, quarantined} asserting enqueue/not-enqueue — DM attachments, exports, upload chunks, live edge, remote thumbnails asserted NEVER enqueued. (2026-07-07: pure default-deny gate `ipfsmirror.Eligible` in `eligibility.go`; the privacy fence `TestEligibilityTable` in `eligibility_test.go` covers every mirror class × {public,private,unlisted,quarantined,draft,processing,scheduled,failed} + all NEVER-mirror classes (dm_attachment/account_export/upload_chunk/live_edge/remote_thumbnail + an unknown class) asserted refused even with permissive facts. Enqueue wired: avatars/banners via `profileimage.WithMirror` (enqueue on set, unpin superseded key on re-upload + on delete); video thumbnails/storyboard/storyboard_vtt/captions via the video publish/update hooks (`SyncVideo`); playlist covers via `playlist.WithMirror` (SetThumbnail/ClearThumbnail/Update). Video originals/webm are deferred to P19.3, HLS to P19.4, per the slice map.)
- [x] Re-evaluation triggers: `users.unlisted` toggle and account deactivate/delete enqueue unpin (pin on the reverse); video privacy/state transitions wired here for the image derivatives (originals/HLS follow in P19.3/P19.4). (2026-07-07: `ipfsmirror.ReevaluateUser` wired at the profile-update handler (`handleUpdateMe`) — flipping `unlisted` unpins/re-pins the owner's + their channels' avatars/banners via `OwnerImageRefs`; account hard-delete unpins every removed media blob via `account.WithMirror` → `blobDelete`; video privacy/state transitions call `SyncVideo` via `video.WithUpdateHook`/`WithPublishHook` (now append-slices so mirror + federation coexist) and delete calls `UnpinVideo` via `WithDeleteHook`. Tests `TestSyncVideoPublishThenPrivate`, `TestEnqueueUserImageGate`.)
- [x] Reconciliation/backfill scan (every `IPFS_RECONCILE_INTERVAL`): re-enqueues `failed` rows, upserts intents for eligible objects missing a ledger row (the outage-backfill path). Failure-semantics test: node down ⇒ avatar upload still 2xx, row stays `pending`, backfill pins it once the fake node "recovers". (2026-07-07: `ipfsmirror.Reconcile` → new sqlc `RearmFailedIPFSPins` re-arms dead-letters — failed PIN (cid='') → `pending`, failed UNPIN (cid<>'') → `unpinning` so a delete is never re-pinned; `TestReconcileReArms`. ## P19.7 Post-ship privacy audit fix: pin/unpin lost-update race (BLOCKER)

- [x] **Finding (adversarial audit, BLOCKER):** non-public bytes could be permanently pinned to public IPFS via a lost-update race between the pin worker and a concurrent privacy flip. The worker leases a `pending` row, streams `client.Add` (up to `IPFS_ADD_TIMEOUT`, outside any DB txn); meanwhile the owner privatizes the video → `SyncVideo`→`unpinAllForVideo`→`EnqueueIPFSUnpin` flips the leased row `pending`→`unpinning`; then the worker's un-guarded `MarkIPFSPinned` (`SET state='pinned' … WHERE object_key=$1`) clobbered `unpinning` back to `pinned`. Result: the now-private video's bytes stayed pinned + DHT-announced, the ledger row read `pinned`, no unpin was ever queued (reconcile only re-arms `failed`), and `PinnedVideoIDs`/`attachIPFSPinned` emitted `ipfs_pinned=true` — a permanent public disclosure. Companion transitions had the same lost-update class (`MarkIPFSPinFailed` could clobber `unpinning`→`failed`, which reconcile then re-arms cid='' → `pending` → **re-pins** a now-private object; `MarkIPFSPinUnpinned` could clobber a concurrent private→public re-arm). **Fix (`fix(ipfs): …`):** `MarkIPFSPinned` is now a single state-aware `:one` statement — the CID/car_root/byte_size are ALWAYS persisted (the bytes are on the node, so the unpin needs the CID to target them), but `state` advances only `CASE WHEN state IN ('pending','pinned') THEN 'pinned' ELSE state END`, so a concurrently-flipped `unpinning` row KEEPS `unpinning`; `RETURNING state` lets the worker detect the loss and, via `pinRacedUnpin`, execute the reference-checked node unpin (+ superseded-root GC) IMMEDIATELY rather than leak until the next drain (structured `ipfs_pin_raced_unpin` log, no CID at info). `MarkIPFSPinFailed` gained a `sqlc.arg(expected_state)` CAS guard (only dead-letters the still-claimed state); `MarkIPFSPinUnpinned` gained `AND state='unpinning'` (a private→public re-arm survives). `RescheduleIPFSPin` left deliberately state-agnostic (it never advances to terminal, so a re-directed row is simply retried in the newer direction; documented). The double-flip public→private→public converges to the latest visibility fact in every interleaving. Migration/schema unchanged; `make sqlc` regenerated, `sqlc-verify` green. **New tests:** `internal/ipfsmirror/service_test.go` `TestPinLosesRaceToPrivacyFlip` (flip-private mid-Add ⇒ CID recorded, state `unpinned`, node pin removed, `UnpinCount==1`, `ipfs_pinned=false`), `TestPinDoubleFlipConvergesToPinned` (public→private→public mid-Add ⇒ `pinned`, `UnpinCount==0`), `TestPinDirectoryLosesRaceToPrivacyFlip` (same race on the HLS `pinDirectory` path), `TestReadModelsExcludeRacedRow` (VideoPins/PinnedVideoIDs exclude a raced `unpinning`+CID row); the fake gained a between-claim-and-MarkPinned interleaving hook (`ipfs.FakeIPFSClient.AddHook`, fired by `Add`/`AddDirectory` after pinning). Handler-level contract regression `internal/httpapi/videos_ipfs_race_test.go` `TestRacedRowNeverReportsPinned` drives the REAL `ipfsmirror.Service` (minimal `stubLedgerRepo`): a raced `unpinning`+valid-CID row yields no detail `ipfs` object and `ipfs_pinned=false` on the card, with a control flip to `pinned` proving the badge still lights up. `make ci` green: fmt-check, vet, openapi-verify, sqlc-verify, test-race.

## P19.8 Post-ship privacy audit fix: unlisted owner's videos mirrored (MAJOR)

- [x] **Finding (adversarial audit, MAJOR):** an unlisted account's public+published videos (and every derivative — original, VP9, HLS, thumbnail, storyboard jpg+vtt, captions) were mirrored to public IPFS, and flipping a user unlisted never unpinned them — contradicting spec §3 ("`users.unlisted` toggle ⇒ unpin … that owner's public videos") and §7 ("Unlisted is treated as private for mirroring"). `SyncVideo`/`OnTranscodeComplete` gated video-derived classes purely on `VideoPrivacy`+`VideoState`; `VideoVisibility` returned `ownerID` but `SyncVideo` discarded it with `_` and never consulted `UserFlags`; `ReevaluateUser` (the unlisted-toggle re-eval wired at `handleUpdateMe`) iterated only `OwnerImageRefs` (avatars/banners), never the owner's videos; and the backfill catalog scan (`ListBackfillVideoObjects`) coarse-filtered on privacy+state only. **Fix (`fix(ipfs): …`):** (1) the eligibility fence (`ipfsmirror.Eligible`, `eligibility.go`) now gates video-derived classes on `!OwnerUnlisted` in ADDITION to public+published — unlisted is treated as private (spec §7). (2) `SyncVideo` + `OnTranscodeComplete` resolve the owner via the (previously-discarded) `ownerID` from `VideoVisibility` and a new `ownerUnlistedForVideo` helper (`UserFlags`; a missing owner row is default-deny ⇒ unlisted ⇒ unpin; account-active stays the separate deactivation trigger) and feed `OwnerUnlisted` into the gate, so an unlisted owner's video unpins ALL its ledger rows and arms nothing at transcode completion. (3) `ReevaluateUser` now, after the identity-image loop, enumerates the owner's videos via a new `OwnerVideoIDs` lookup (sqlc `ListVideoIDsByOwner`, videos → channels.owner_id) and routes each through `SyncVideo` — one fence in one place: unlisting unpins every video-derived row, re-listing re-pins the still-eligible single-file refs (HLS re-pin stays transcode-driven, identical to the existing private→public path). All queue-only (ledger upsert/unpin) — no synchronous node calls in the request path. (4) the backfill catalog (`ListBackfillVideoObjects`) joins videos → channels → users, returns `owner_unlisted`, coarse-filters `AND NOT t.owner_unlisted`, and `Backfill` re-checks it through the same `Eligible` fence; `VideoObjectRow` gained `OwnerUnlisted`. Migration unchanged; `make sqlc` regenerated (`ListVideoIDsByOwner`, `ListBackfillVideoObjectsRow.OwnerUnlisted`), `sqlc-verify` green. **New/extended tests** (`internal/ipfsmirror`): `TestEligibilityTable` extended with the video-class × owner{listed,unlisted} matrix (unlisted owner ⇒ refused even when public+published); `TestSyncVideoUnlistedOwnerNeverPins` (SyncVideo + OnTranscodeComplete arm NOTHING for an unlisted owner's public video); `TestReevaluateUserUnlistsThenRelistsVideos` (unlist ⇒ avatar + all video-derived rows → `unpinning` → drained `unpinned`; relist ⇒ eligible single-file refs re-armed `pending`; reconcile idempotent afterwards); `TestPinLosesRaceToUnlistedFlip` (MAJOR × BLOCKER interaction — a video mid-Add whose owner flips unlisted via `ReevaluateUser` converges to `unpinned`, node pin removed, `ipfs_pinned=false`); `TestBackfillSeedsEligibleAndFencesNonPublic` extended with an unlisted-owner public video that must be fenced. `product-decisions.md §5` privacy-invariant note now states video-derived classes gate on owner-unlisted. `make ci` green: fmt-check, vet, openapi-verify, sqlc-verify, test-race.

## P19.9 Post-ship privacy audit round-2 fix: raced-to-terminal-`unpinned` pin leak (MAJOR)

- [x] **Finding (adversarial audit round 2, MAJOR):** the round-1 lost-update fix covered the SINGLE-worker race (a mid-Add privacy flip leaving the leased row `unpinning`), but a MULTI-worker interleaving still leaked. Worker A leases a `pending` row and starts `client.Add`; mid-Add a privatize/unlist runs `EnqueueIPFSUnpin`, flipping the leased row `pending`→`unpinning` with `next_attempt_at=now()` (overriding A's lease — the claim query's lock is already released), so a SECOND drain's `ClaimDue` grabs the now-due `unpinning` row (`cid=''` at that instant) and takes `unpin()`'s empty-cid branch → `MarkIPFSPinUnpinned` → terminal `unpinned`, ALL before A's `Add` returns. A's `MarkIPFSPinned` then recorded the CID but the CASE (`WHEN state IN ('pending','pinned')…ELSE state`) KEPT `unpinned`, so `pin()` ran `pinRacedUnpin` with the in-memory row set to `unpinning` while the DB row read `unpinned`. On a TRANSIENT node `Unpin` failure, `recordFailure` operated on the in-memory `unpinning` — `MarkIPFSPinFailed`'s CAS no-op'd (DB was `unpinned`) and `RescheduleIPFSPin` left `state='unpinned'`, which `ClaimDue` never re-selects, `RearmFailedIPFSPins` (failed-only) never re-arms, and `Upsert`/`Backfill` only ever re-PIN — so the bytes for a now-non-public object stayed pinned + DHT-announced forever. Reachable with ≥2 concurrent drains; the round-1 tests used single-worker `DrainDue` and a never-failing `FakeIPFSClient.Unpin`, so they missed it. **Fix (`fix(ipfs): …`):** made the raced-unpin durable AT THE SOURCE — `MarkIPFSPinned`'s CASE now also flips `unpinned`→`unpinning` (atomically with recording the CID) in `internal/store/queries/media_ipfs_pins.sql`. `MarkIPFSPinned` only ever runs right after adding bytes to the node, so a CID landing on a terminal `unpinned` row ALWAYS means those bytes need removal — never a genuinely-clean row — so re-arming is correct. This closes the crash window too (no in-memory/DB divergence: the DB is `unpinning` the instant the CID lands, so `pinRacedUnpin`/`recordFailure`/`RescheduleIPFSPin` all operate on DB truth, and a crash right after `MarkIPFSPinned` still leaves a claimable `unpinning` row). No separate re-arm of clean `unpinned` rows is done (it would churn genuinely-removed/reference-kept rows forever); the general eligibility sweep (P19.10) is the broad backstop. Migration/schema unchanged; `make sqlc` regenerated, `sqlc-verify` green. **New tests** (`internal/ipfsmirror/service_test.go`): `TestPinRacedToUnpinnedRetriesTransientUnpinFailure` — the actual MULTI-worker interleave (mid-Add flip + a nested `DrainDue` completing the empty-cid unpin to terminal `unpinned`, then a failing-then-succeeding `FakeIPFSClient.UnpinHook`): asserts (a) transient-unpin failure ⇒ row re-armed `unpinning` (NOT stranded `unpinned`) with the node pin still present ⇒ next drain removes the bytes ⇒ terminal `unpinned`, `UnpinCount==1` with ≥2 attempts, `ipfs_pinned=false`; verified RED without the CASE branch (state stranded `unpinned`), GREEN with it; `TestPinRacedToUnpinnedRemovesInline` (success companion (b): the same interleave with a first-try-succeeding removal converges terminal-correct in one drain); the single-worker `TestPinLosesRaceToPrivacyFlip`/`…ToUnlistedFlip`/`…DirectoryLosesRace`/`TestPinDoubleFlipConvergesToPinned` still green (c: unchanged). New `ipfs.FakeIPFSClient.UnpinHook` (transient node-unpin failure injection). `make ci` green: fmt-check, vet, openapi-verify, sqlc-verify, test-race.

## P19.10 Post-ship privacy audit round-2 fix: unlisted re-eval inline/unbounded + no backstop (MAJOR)

- [x] **Finding (adversarial audit round 2, MAJOR):** `handleUpdateMe` fired `ReevaluateUser` SYNCHRONOUSLY on the request goroutine with the request context and DISCARDED its error (`_ = s.ipfsmirrorsvc.ReevaluateUser(c.Request().Context(), userID)`). `ReevaluateUser` enumerated EVERY video the owner has and looped `SyncVideo` with FIRST-ERROR-ABORT (2+ DB round-trips each): thousands of sequential queries in the request path, and a transient error or a client disconnect mid-loop stranded videos k+1..N of a now-UNLISTED owner still pinned while the user got 200 OK. No periodic backstop re-checked eligibility (reconcile re-armed only `failed`; the backfill fence is a one-shot admin action). **Fix (`fix(ipfs): …`):** (1) moved the fan-out OFF the request path into a durable queue — new migration `0072_ipfs_user_reevals` (`media_ipfs_user_reevals`: `user_id` PK, attempts/next_attempt_at/last_error, due index) + queries `internal/store/queries/ipfs_user_reevals.sql` (`EnqueueIPFSUserReeval` idempotent upsert coalescing rapid toggles; `ClaimDueIPFSUserReevals` FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED + lease; `Delete`/`Reschedule`). `handleUpdateMe` now calls the cheap `s.ipfsmirrorsvc.EnqueueUserReeval` (a single DB write, error logged not discarded — best-effort) and the provider interface exposes ONLY `EnqueueUserReeval`, so the handler STRUCTURALLY cannot run `SyncVideo` inline; committed durably so a crash right after the toggle still converges. The mirror worker (`runIPFSMirrorWorker`, `cmd/api/main.go`) expands jobs via new `Service.DrainDueUserReevals` on the drain tick. (2) per-video error ISOLATION: `ReevaluateUser` now processes ALL images + videos, logs-and-counts each failure (never aborts the batch), and returns an error only if ≥1 failed → `DrainDueUserReevals` reschedules the WHOLE job with backoff (successful items are idempotent no-ops on retry; never dead-letters — a stranded re-eval is a privacy concern). (3) periodic BACKSTOP: new set-based `SweepIneligibleIPFSPins` (`media_ipfs_pins.sql`) joins `media_ipfs_pins → videos → channels → users` (and `→ users` for identity images) and flips every live (pinned/pending) row that is now ineligible (video not public+published, OR owner unlisted, OR owner inactive/soft-deleted) to `unpinning`, mirroring the backfill catalog join in reverse; `Service.SweepIneligible` runs it on the reconcile tick, closing the whole class (any missed toggle, crash, or future eligibility-rule change converges on the next sweep). Playlist-cover rows (no provenance columns) stay owned by their own `EnqueuePlaylistCover` hook and are out of the sweep's scope (documented). Migration added; `make sqlc` regenerated; `sqlc-verify` green. **New tests** (`internal/ipfsmirror/service_test.go`): `TestEnqueueUserReevalExpandsOffRequestPath` (enqueue does NOT flip any ledger row inline — rows stay `pinned`; a FRESH service instance over the same store expands the committed job and converges — the crash-shaped case); `TestUserReevalPerVideoIsolationRetries` (owner-unlisted with 2 videos, one video's unpin fails transiently ⇒ the other converges, the job is rescheduled not deleted, a retry drains the straggler and completes); `TestSweepReArmsIneligiblePinnedRow` (a pinned row made ineligible WITHOUT any enqueue is re-armed by the sweep and then unpinned by the worker, while a clean eligible row is untouched); `internal/httpapi` `TestUpdateMeUnlistedEnqueuesReevalNotInline` (PATCH `/auth/me` `{unlisted}` enqueues exactly once, a non-unlisted edit enqueues zero, a mirror enqueue error still returns 200). `make ci` green: fmt-check, vet, openapi-verify, sqlc-verify, test-race.

## P19.P Private IPFS Mirroring Phase (P2P-01/03, STOR-05 for non-public media)

Spec: `.ralph/specs/ipfs-media-private.md` (cross-linked from `ipfs-media.md §7`).
Builds the PRIVATE replication tier on top of the shipped public mirror
(P19.1–P19.10). Standing rules: one vertical slice per loop; contract-first; the
P19 privacy fence (eligibility + read-model unit tables) is EXTENDED in every
slice that touches routing, never weakened. **Cardinal invariant, tested
everywhere:** a `network='private'` row can NEVER be pinned via the public client
(and vice versa), and no private CID is ever emitted in a public API payload or
with a gateway URL — the failure mode is always "private content unreachable",
never "private content public". User-resolved decisions (2026-07-07, recorded in
`product-decisions.md §5.P`): v1 private classes = private+unlisted videos & all
derivatives, non-public playlist covers, unlisted/deactivated-owner avatars/banners;
quarantined → nowhere; plaintext DM attachments → never, any network; e2ee-blobs
join later (deferred). Self-hosted second swarm.key'd node, `LIBP2P_FORCE_PNET=1`,
never dual-home. NO encryption layer this phase (Class B rejected on the merits;
Lit rejected). Replication-not-distribution: private CIDs never in any API response.
